arXiv:2604.11665v1 [cs.NE] 2026 年 4 月 13 日arXiv:2604.11665v1 [cs.NE] 13 Apr 2026

超越法学硕士、稀疏分布式内存和神经形态学Beyond LLMs, Sparse Distributed Memory, and Neuromorphics
¡超维 SRAM-CAM“VaCoAl”¡A Hyper-Dimensional SRAM-CAM ”VaCoAl”
实现超高速、超低功耗和低成本¿for Ultra-High Speed, Ultra-Low Power, and Low Cost¿

Hiroyuki Chuma (Professor Emeritus, Hitotsubashi University)
Kanji Otsuka (Professor Emeritus, Meisei University)
Yoichi Sato (Shuhari System) Corresponding author. While the source code remains proprietary, the implementation is available for commercial use and collaborative projects. For inquiries, please contact chuma@iir.hit-u.ac.jp.
摘要Abstract

本文报告了一个意想不到的发现。在确定性的 基于伽罗瓦域代数构建的超维计算架构， 路径依赖的语义选择机制自发出现， 而不是原始设计的一部分。从功能上来说是 相当于生物中的尖峰时间依赖性可塑性 (STDP) 神经回路，其大小是可预测的This paper reports an unexpected finding. In a deterministic hyperdimensional computing architecture built on Galois-field algebra, a path-dependent semantic selection mechanism emerges spontaneously, without being part of the original design. It is functionally equivalent to Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity (STDP) in biological neural circuits, and its magnitude is predictable先验的a priori来自 与实际数据的测量值相匹配的封闭式表达式 数千万条记录的规模。该发现源于 旨在解决结构限制的架构 现代人工智能——灾难性遗忘、学习停滞等 所有信息不可逆混合的绑定问题 且各构成要素之间的关系不能严格界定 保持在代数水平而不是统计水平。from a closed-form expression that matches the measured value on real data at the scale of tens of millions of records. The finding arises from an architecture designed to address the structural limitations of modern AI—catastrophic forgetting, learning stagnation, and above all the Binding Problem in which information is irreversibly mixed and the relationships between constituent elements cannot be strictly maintained—at the algebraic level rather than the statistical one.

为了突破这些限制，我们提出了一种新的架构“VaCoAl（模糊重合算法）”，采用超高速、低功耗、低成本的超高维SRAM/DRAM-CAM，及其Python实现“PyVaCoAl”。该架构植根于 Pentti Kanerva 的稀疏分布式内存 (SDM)，通过采用伽罗瓦域扩散的代数确定性数字逻辑，解决了超高维（此处为一百万）维二进制空间中的正交化和检索的艰巨挑战（SDM 在物理实现中面临这一挑战）。To break through these limitations, we propose a new architecture, “VaCoAl (Vague Coincident Algorithm),” employing an ultra-high-speed, low-power, low-cost ultra-high-dimensional SRAM/DRAM-CAM, together with its Python implementation “PyVaCoAl.” Rooted in Pentti Kanerva’s Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM), this architecture solves the formidable challenges of orthogonalisation and retrieval in ultra-high (here, one million)-dimensional binary spaces—which SDM faced in physical implementation—through algebro-deterministic digital logic employing Galois-field diffusion.

因此，传统超维计算（HDC）固有的大量计算开销被消除，成功地在极低负载下实现了实用的HDC。这为将逻辑符号推理与统计学习相结合的下一代神经符号人工智能提供了重要的工程基础。此外，该系统作为“基于内存的架构”运行，优先考虑检索和关联而不是计算，其块划分和多数投票结构与生物锥体神经元中的树突计算具有结构类比。Consequently, the massive computational overhead inherent in conventional Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) is eliminated, successfully realising practical HDC at extremely low load. This provides a crucial engineering foundation for next-generation neuro-symbolic AI that integrates logical symbolic reasoning with statistical learning. Moreover, the system operates as a “memory-based architecture” that prioritises retrieval and association over computation, and its block-division-and-majority-voting structure bears a structural analogy to Dendritic Computation in biological pyramidal neurons.

通过这种代数结构，VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 以与 LLM 的统计嵌入根本不同的方式解决了绑定问题。 HDC 型代数运算能够以极低的计算成本实现概念的可逆积分和分解，同时保持元素独立性，从而实现组合泛化。同时，它实现了配备“数学可审计性”的可解释人工智能（XAI）——允许在不降低信息质量的情况下对计算过程进行事后验证——并提出一个高度透明的可靠性指标，即基于代数多数投票的“置信比（CR）分数”。Through this algebraic structure, VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl addresses the Binding Problem in a fundamentally different way from the statistical embeddings of LLMs. HDC-type algebraic operations enable the reversible integration and decomposition of concepts at extremely low computational cost while preserving element independence, thereby achieving compositional generalisation. Simultaneously, it realises Explainable AI (XAI) equipped with “mathematical auditability”—allowing post-verification of the computational process without information degradation—and presents a highly transparent reliability metric, the “Confidence Ratio (CR) score,” based on algebraic majority voting.

为了在实际规模上验证 VaCoAl 的搜索性能和多步语义推理能力，我们使用 PyVaCoAl 进行了实证 HDC 实验。这种架构的真正价值不是通过简单的相似性搜索来证明，而是通过大规模有向无环图（DAG）的多跳关联推理来证明。作为旨在将这一特性推向极限的基准，我们利用了一个由 WIKIDATA 构建的包含一千年来约 470,000 名学者导师与学生关系的本体数据库，并执行了大规模的计算相谱学：从所有 64 位历史菲尔兹奖获得者开始，我们将导师的家谱追溯到 57 代，相当于超过 2550 万条总家谱路径。To verify VaCoAl’s search performance and multi-step semantic reasoning capabilities at a practical scale, we conducted empirical HDC experiments using PyVaCoAl. The true value of this architecture is demonstrated not in simple similarity searches but in multi-hop associative reasoning over massive directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). As a benchmark designed to push this property to its limits, we utilised an ontology database of approximately 470,000 scholar mentor-student relationships over a millennium constructed from WIKIDATA, and executed a large-scale Computational Prosopography: starting from all 64 historical Fields Medalists, we traced the genealogies of mentors back up to 57 generations, equivalent to over 25.5 million total genealogical paths.

在HDC分析中，我们利用HDC特有的Bundling操作合成了“微积分程度”、“巨分数”等复合概念向量，通过Unbind操作和汉明距离不断测量各种概念在每个节点中的嵌入程度，并应用CR分数来消除噪声。主要调查结果如下：In the HDC analysis, we synthesised composite concept vectors such as “calculus degree,” “Giant Score,” etc. using Bundling operations specific to HDC, continuously measured the degree to which various concepts are embedded in each node through Unbinding operations and Hamming distance, and applied the CR score to eliminate noise. The principal findings are as follows:

  • •

    对牛顿与莱布尼茨关于微积分创立的优先权之争的历史性反思。A historical reconsideration of the Newton–Leibniz priority dispute over the founding of calculus.

  • •

    对不连续动态的定量可视化，直到 17 世纪，不同的学术领域都在莱布尼茨汇聚，形成无数条狭窄的路径，平均约 5 条“穿过人的路线”，而从莱布尼茨追溯至今，揭示了一条通往菲尔兹奖章获得者的“高速公路”，平均扩展至约 53 条路径。Quantitative visualisation of the discontinuous dynamics whereby diverse academic fields up to the 17th century converge on Leibniz as numerous narrow paths averaging approximately 5 “routes passing through the person,” while tracing from Leibniz to the present reveals a “superhighway” to Fields Medalists expanding to an average of approximately 53 paths.

  • •

    通过 HDC 分析，采用七个独立的证据——高速公路的出现、社会成员的爆炸性增长、研究机构的中心多元化、学生生产率的激增等等——莱布尼茨之后发生的托马斯·库恩式范式转变（新常态科学的形成）。Structural demonstration, via HDC analysis employing seven independent lines of evidence—emergence of the Super-Highway, explosive growth of society memberships, hub diversification of research institutions, surge in student production rate, among others—of a Thomas Kuhn style paradigm shift (the formation of a new normal science) that occurred in the wake of Leibniz.

此外，比较 PyVaCoAl 中内置的碰撞避免（救援）电路的存在和不存在表明，VaCoAl 独特的碰撞容忍（无关）机制在多阶段推理中触发了由路径积分值介导的历史剔除过程。这种机制就像“奥卡姆剃刀”一样，自然地修剪迂回路径并选择性地保留直接路径，从而在真实数据中产生“紧急语义选择”，其功能相当于大脑中的 STDP（尖峰时序依赖性可塑性）。 VaCoAl 从而开辟了决定性的第三条道路“HDC-AI”——超越单纯的高速搜索引擎。第三条路径远不是大型语言模型的竞争对手，而是通过提供它们在结构上缺乏的可逆、可审计的推理基础来补充它们。其结果是下一代神经符号人工智能的自然分工：一方面是语言流动性，另一方面是多跳逻辑完整性。Furthermore, comparing the presence and absence of the collision-avoidance (rescue) circuit built into PyVaCoAl revealed that VaCoAl’s distinctive Collision Tolerance (Don’t Care) mechanism triggers, in multi-stage reasoning, a historical culling process mediated by path-integral values. This mechanism functions as an “Occam’s razor” that naturally prunes circuitous routes and selectively preserves direct ones, giving rise on real data to an “emergent semantic selection” functionally equivalent to STDP (Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity) in the brain. VaCoAl thereby opens a decisive third path as “HDC-AI”—one that transcends a mere high-speed search engine. Far from rivalling Large Language Models, this third path complements them by supplying the reversible, auditable reasoning substrate they structurally lack. The result is a natural division of labour for next-generation neuro-symbolic AI: linguistic fluidity on one side, multi-hop logical integrity on the other.

1 简介：从概率模仿到 “模拟数字（AD）功能归纳”Introduction: From Probabilistic Mimicry to “Analog–Digital (AD) Functional Induction”

1.1 解决现代生成人工智能的“奇点” 以及超越卡内尔瓦的理论Resolving the “Singularities” of Modern Generative AI and Transcending Kanerva’s Theory

在讨论现代人工智能的结构限制之前 为了推动这项工作，我们首先声明核心经验 发现本文的其余部分已组织好进行解释。在 第 5 节我们报告确定性超维计算 建立在伽罗瓦域代数展示和现实世界之上的建筑 已遍历约 470,000 条师生记录的本体 高达 57 代，一种新兴的路径依赖语义选择 机制不属于原始设计的一部分。这个机制是 功能上相当于尖峰时间相关可塑性 (STDP) 生物神经回路，并且——与大多数关于出现的报告不同 当代人工智能——其规模是可以预测的Before turning to the structural limitations of modern AI that motivate this work, we state at the outset the central empirical finding that the rest of the paper is organised to explain. In Section 5 we report that a deterministic hyperdimensional computing architecture built on Galois-field algebra exhibits, on a real-world ontology of approximately 470,000 mentor–student records traversed up to 57 generations, an emergent path-dependent semantic selection mechanism that was not part of the original design. This mechanism is functionally equivalent to Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity (STDP) in biological neural circuits, and—unlike most reports of emergence in contemporary AI—its magnitude is predictable先验的a priori来自 封闭式表达式from the closed-form expression0.99756≈0.8460.997^{56}\!\approx\!0.846，它坐在 在测量值的方差范围内, which sits within the variance of the measured valueCR2≈0.905\mathrm{CR}_{2}\approx 0.905到了第56代。本介绍的其余部分 阐述了本研究的理论和工程背景 发现变得容易理解：为什么代数确定性底物， 第一个需要的不是概率或模拟的 地方。at the 56th generation. The remainder of this introduction sets out the theoretical and engineering background against which this finding becomes intelligible: why an algebro-deterministic substrate, rather than a probabilistic or analog one, was needed in the first place.

深度学习的成功是不可否认的，但其本质上是低效率的 是硬连线到其学习过程中的。现代生成人工智能模型是 主要建立在 McCulloch-Pitts 点神经元模型上，该模型的数十个 数千亿个参数通过梯度下降进行调整 反向传播。然而，阿玛里的信息几何理论揭示了：The success of deep learning is undeniable, yet a fundamental inefficiency is hard-wired into its learning process. Modern generative AI models are built predominantly on McCulloch–Pitts point-neuron models whose tens to hundreds of billions of parameters are tuned by gradient descent through backpropagation. Amari’s information-geometry theory, however, reveals that奇点singularities在深度神经网络的层次参数空间中 网络退化了 Fisher 信息矩阵并导致严重的in the hierarchical parameter space of deep neural networks degenerate the Fisher information matrix and induce severe学习高原learning plateaus [2, 3]。补救措施如 Amari 的自然梯度方法需要非常大的逆矩阵 计算，使得它们对于边缘设备来说不切实际。此外，任何 尝试用新的知识风险来增强预先训练的模型. Remedies such as Amari’s natural-gradient method demand prohibitively large inverse-matrix computations, rendering them impractical for edge devices. Moreover, any attempt to augment a pre-trained model with new knowledge risks灾难性遗忘catastrophic forgetting——覆盖和破坏 先前获得的记忆——由柯克帕特里克等人正式化 等人。—the overwriting and destruction of previously acquired memories—as formalized by Kirkpatrick et al.[19].111AAAI 主席小组报告The AAAI presidential panel report[5]提供了一个 对法学硕士更基本局限性的全面调查。其 观察结果与目前的工作特别密切，因为 这里提出的 VaCoAl 架构能够实现多跳语义逻辑 推理——严格追踪多步关系——作为代数 操作，正是当前人工智能陷入困境的领域： “provides a comprehensive survey of the more fundamental limitations of LLMs. Its observations are particularly germane to the present work, because the VaCoAl architecture proposed here enables multi-hop semantic logical inference—strictly tracing multi-step relationships—as algebraic operations, precisely the domain in which current AI falters: “因果推理和反事实推理：Causal and Counterfactual Reasoning:虽然人工智能模型可以检测 大量数据集中的相关性，他们在因果推理上苦苦挣扎 反事实推理。”While AI models can detect correlations in vast datasets, they struggle with causal inference and counterfactual reasoning.”

除了这些学习过程的限制之外，现代法学硕士还拥有更深入的知识 结构性缺陷源于不可逆的信息混合： 的Beyond these learning-process limitations, modern LLMs harbor a deeper structural deficiency rooted in the irreversible mixing of information: the绑定问题Binding Problem。作为萨加德. As Thagard[42]和 安德森and Anderson[6]强调，这是一个令人敬畏的 任何受大脑启发的认知架构的实施挑战。 在法学硕士的统计嵌入中，构成元素融合 当多个概念融合在一起时，就不可逆转地 忠实地保留它们的构图关系。的emphasize, this is a formidable implementation challenge for any brain-inspired cognitive architecture. In the statistical embeddings of LLMs, constituent elements fuse irreversibly when multiple concepts are integrated, making it impossible to preserve their compositional relationships faithfully. The语义指针架构Semantic Pointer Architecture(SPA) 的 板材(SPA) of Plate[32]和埃利亚史密斯and Eliasmith[11]提供 数学原理的解决方案，但它依赖于循环 卷积需要大量计算，严重阻碍了部署 低功耗设备。offers a mathematically principled solution, but its reliance on circular convolution entails heavy computation that severely hampers deployment on low-power devices.

1988 年，Pentti Kanerva 提出了一种根本不同的方法：In 1988, Pentti Kanerva proposed a fundamentally different approach:稀疏分布式内存Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM) [17]。通过将大脑输入/输出表示为 Kanerva 利用超高维空间中的向量 接近正交以避免信息干扰。瓦科阿尔 本文提出的架构在概念上植根于 Kanerva 的 SDM 利用现代数字电路超越了 SDM 和 SPA 技术。其核心是代数确定性伽罗瓦域 扩散：如第 5 节所示，VaCoAl 采用 HDC 型代数 操作——绑定、解除绑定和捆绑——可逆地组合 并在保持元素独立性的同时分解概念，从而 以一小部分计算成本解决绑定问题 SPA 要求。伽罗瓦场扩散机制详细介绍于 第 3 节。. By representing brain input/output as vectors in an ultra-high-dimensional space, Kanerva exploited their near-orthogonality to sidestep information interference. The VaCoAl architecture proposed herein is conceptually rooted in Kanerva’s SDM yet surpasses both SDM and SPA by leveraging modern digital-circuit technology. At its core lies algebro-deterministic Galois-field diffusion: as Section 5 demonstrates, VaCoAl employs HDC-type algebraic operations—Binding, Unbinding, and Bundling—to reversibly compose and decompose concepts while preserving element independence, thereby resolving the Binding Problem at a fraction of the computational cost required by SPA. The Galois-field diffusion mechanism is detailed in Section 3.

1.2 “随机性”的工程障碍和局限性 仿生学The Engineering Barriers of “Randomness” and the Limits of Biomimicry

卡内尔瓦的理论在抽象上很优雅，但将其转化为 硅暴露了一个巨大的工程障碍：Kanerva’s theory was elegant in the abstract, but translating it into silicon exposed a formidable engineering obstacle: the地址译码器 问题address-decoder problem。这个障碍源于概率随机性和 支持 SDM 和主流神经形态的物理仿生学 硬件（例如 IBM 的 TrueNorth. This obstacle originates in the probabilistic randomness and physical biomimicry that underpin both SDM and mainstream neuromorphic hardware (e.g., IBM’s TrueNorth[26]，英特尔的 洛伊希, Intel’s Loihi[9]）。基于三进制的早期硬件原型 CAM (TCAM) 尝试通过强力并行匹配来模拟 大脑立即定位相似模式的能力，但是 由此产生的功耗使它们发热 不可扩展的). Early hardware prototypes based on ternary CAM (TCAM) attempted brute-force parallel matching to emulate the brain’s ability to locate similar patterns instantaneously, but the resulting power dissipation rendered them thermally unscalable[31].

此外，仅靠物理神经元模仿无法解决高阶问题 认知任务。正如萨加德在《Physical neuron mimicry alone, moreover, cannot address higher-order cognitive tasks. As Thagard argues in大脑-思维Brain–Mind [42]，一个能够解释的认知架构 创造力、意识和情感必须解决束缚 问题——构建层次结构思维的挑战 来自“红色”和“苹果”等基本概念。 安德森, a cognitive architecture capable of explaining creativity, consciousness, and emotion must solve the Binding Problem—the challenge of composing hierarchically structured thoughts from elementary concepts such as “red” and “apple.” Anderson[6]补充说，人类认知从根本上来说是adds that human cognition is fundamentally联想性的associative而不是基于搜索，强调需要 设计大脑从嘈杂中具体化强大概念的能力 感官流。rather than search-based, underscoring the need to engineer the brain’s capacity to crystallize robust concepts from noisy sensory streams.

数学复杂的框架，例如 SPA 板材Mathematically sophisticated frameworks such as the SPA of Plate[32]和埃利亚史密斯and Eliasmith[11]地址 原则上这些要求，但它们依赖于计算 昂贵的操作（例如循环卷积）妨碍了实际应用 部署在低功耗设备上。一个轻量级的实现可以 用代数方法解决结合问题 — 正是 VaCoAl 提供——因此是必不可少的。简而言之，传统的 物理模仿大脑概率行为的策略 本身就构成了address these requirements in principle, yet their dependence on computationally expensive operations (e.g., circular convolution) precludes practical deployment on low-power devices. A lightweight implementation that can solve the Binding Problem algebraically—precisely what VaCoAl provides—is therefore indispensable. In short, the conventional strategy of physically mimicking the brain’s probabilistic behavior has itself constituted the缺少链接missing link介于神经科学启发之间 理论与工程实用性。between neuroscience-inspired theory and engineering practicality.

1.3 本研究的核心：“AD功能归纳”The Core of This Study: “AD Functional Induction”

本研究的目的是证明 VaCoAl 构成 果断地解决上述缺失的环节。而不是 依靠概率随机数或模拟组件，VaCoAl 实现了 Kanerva 和 Amari 设想的正交化，并且 解决了 Thagard 和 Anderson 提出的绑定问题 严格确定性的数字逻辑——具体来说，扩散 伽罗瓦域（BCH 代码）The purpose of this study is to demonstrate that VaCoAl constitutes a decisive solution to the missing link identified above. Rather than relying on probabilistic random numbers or analog components, VaCoAl achieves the orthogonalization envisioned by Kanerva and Amari and resolves the Binding Problem raised by Thagard and Anderson through strictly deterministic digital logic—specifically, diffusion over Galois fields (BCH codes)[23]。而不是模仿 生物神经元的物理结构，我们独立解决 相同的计算问题（高维正交化、模式 完成）通过伽罗瓦域代数。我们将这种方法称为. Instead of mimicking the physical structure of biological neurons, we independently solve the same computational problems (high-dimensional orthogonalization, pattern completion) via Galois-field algebra. We term this approachAD功能归纳AD Functional Induction;表; Table1总结了与现有的主要结构差异 神经形态方法。summarizes the principal structural differences from existing neuromorphic approaches.

We demonstrate that, even at big-data scale, a novel multi-stage collision-avoidance circuit deterministically achieves Frontier-Size-relative exact matching while retaining HDC’s flexible retrieval capabilities. We also delineate the qualitatively distinct characteristics and trade-offs of the two operating modes:救援 模式Rescue mode（DICT 等效的完美再现性）和(DICT-equivalent perfect reproducibility) and不在乎 模式Don’t Care mode（基于 CR2 的语义选择）。(CR2-based semantic selection).

2 理论背景： 的几何性质 高维正交空间 （“维度的祝福”）和实施障碍Theoretical Background: Geometric Properties of High-Dimensional Orthogonal Spaces (“Blessing of Dimensionality”) and Implementation Barriers

本节回顾稀疏分布式的理论框架 Kanerva提出的内存（SDM）This section reviews the theoretical framework of Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM) proposed by Kanerva[17]并分析，从 从数学和工程的角度来看，为什么实用 事实证明，其“理想地址解码器”的实现非常困难 在传统的计算机架构内。and analyzes, from both mathematical and engineering perspectives, why the practical implementation of its “ideal address decoder” has proved so difficult within conventional computer architectures.

2.1 Kanerva 的稀疏分布式内存 (SDM) 模型Kanerva’s Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM) Model

Kanerva 的 SDM 将人类长期记忆建模为非确定性模型 地址结构类似于有组织的书架 (RAM)，但作为云 分散在广阔的高维空间中的信息点 访问的基础上Kanerva’s SDM models human long-term memory not as a deterministic address structure akin to an organized bookshelf (RAM), but as a cloud of information points scattered across a vast high-dimensional space and accessed on the basis of汉明距离Hamming distance（按位 相似度）(bitwise similarity)[17]。正如 Kanerva 所阐述的，SDM 是 小脑电路的数学形式化。两种细胞类型发挥作用 明确划分的角色：. As Kanerva elaborated, SDM is a mathematical formalization of cerebellar circuitry. Two cell types play clearly delineated roles:颗粒细胞granule cells（对应于 地址解码器）稀疏扩散并将输入编码为长并行 纤维进入高维空间，同时(corresponding to the address decoder) sparsely diffuse and encode inputs as long parallel fibers into a high-dimensional space, while浦肯野细胞Purkinje cells（对应存储介质）延伸扇形树突 与这些纤维正交，捕获并存储结果 正交化高维向量。(corresponding to the storage medium) extend fan-shaped dendrites orthogonal to those fibers, capturing and storing the resulting orthogonalized high-dimensional vectors.

这种几何结构的计算意义 正交化机制得到了现代神经科学的充分支持。 坎德尔等人。The computational significance of this geometric architecture as an orthogonalization mechanism is well supported by modern neuroscience. Kandel et al.[16]认为小脑颗粒细胞argue that cerebellar granule cells展开expand数十亿个神经元的感觉输入，从而 在高维空间中分离相似的输入模式（模式 分离）并促进浦肯野的学习和歧视 细胞。劳斯莱斯sensory inputs across billions of neurons, thereby separating similar input patterns in high-dimensional space (pattern separation) and facilitating learning and discrimination by Purkinje cells. Rolls[35]同样强调扩张 颗粒细胞重新编码是数学正交化的关键 感官模式并实现无干扰学习。的similarly emphasizes that expansion recoding by granule cells is the key to mathematically orthogonalizing sensory patterns and enabling interference-free learning. The维度的祝福blessing of dimensionality因此，SDM 所阐明的不是 只是一个抽象的数学属性，但反映了物理 神经回路代表和学习信息的基质。articulated by SDM is therefore not merely an abstract mathematical property but reflects the physical substrate through which neural circuits represent and learn information.

2.1.1 高维空间的数学性质Mathematical Properties of High-Dimensional Spaces

在 SDM 中，所有信息——外部感官输入（视觉、听觉、 触摸）以及内部状态（身体位置、持续思考 流程、上下文）—表示为In SDM, all information—external sensory inputs (vision, hearing, touch) as well as internal states (body position, ongoing thought processes, context)—is represented asnn位二进制向量 （-bit binary vectors (n≈1,000n\approx 1{,}000–10,00010{,}000）。由此产生的空间). The resulting space𝒩={0,1}n\mathcal{N}=\{0,1\}^{n}具有几何性质 与日常的三维直觉截然不同。possesses geometric properties that differ sharply from everyday three-dimensional intuition.

  • •

    正交性（高维的祝福）。Orthogonality (blessing of high dimensionality).均匀地随机抽取两个点Two points drawn uniformly at random from{0,1}n\{0,1\}^{n}有汉明 服从二项式分布的距离have a Hamming distance that follows a binomial distribution centered atn/2n/2。 因此，任意两个随机向量几乎肯定是 准正交；两个这样的向量“相似”的概率 是天文数字般的小。这种几何特性使大脑 （和 SDM）将大量模式叠加在同一个模式上 物理内存基板无灾难性 干扰. Consequently, any two random vectors are almost certainly quasi-orthogonal; the probability of two such vectors being “similar” is astronomically small. This geometric property allows the brain (and SDM) to superimpose a vast number of patterns on the same physical memory substrate without catastrophic interference[18].

2.1.2 SDM中的最佳匹配机制Best-Match Mechanism in SDM

SDM中的读和写操作与SDM中的读和写操作有根本的不同 传统RAM：他们依赖Read and write operations in SDM differ fundamentally from those of conventional RAM: they rely on最佳匹配best match而不是精确的 比赛。rather than exact match.

  • •

    分布式写入。Distributed write.存储二进制数据To store binary datumDD在地址at addressxx，系统不寻求 匹配的单个位置, the system does not seek a single location that matchesxx正是如此。相反，它会激活exactly. A single memory trace is thus physically distributed across thousands of locations (neurons).

    统计读取（多数规则）。Statistical read (majority rule).在给定噪声探针的情况下检索数据To retrieve data given a noisy probex′x^{\prime}，系统激活 附近的硬位置并总结它们的内容。高维 正交性确保噪声向量随机定向， 根据大数定律抵消，而目标信号 建设性地积累。然后恢复阈值（多数票） 从嘈杂的聚合中得到干净的输出。, the system activates nearby hard locations and sums their contents. High-dimensional orthogonality ensures that noise vectors, being randomly oriented, cancel by the law of large numbers, whereas the target signal accumulates constructively. A threshold (majority vote) then recovers a clean output from the noisy aggregate.

2.2 实施瓶颈：“地址解码器”墙Implementation Bottlenecks: The “Address Decoder” Wall

作为一个数学模型，SDM 是引人注目的；将其转化为硅， 然而，面临着严重的工程瓶颈——As a mathematical model, SDM is compelling; translating it into silicon, however, confronts a severe engineering bottleneck—the地址解码器问题address-decoder problem.

2.2.1 计算爆炸Computational Explosion

理想的 SDM 必须针对每个输入向量计算汉明 到的距离An ideal SDM must, for every incoming vector, compute the Hamming distance to全部all存储的硬位置地址并选择那些 在阈值内。与stored hard-location addresses and select those within a threshold. With10510^{5}困难地点和hard locations and1,0001{,}000位 载体，维持-bit vectors, sustaining10610^{6}每秒内存访问次数需要memory accesses per second would demand101510^{15}位操作 (peta-ops) 每 第二bit-operations (peta-ops) per second[29, 37]——超级计算机级的吞吐量 单一的认知行为导致边缘部署不可行。—supercomputer-class throughput for a single cognitive act, rendering edge deployment infeasible.

2.2.2 热量与准确性的困境The Heat–Accuracy Dilemma

之前的工作已经探索了两条逃生路线，每条都受到关键的阻碍 权衡。Prior work has explored two escape routes, each encumbered by a critical trade-off.

  • •

    全搜索 CAM (TCAM)。Full-search CAM (TCAM).专用 TCAM 硬件并行比较所有位，并且 因此速度很快，但同时驱动数百万个存储单元 会产生极端的功耗和热量。实际TCAM容量为 因此仅限于千比特范围，排除了大规模人工智能 应用Dedicated TCAM hardware compares all bits in parallel and is therefore fast, but simultaneously driving millions of memory cells incurs extreme power dissipation and heat. Practical TCAM capacity is consequently limited to the kilobit range, precluding large-scale AI applications[31].

  • •

    哈希（RAM）。Hashing (RAM).哈希表报价Hash tables offerO​(1)O(1)摊销访问并被广泛认为 最快的查找机制。然而，与 VaCoAl 相比，两个 必须作出根本区别。amortized access and are widely regarded as the fastest lookup mechanism. Compared with VaCoAl, however, two fundamental distinctions must be drawn.

    首先，性质First, the nature ofO​(1)O(1)不同。differs.哈希本质上是 顺序软件处理：CPU 必须计算哈希值 高维数据并通过指针追踪解决冲突，两者 其中需要不小的延迟。 VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl，作为 CAM 实现 SRAM/DRAM，通过内存中并行匹配以纳秒/十纳秒运行 规模——速度提高了几个数量级。简而言之，哈希实现了Hashing is inherently sequential software processing: the CPU must compute a hash value for high-dimensional data and resolve collisions via pointer chasing, both of which entail non-trivial latency. VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl, realized as CAM on SRAM/DRAM, operates via in-memory parallel matching at the nano/ten nano-second scale—orders of magnitude faster. In short, hashing achieves逻辑的logical恒定时间，而基于 SRAM/DRAM 的 VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 方法constant time, whereas SRAM/DRAM-based VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl approaches物理的physical即时性。instantaneity.

    其次，散列是为了去相关而设计的Second, hashing is designed for decorrelation: 故意的 将相似的输入映射到遥远的地址，破坏了相似性 保存对于 SDM 最佳匹配检索至关重要 来自嘈杂的输入。尽管哈希函数对于 小规模精确匹配查询（例如，: it deliberately maps similar inputs to distant addresses, destroying the similarity preservation that is central to SDM’s best-match retrieval from noisy inputs.     需要注意一个重要的警告：上面的速度比较主要假设 SRAM-CAM 硬件。 PyVaCoAl——一个软件 实施——救援电路调用Python的标准散列 块碰撞解决机制和单谓词 精确匹配查询可能比 Python 运行得慢An important caveat applies: the speed comparison above mainly assumes SRAM-CAM hardware. PyVaCoAl’s true software-level advantage lies not in raw speed but in CR2-based path-quality quantification and integrated HDC multi-predicate operations (Binding/Unbinding), as detailed in Sections 3.5 and 5.

2.3 结论：随机性陷阱（缺失的环节）Conclusion: The Trap of Randomness (The Missing Link)

总而言之，根本瓶颈不是晶体管数量或功耗 密度本身，但工程依赖于In summary, the fundamental bottleneck is not transistor count or power density per se, but an engineering dependence on随机性randomness。 Kanerva 的 SDM 依赖于随机得出的统计保证 预测；忠实地再现确定性的随机性 硅需要生成和存储大量随机数 整个矩阵或强力汉明距离计算 记忆。所缺少的是一个地址解码器，它可以保留. Kanerva’s SDM relies on statistical guarantees derived from random projections; faithfully reproducing that randomness on deterministic silicon demands either the generation and storage of massive random matrices or brute-force Hamming-distance computation over the entire memory. What has been missing is an address decoder that preserves the相似性similarity（最佳匹配）原则，同时操作 RAM 的确定性和效率。这种工程空白——之间 概率生物学理论和确定性数字 计算——是(best-match) principle while operating with the determinism and efficiency of RAM. This engineering void—between probabilistic biological theory and deterministic digital computation—is the缺少链接missing link这使得 SDM 仅限于 几十年来一直是理论好奇心的领域。that has kept SDM confined to the realm of theoretical curiosity for decades.

3 VaCoAl 架构：AD 功能归纳开创的下一代语义推理基础The VaCoAl Architecture: A Next-Generation Semantic Reasoning Foundation Pioneered by AD Functional Induction

本节详细介绍 VaCoAl 如何以超高速、低功耗和低成本实现 Kanerva SDM 所寻求的数学要求，使用This section details how VaCoAl implements the mathematical requirements sought by Kanerva’s SDM at ultra-high speed, with low power consumption and low cost, using仅only标准数字技术（SRAM、DRAM 和数字逻辑电路）——无需依赖随机数生成器或专用模拟设备等概率组件。在其软件版本 (PyVaCoAl) 中，前沿尺寸为standard digital technology (SRAM, DRAM, and digital logic circuits)—without recourse to probabilistic components such as random-number generators or specialised analog devices. In its software incarnation (PyVaCoAl), at Frontier Sizes ofFS≥20,000\mathrm{FS}\geq 20{,}000对于大规模搜索，它比 Python DICT 快 15-20%；在 SRAM-CAM 硬件中，1/it is 15–20% faster than Python DICT for large-scale search; in SRAM-CAM hardware, the 1/NN理论上，块激活规则有望实现低功耗，尽管后者的实证验证仍然是未来工作的任务。block-activation rule is theoretically expected to deliver low power consumption, though empirical verification of the latter remains a task for future work.

计算复杂性的精确定义：什么“Precise Definition of Computational Complexity: What “O​(1)O(1)-like”的意思-like” Means

VaCoAl 的搜索成本与存储条目总数无关The search cost of VaCoAl is independent of the total number of stored entries|D​B||DB|。更准确地说，每个查询的计算量固定为. More precisely, the computation per query is fixed atO​(B⋅q)O(B\cdot q), 其中, whereBB是块的数量，is the number of blocks andq=L/Bq=L/B是每个块的位长度——这两个常数都是在系统设计时确定的。无论数据库从 100,000 个条目扩展到 1000 万个条目，单次查询查找的成本都保持不变。is the bit-length of each block—both constants determined at system-design time. Whether the database expands from 100,000 entries to 10 million, the cost of a single-query look-up remains unchanged.

这是我们使用“This is the basis for our use of the expression “O​(1)O(1)——就像。而传统的向量搜索需要-like.” Whereas conventional vector search requires anO​(|D​B|×D)O(|DB|\times D)扫描（所有条目scan (all entries×\times维），VaCoAl 具有通过伽罗瓦场扩散“直接跳转到正确答案的地址”的结构。的dimensionality), VaCoAl possesses a structure that “jumps directly to the address of the correct answer” via Galois-field diffusion. TheO​(1)O(1)在这个意义上，与in this sense occupies the same logical standing as theO​(1)O(1)的哈希函数。of a hash function.

在多阶段推理（多跳关联语义搜索）中，前沿大小 (FS) 设置控制搜索空间的上限。 FS 可能会随着数据库规模的增长而增长，但它是一个“搜索深度和广度的控制参数”，并且在概念上与每次查找成本不同In multi-stage reasoning (multi-hop associative semantic search), the Frontier Size (FS) setting governs the upper bound of the search space. FS may grow with database scale, but it is a “control parameter for search depth and breadth” and a conceptually distinct quantity from the per-look-up costO​(B⋅q)O(B\cdot q)。保持两者之间的明确区别对于准确评估架构的性能是必不可少的。. Maintaining a clear distinction between the two is indispensable for an accurate assessment of the architecture’s performance.

另一个值得注意的点是这种 AD 功能归纳所带来的信息处理能力的飞跃。传统的哈希函数仅限于单个谓词的精确匹配检索； VaCoAl，通过运用 HDC 风格的代数运算（绑定/解除绑定，A further point of note is the leap in information-processing capability that this AD functional induction engenders. Conventional hash functions are confined to exact-match retrieval on a single predicate; VaCoAl, by wielding HDC-style algebraic operations (Binding/Unbinding,等等etc.），可以将多谓词本体数据库的复杂语义结构直接高速处理为超高维向量。这一属性打破了现有算法的单谓词依赖性，实现了复杂的“多跳语义搜索”，可以快速且低成本地遍历庞大的关系网络。), can process the complex semantic structures of a multi-predicate ontology database directly as ultra-high-dimensional vectors at high speed. This property shatters the single-predicate dependency of existing algorithms, enabling sophisticated “Multi-Hop Semantic Searching” that traverses vast networks of relationships—rapidly and at low cost.

从这个意义上说，VaCoAl架构不仅提供了一种新的工程视角——“数字基底上的AD功能归纳”——作为物理复制神经科学模拟机制的传统方法的替代方案，而且构成了非常适合开拓新领域的下一代推理基础，例如阐明现有搜索引擎和法学硕士无法达到的迄今为止未知的知识结构和历史动态。In this sense, the VaCoAl architecture offers not merely a new engineering perspective—“AD functional induction on a digital substrate”—as an alternative to the conventional approach of physically reproducing neuroscientific analog mechanisms, but constitutes a next-generation reasoning foundation ideally suited to pioneering new fields, such as the elucidation of hitherto-unknown knowledge structures and historical dynamisms that lie beyond the reach of existing search engines and LLMs.

3.1 VaCoAl 的代数扩散和 Kanerva 的随机投影VaCoAl’s Algebraic Diffusion and Kanerva’s Random Projection

VaCoAl 正交化的定义特征是，它不是作为概率事故执行的，而是作为代数确定性执行的The defining characteristic of VaCoAl’s orthogonalisation is that it is executed not as a probabilistic accident but as an algebro-deterministic必要性necessity。为了清楚地说明这一点，我们将这个过程与 Kanerva 的稀疏分布式内存 (SDM) 采用的随机投影进行比较。. To make this clear, we formulate the process in comparison with the random projection employed by Kanerva’s Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM).

两个系统在映射输入数据时Both systems, when mapping input dataP​(x)P(x)进入高维空间，生成高维向量into a high-dimensional space, generate a high-dimensional vectorF​(x)F(x)它将“地址（转换部分）”（系统的内部语言）与“原始数据（保留部分）”集成在一起。从最广泛的意义上来说，两者都可以用以下通式来描述：that integrates an “address (transformation part)”—the system’s internal language—with the “original data (retention part).” In the broadest terms, both can be described by the following general formula:

F​(x)=xm​P​(x)+Ψ​(P​(x))F(x)=x^{m}\,P(x)+\Psi(P(x)) (1)

这里，第一项Here, the first termxm​P​(x)x^{m}\,P(x)是输入数据已移位到高位的区域（系统码中的信息点区域）；这对于两种模型来说都是共同的。决定性的区别在于第二项的生成机制，即变换函数is the region in which the input data has been shifted to the upper bits (the information-point region in the systematic code); this is common to both models. The decisive difference resides in the generation mechanism of the second term, the transformation functionΨ​(P​(x))\Psi(P(x)).

  • •

    Kanerva 随机投影 (SDM)：Kanerva’s Random Projection (SDM):在传统的 SDM 和超维计算中，这种变换被定义为概率投影函数In conventional SDM and Hyperdimensional Computing, this transformation is defined as a probabilistic projection functionΦr​a​n​d\Phi_{rand}使用固定的随机矩阵using a fixed random matrixMM:

    ΨS​D​M​(P​(x))=Φr​a​n​d​(P​(x))≈sgn​(M⋅p)\Psi_{SDM}(P(x))=\Phi_{rand}(P(x))\approx\mathrm{sgn}(M\cdot p) (2)

    哪里wheresgn​(u)\mathrm{sgn}(u)是符号函数，定义为：is the signum function defined as:

    sgn​(u)={+1(u>0)0(u=0)−1(u<0)\mathrm{sgn}(u)=\begin{cases}+1&(u>0)\\ \phantom{+}0&(u=0)\\ -1&(u<0)\end{cases} (3)

    正如这个定义所表明的，矩阵乘积的结果As this definition makes plain, the result of the matrix productM⋅pM\cdot p是一个模拟值（整数或实数），并且在这种情况下is an analog value (integer or real), and in the caseu=0u=0“第三状态”出现。因此，实际的 SDM 实现需要打破平局——强制将零分配给阈值步骤。a “third state” arises. Practical SDM implementations therefore require tie-breaking—a thresholding step that forcibly assigns the zero to+1+1 or −1-1。换句话说，为了获得“概率正交性”，Kanerva 的方法付出了大规模矩阵乘法的代价（. In other words, to obtain “probabilistic orthogonality,” Kanerva’s method pays the cost of a massive matrix multiplication (O​(N2)O(N^{2})) 加号plus解决此类歧义的额外开销。the additional overhead of resolving such ambiguities.

    用确定性计算代替随机投影的想法并不是这项工作所独有的：Kleyko 和 OsipovThe idea of replacing stochastic projections with deterministic computation is not unique to this work: Kleyko and Osipov[20]证明可以使用细胞自动机动力学而不是新鲜的随机性来克隆超维联想记忆。 VaCoAl 追求相同的确定性策略，但以伽罗瓦域代数为基础，产生准正交性的封闭形式保证以及作为 LFSR 的直接硬件实现。demonstrated that a hyperdimensional associative memory can be cloned using cellular-automata dynamics rather than fresh randomness. VaCoAl pursues the same deterministic strategy but grounds it in Galois-field algebra, which yields closed-form guarantees of quasi-orthogonality and a direct hardware realisation as an LFSR.

  • •

    VaCoAl 的代数确定性扩散：VaCoAl’s algebro-deterministic Diffusion:相比之下，VaCoAl 将此变换定义为相对于生成多项式的余数运算VaCoAl, by contrast, defines this transformation as a remainder operation with respect to a generator polynomialG​(x)G(x):

    ΨV​a​C​o​A​l​(P​(x))=xm​P​(x)(modG​(x))\Psi_{VaCoAl}(P(x))=x^{m}\,P(x)\pmod{G(x)} (4)

    方程（1）具体化为：Equation (1) is thereby concretised as:

    F​(x)=xm​P​(x)+(xm​P​(x)(modG​(x)))F(x)=x^{m}\,P(x)+\bigl(x^{m}\,P(x)\pmod{G(x)}\bigr) (5)

式（5）在形式上等价于编码理论（系统码）中BCH码的生成过程。然而，在 VaCoAl 中，这个公式作为关键的数学技巧，通过计算成本可忽略不计的 LFSR（线性反馈移位寄存器）和完全代数唯一性，实现了 Kanerva 使用随机矩阵和符号函数（连同其异常处理）完成的“信息的扩散和白噪声转换”。Equation (5) is formally equivalent to the generation process of a BCH code in coding theory (systematic code). In VaCoAl, however, this formula serves as the pivotal mathematical trick that realises, with an LFSR (Linear Feedback Shift Register) of negligible computational cost and with full algebraic uniqueness, the very “diffusion and white-noise conversion of information” that Kanerva accomplished with a random matrix and a signum function (together with its exception handling).

基于 LFSR 的代数扩散在计算方面非常高效，但随着数据规模增长到巨大比例，出现了新的工程挑战。具体来说，一旦目标样本的数量超过大约 500,000 个，在有限的地址空间内不可避免地会发生内存冲突（地址冲突），并且 HDC 所必需的正交化不再能够仅通过 LFSR 扩散来充分维持。为了防止正交性的退化并实现相对于边界大小的精确匹配精度（语义搜索的先决条件），除了 LFSR 扩散机制之外，还必须引入内存冲突避免电路（无论是内源性还是外源性）。LFSR-based algebraic diffusion is superbly efficient in computation, yet as data scales grow to massive proportions, a new engineering challenge emerges. Specifically, once the number of target samples exceeds roughly 500,000, memory collisions (address conflicts) inevitably occur within the finite address space, and the orthogonalisation essential to HDC can no longer be adequately maintained by LFSR diffusion alone. To forestall this degradation of orthogonality and to achieve Exact Match precision relative to the Frontier Size—a prerequisite for semantic search—a memory collision avoidance circuit,” whether endogenous or exogenous, must be introduced in addition to the LFSR diffusion mechanism.

这里的“内生”是指VaCoAl本身的内部结构天生就具有这种回避能力——比如通过扩大记忆深度（Here, ”endogenous” means that VaCoAl’s own internal structure inherently possesses such avoidance capability—for instance, by enlarging the memory depth (2m2^{m}）在没有外部干预的情况下使碰撞变得可以忽略不计； “外源性”意味着专用救援电路另外嵌入到本文描述的基本 VaCoAl 架构中，明确地拦截和解决内源性机制在给定存储深度下无法单独消除的冲突。内源性机制相对于外源性机制的优越性将在第 5 节中详细检查。) to render collisions negligibly rare without external intervention; exogenous” means that a dedicated rescue circuit is additionally embedded into the base VaCoAl architecture described herein, explicitly intercepting and resolving collisions that the endogenous mechanism alone cannot eliminate at a given memory depth. The superiority of the endogenous mechanism over the exogenous one will be examined in detail in Section 5.

但在实践中，两者并不相互排斥，而是相辅相成。在本文实证实验（第 5 节）的大数据环境中，涉及超过 2550 万条记录，碰撞概率呈指数级上升，单一内生层和单一外生层都不够；In practice, however, the two are not mutually exclusive but complementary. In the big-data environment of this paper’s empirical experiments (Section 5), which involves upwards of 25.5 million records, collision probability escalates exponentially, and neither a single endogenous nor a single exogenous tier suffices;多级multi-stage需要以越来越高的精度集成这两种原理的防碰撞电路。至关重要的是，这种多级电路并不是仅限于 VaCoAl 未来硬件实现 (SRAM-CAM) 的理论规范。在本文用于大规模实证演示的专有程序“PyVaCoAl”中，高精度多级防碰撞电路完全实现为事实上的DRAM-CAM。正是这种强大的架构基础，即使在千万条记录规模的多跳语义逻辑推理中，也能在救援模式（救援电路ON）下将数据冲突完全抑制为零，在给定的Frontier Size内实现精确匹配。相比之下，在“无关”模式（救援电路关闭）下，可以容忍微小的冲突，以换取性质不同的功能：基于 CR2 路径积分的语义选择（请参见第 3.5 节）。collision-avoidance circuitry integrating both principles at progressively higher precision is required. Crucially, this multi-stage circuit is not a theoretical specification limited to a future hardware implementation (SRAM-CAM) of VaCoAl. In the proprietary programme “PyVaCoAl” used for large-scale empirical demonstration in this paper, the high-precision multi-stage collision-avoidance circuit is fully implemented as a de facto DRAM-CAM. It is precisely this robust architectural underpinning that enables, even in multi-hop semantic logical reasoning at the scale of tens of millions of records, the complete suppression of data collisions to zero in Rescue mode (rescue circuit ON), achieving Exact Match within the given Frontier Size. In Don’t Care mode (rescue circuit OFF), by contrast, minute collisions are tolerated in exchange for a qualitatively different capability: semantic selection based on the CR2 path integral (see Section 3.5).

为什么是伽罗华域？有限资源的有效利用和正交性的保证Why Galois Fields? Effective Use of Finite Resources and the Guarantee of Orthogonality

在深入研究方程（5）的细节之前，有必要解释一下为什么“伽罗瓦域”这个数学概念会出现在这里——似乎是凭空而来——以及是什么实际需要推动了它的采用。Before delving into the details of equation (5), it is necessary to explain why the mathematical concept of “Galois fields” appears here—seemingly out of nowhere—and what practical necessity drives its adoption.

在任何真实的计算机中，内存容量和计算资源都是有限的。无论计算多么精细，数字都无法无限制地增殖；每个操作的结果都必须符合预定的“有限世界”。这一点对于二元超高维空间中的 VaCoAl 向量正交化等算法尤其重要。对于 PyVaCoAl 来说，这一点变得更加重要，因为它允许超高维空间的维数接近一百万。在数学中，一个集合（或代数系统），其中四个算术运算可以自由执行，并且其结果始终保证保持在原始集合内（即该集合是In any real computer, memory capacity and computational resources are finite. No matter how elaborate the computation, digits cannot proliferate without bound; the result of every operation must fit within a predetermined “finite world.” This point is particularly salient for algorithms such as VaCoAl’s vector orthogonalisation in a binary ultra-high-dimensional space. It becomes still more critical for PyVaCoAl, which permits the dimensionality of the ultra-high-dimensional space to approach one million. In mathematics, a set (or algebraic system) in which the four arithmetic operations can be performed freely and whose results are guaranteed always to remain within the original set (i.e. the set is关闭closed) 被称为) is called a领域field.

VaCoAl 采用伽罗瓦域（有限域），因为它们是传输二进制数据（0 和 1）的计算机的理想“域”。对于 VaCoAl 来说，这种“在封闭世界中循环”具有决定性的重要性。只有通过在有界空间内循环运算——通过“取余数”——人们才能在相互无关的方向上爆炸相似的输入数据，从而以代数确定性的方式在高维空间中创建“正交性”。VaCoAl adopts Galois fields (finite fields) because they are the ideal “field” for a computer that traffics in binary data (0s and 1s). For VaCoAl, this “cycling within a closed world” is of decisive importance. It is only by cycling operations within a bounded space—by “taking remainders”—that one can blast similar input data in mutually unrelated directions, thereby creating “orthogonality” in the high-dimensional space in an algebro-deterministic fashion.

具体来说，Concretely, the(modG​(x))\pmod{G(x)}方程（5）第二项中的运算通过伽罗瓦域固有的属性产生确定性的“雪崩效应”，如下所述。为了深入理解这一机制，让我们逐步解开公式描述的物理位级过程。operation in the second term of equation (5) engenders, through properties intrinsic to Galois fields, a deterministic “Avalanche Effect,” as elaborated below. To develop a deep understanding of this mechanism, let us unpack, step by step, the physical bit-level process described by the formula.

By treating a bit string not as a mere numerical value but as the coefficients of a polynomial, complex bit operations (carry shifts) and XOR (exclusive OR)—which in a Galois field correspond to addition and subtraction—can be handled uniformly through the mathematically perspicuous formalism of “polynomial division.” The representation of the polynomialP​(x)P(x) in nn维空间采用以下形式，其中-dimensional space takes the following form, wherenn是一个非常大的数并且大部分系数is a very large number and most of the coefficientsaia_{i}为零：are zero:

The XOR rule: if two bits are the same (1&1 or 0&0), the result is 0; if they differ (1&0 or 0&1), the result is 1. It is through this process that the “Avalanche Effect” on the input binary vector is triggered.

This “50% inversion” is the geometric key. 为什么这很重要？Why Does This Matter?

伽罗瓦域中除法的最大好处是“雪崩效应”，即输入的微小变化会产生输出的巨大差异。与普通算术不同，异或除法不涉及进位吸收（平滑）。因此，单个输入位的变化会触发类似于推倒多米诺骨牌的连锁反应，从而改变最终的余数The supreme benefit of division in a Galois field is the “Avalanche Effect,” whereby a minute change in the input produces a dramatic difference in the output. Unlike ordinary arithmetic, division by XOR involves no carry absorption (smoothing). A change of a single input bit therefore triggers a chain reaction akin to toppling dominoes, transforming the final remainderR​(x)R(x)变成完全不同的值。通过这一数学特性，VaCoAl 实现了以下关键功能：into an entirely different value. Through this mathematical property, VaCoAl realises the following critical functions:

The generatednn维向量-dimensional vectorsF​(x)F(x)在扩展的向量空间中占据几乎“正交”的位置。come to occupy nearly “orthogonal” positions in the expanded vector space.

The value ofmm确定单个 SRAM/DRAM 块内的“搅拌深度”并用于防止局部冲突。的价值determines the “depth of stirring” within a single SRAM/DRAM block and serves to prevent local collisions. The value ofnn相比之下，是整个系统的“内存总量”——支持类脑大规模并行处理的基础。据此，, by contrast, is the “total volume of memory” for the system as a whole—the substrate that supports brain-like massively parallel processing. Accordingly,nn的数量级大于is orders of magnitude larger thanmm.

二十岁天才的预言The Prophecy of a Twenty-Year-Old Genius

最后，谈谈伽罗瓦域理论的起源。埃瓦里斯特·伽罗瓦 (Évariste Galois) 奠定了这一基础，他在 1832 年的一场决斗中阵亡，年仅 20 岁。他在告别信中留下了“伽罗瓦域（有限域）”——特别是A word, finally, on the provenance of Galois-field theory. The foundations were laid by Évariste Galois, who fell in a duel in 1832 at the age of just twenty. The “Galois field (finite field)” he bequeathed in his farewell letter—in particular the world ofG​F​(2)GF(2)令人惊讶的是，现代计算机运行的平台是为二进制机器定制的。在这个二进制世界中，“加法”和“减法”的行为与异或相同，而十进制算术中熟悉的“进位”根本不存在。正是这种“无进位算术”才是爆炸性信息搅动的引擎。两个世纪后，天才伽罗瓦的预言理论已成为在 VaCoAl（一种“基于记忆的架构”）中再现大脑灵活性的关键。with which modern computers operate—was, astonishingly, a stage custom-built for the binary machine. In this binary world, “addition” and “subtraction” behave identically as XOR, and the “carry” familiar from decimal arithmetic simply does not exist. It is precisely this “carry-free arithmetic” that is the engine of explosive information stirring. The prophetic theory of the genius Galois, two centuries later, has become the key to reproducing the suppleness of the brain within VaCoAl—a “memory-based architecture.”

FS相对论——基本问题的预览FS Relativity—A Preview of the Fundamental Issue

前面对内存冲突避免电路的讨论暗示了一个更基本的问题：在受组合爆炸困扰的大规模图搜索中，“绝对精确匹配”到底意味着什么？本文通过“FS 相对精确匹配”的概念形式化了这个问题，并在第 5 节中用实验数据详细研究了它。作为预览，我们在此明确声明了以下立场：The foregoing discussion of the memory-collision avoidance circuit intimates a more fundamental question: in large-scale graph searches beset by combinatorial explosion, what does “absolute Exact Match” even mean? This paper formalises the question through the concept of “FS-relative Exact Match” and examines it in detail, with experimental data, in Section 5. As a preview, we state explicitly here the position thatFS 相对论不是 VaCoAl 的限制，而是组合爆炸问题本身的固有属性FS relativity is not a limitation of VaCoAl but an intrinsic property of the combinatorial-explosion problem itself.

3.2 模拟扩散的数字实现：随机投影的AD功能归纳Digital Implementation of Analog Diffusion: AD Functional Induction of Random Projection

在 Kanerva 的 SDM 和生物大脑模型中，将输入模式映射到高维空间的过程称为“随机投影”。从数学上来说，它相当于将输入向量乘以一个巨大的随机矩阵。从生物学上讲，它模拟了感觉信号通过突触连接物理分支和扩散（模拟扩散）到许多神经元的过程。这种“扩散”确保信息在整个空间中均匀传播，没有偏见，保证了“正交性”。In Kanerva’s SDM and biological brain models, the process of mapping input patterns into a high-dimensional space is called “random projection.” Mathematically, it corresponds to multiplying the input vector by a massive random matrix. Biologically, it models the process by which sensory signals physically branch and diffuse (analog diffusion) to many neurons through synaptic connections. This “diffusion” ensures that information spreads uniformly across the entire space without bias, guaranteeing “orthogonality.”

然而，在数字电路上忠实地再现这种“矩阵计算（模拟扩散）”需要大量的计算资源和浮点运算。However, faithfully reproducing this “matrix computation (analog diffusion)” on digital circuits demands enormous computational resources and floating-point arithmetic.

VaCoAl 如何解决这个问题？How Does VaCoAl Solve This?

VaCoAl 通过将生物函数功能转换（归纳）为“伽罗瓦域上的多项式运算”来解决这个问题。上述“移位”和“异或”运算具有彻底扰乱输入信息比特的数学特性。不需要复杂的计算机器；整个过程可以通过基本数字电路 LFSR（线性反馈移位寄存器）来实现。 LFSR只不过是一种移位和循环位串的简单结构，但它能高速生成统计上优异的随机模式，从而显着提高系统的资源效率。VaCoAl solves the problem by functionally converting (inducing) biological functions into “polynomial operations over Galois fields.” The “shift” and “XOR” operations described above possess the mathematical property of thoroughly scrambling the bits of the input information. No elaborate computing machinery is required; the entire process can be implemented with an LFSR (Linear Feedback Shift Register)—a basic digital circuit. An LFSR is nothing more than a simple structure that shifts and cycles a bit string, yet it generates statistically excellent random patterns at high speed, dramatically boosting the resource efficiency of the system.

通过这种简单的操作，可以确定性地生成“理想随机分布”，而无需任何随机数生成器。换句话说，VaCoAl成功地将神经网络“在整个空间中物理扩散信息”的功能重构为硅芯片上的“代数运算”。这也是神经形态计算中实现模式分离和模式完成（在海马系统内执行的操作）的“AD 功能诱导”的核心属性，如下所述。Through such simple operations, an “ideal random distribution” can be generated deterministically, without any random-number generator. VaCoAl has, in other words, successfully reconstructed the neural network’s function of “physically diffusing information across the entire space” as an “algebraic operation” on a silicon chip. This is also the core property that enables the “AD functional induction” of pattern separation and pattern completion—operations performed within the hippocampal system—in neuromorphic computing, as discussed later.

随机独立性与线性正交性的区别及拟正交性的建立The Distinction between Stochastic Independence and Linear Orthogonality, and the Establishment of Quasi-Orthogonality

区分向量分量的“随机独立性”和向量的“线性代数正交性”至关重要。在标准 HDC 中，随机数生成用于生成满足前者（独立性）的分量，从而得出后者（正交性）的结果。It is critically important to distinguish between “stochastic independence” of vector components and “linear-algebraic orthogonality” of vectors. In standard HDC, random-number generation is used to produce components that satisfy the former (independence), from which the latter (orthogonality) follows as a consequence.

相比之下，VaCoAl 的扩散过程是代数确定性的，因此生成的比特不具有真正的随机独立性。 VaCoAl 通过利用由本原多项式保证的“最大长度序列（m 序列）”的数学特性进行补偿VaCoAl’s diffusion process, by contrast, is algebro-deterministic, so the generated bits do not possess genuine stochastic independence. VaCoAl compensates by leveraging the mathematical properties of the “maximal-length sequence (m-sequence)” guaranteed by the primitive polynomialG​(x)G(x)，从而直接获得后者——正交性。, thereby acquiring the latter—orthogonality—directly.

正如上一节详细介绍的，“数学混合器”的作用As detailed in the preceding section, the action of the “mathematical mixer”==严格设计的本原多项式产生的“雪崩效应”“Avalanche Effect” produced by the rigorously designed primitive polynomialG​(x)G(x)仅通过确定性计算且无需生成概率随机数，就能保证“50% 反转规则”在统计上成立。这确保了，随着超高维空间的维数增加，生成的向量之间的相关性（内积）相对收敛于零——这是数学上保证的属性。我们将其称为“准正交性”。guarantees, through deterministic computation alone and without probabilistic random-number generation, that the “50% inversion rule” holds statistically. This ensures that, as the dimensionality of the ultra-high-dimensional space increases, the correlation (inner product) between generated vectors converges relatively to zero—a property mathematically guaranteed. We term this “quasi-orthogonality.”

换句话说，VaCoAl 通过确定性代数结构而不用掷骰子，再现了 Kanerva 的属性In other words, VaCoAl reproduces, through a deterministic algebraic structure and without rolling a single die, the property that Kanerva[18]指定为“维度的祝福”：“在高维空间中，随机向量在几何上几乎是正交的。”designated the “Blessing of Dimensionality”: “in high-dimensional spaces, random vectors are geometrically almost orthogonal.”

然而，这种代数获得的准正交性不可避免地与完美正交性（零相关）存在微小偏差。随着目标样本的数量从数十万增加到数千万，这些微小的相关性不断累积，不可避免地增加了内存冲突（地址冲突）的可能性。为了突破这一扩展障碍，存在两种互补的策略。第一个是内源性的：扩大记忆深度（This algebraically acquired quasi-orthogonality, however, inevitably harbours minute deviations from perfect orthogonality (zero correlation). As the number of target samples scales from hundreds of thousands into the tens of millions, these minute correlations accumulate, ineluctably raising the probability of memory collisions (address conflicts). Two complementary strategies exist for breaking through this scaling barrier. The first is endogenous: enlarging the memory depth (2m2^{m}），这样地址空间本身就变得足够大，使得冲突很少发生，可以忽略不计——这种方法完整地保留了 VaCoAl 的原生 CR2 路径辨别能力。第二个是外生的：引入专用的多级救援电路，明确拦截并解决给定内存深度无法消除的冲突。实际上，正如前面所讨论的，两者并不相互排斥；大规模部署受益于它们的集成，内生机制抑制大量碰撞，外生电路清除残留物。) so that the address space itself becomes vast enough to render collisions negligibly rare—an approach that preserves VaCoAl’s native CR2 path-discriminating power intact. The second is exogenous: introducing a dedicated multi-stage rescue circuit that explicitly intercepts and resolves the collisions that a given memory depth cannot eliminate. In practice, as discussed earlier, the two are not mutually exclusive; large-scale deployments benefit from their integration, with the endogenous mechanism suppressing the bulk of collisions and the exogenous circuit mopping up the residue.

本文的 VaCoAl 架构（及其软件实现 PyVaCoAl）结合了这两种策略。通过确定性准正交性、通过大存储深度进行内源性碰撞抑制以及外源性多级救援电路的结合，VaCoAl克服了Kanerva的SDM依赖于纯概率的局限性，并面临着由于数据饱和而导致灾难性故障（淹没在噪声海洋中）的风险。简而言之，VaCoAl是SDM的“更高精度、更稳健的扩展架构”，即使在大数据环境下也能够充分保护和分离信息独立性。这种鲁棒性体现在两种互补的操作模式中：救援模式中的 FS 相对精确匹配（其中外源电路处于活动状态）和不关心模式中基于路径质量的语义选择（其中内源机制单独控制，并且驱动紧急语义选择的 CR2 衰减被保留）。The VaCoAl architecture of this paper (and its software implementation, PyVaCoAl) incorporates both strategies. Through the combination of deterministic quasi-orthogonality, endogenous collision suppression via large memory depth, and exogenous multi-stage rescue circuitry, VaCoAl overcomes the limitations of Kanerva’s SDM, which relies on pure probability and faces the risk of catastrophic failure (drowning in a sea of noise) due to data saturation. VaCoAl is, in short, a “higher-precision, more robust extension architecture” of SDM, capable of fully protecting and separating information independence even in big-data environments. This robustness manifests in two complementary operating modes: FS-relative Exact Match in Rescue mode (where the exogenous circuit is active), and path-quality-based semantic selection in Don’t Care mode (where the endogenous mechanism alone governs, and the CR2 decay that drives emergent semantic selection is preserved).

与BCH代码的决定性区别（“严格恢复”与“灵活关联”）The Decisive Difference from BCH Codes (“Strict Restoration” versus “Flexible Association”)

为了欣赏 VaCoAl 的独创性和创新性，我们将其与 BCH 代码进行比较——通信和存储中使用的标准纠错技术（SSD、To appreciate VaCoAl’s originality and innovativeness, a comparison with BCH codes—the standard error-correction technology used in communications and storage (SSDs,等等etc.）——缺一不可。两者都采用相同的数学引擎，“伽罗瓦域上的多项式运算”，但它们的目的和应用向量却截然相反。)—is indispensable. Both employ the same mathematical engine, “polynomial operations over Galois fields,” yet their purposes and vectors of application are diametrically opposite.

  • •

    BCH 代码（通信/存储）：BCH Codes (Communications / Storage):目标是“收敛到一点”和“完美还原”。传统的BCH是一个“严格的校对者”，可以保护数据免受噪音影响。当数据损坏时，它会执行计算量大的代数逆运算（校正式解码），以严格识别和修复错误。简而言之，所有计算能力都消耗在“将损坏的数据返回（收敛）到过去的正确答案”上。The objective is “convergence to a single point” and “perfect restoration.” Conventional BCH is a “strict proofreader” that protects data from noise. When data is corrupted, it performs computationally expensive algebraic inverse operations (syndrome decoding) to identify and repair errors with rigour. In short, all computational power is expended on “returning (converging) corrupted data to the correct answer of the past.”

  • •

    VaCoAl（脑型记忆）：VaCoAl (Brain-Type Memory):其方法是“扩散到太空”和“统计共识+确定性保证”。 VaCoAl 完全放弃了繁重的解码过程（逆运算）。相反，它使用多项式运算来“搅拌”（扰乱）输入数据。通过雪崩效应，微小的输入变化会波及整个输出，从而将数据概率性地分散到整个存储空间。然后，纠错（修复）会立即完成——不是通过复杂的逆运算，而是通过 SRAM 或 DRAM 阵列上的并行“统计共识”（多数投票）。特别值得注意的是，这种统计修复过程并不仅仅止于单纯的概率近似（粗略推理）。由于上述“多级内存防冲突电路”同时运行，完全消除了多数表决特有的噪声和地址冲突风险，并且救援模式（救援电路ON）确定性地实现了相对于Frontier Size的“精确匹配”精度，同时保持了灵活的关联能力。然而，正如第 5.3 节中所讨论的，在这种救援模式下，CR2 的路径辨别能力确实丢失了，这需要与语义选择能力进行权衡。另一方面，如果救援模式关闭并且内存深度（The approach is “diffusion into space” and “statistical consensus + deterministic guarantee.” VaCoAl entirely abandons the heavy decoding process (inverse operations). Instead, it uses polynomial operations to “stir” (scramble) the input data. Through the Avalanche Effect, minute input changes ripple across the entire output, probabilistically scattering data throughout the memory space. Error correction (repair) is then completed instantaneously—not by complex inverse operations but by parallel “Statistical Consensus” (Majority Voting) on SRAM or DRAM arrays. What is particularly noteworthy is that this statistical repair process does not terminate in mere probabilistic approximation (rough reasoning). Because the aforementioned “multi-stage memory collision avoidance circuit” operates concurrently, the risks of noise and address conflict peculiar to majority voting are completely eliminated, and Rescue mode (rescue circuit ON) deterministically achieves the precision of “Exact Match” relative to the Frontier Size while maintaining flexible associative capabilities. However, as discussed in Section 5.3, in this Rescue mode the path-discriminating power of CR2 is indeed lost, presenting a trade-off with semantic selection capability. If, on the other hand, the Rescue mode is turned off and the memory depth (2m2^{m}）在每个块内增加，内存冲突避免本身可以被驱动到可以忽略不计的小水平（参见附录B.3）。) within each block is increased, memory collision avoidance itself can be driven to negligibly small levels (see Appendix B.3).555例如，这种状态实际上可以通过内存宽度消除For example, this state can be virtually eliminated with memory widths of2272^{27}, 2262^{26}, 2252^{25}, 2242^{24}分别适用于 128、256、512 和 1024 块的配置。for configurations of 128, 256, 512, and 1024 blocks, respectively.（详情请参阅附录 B.3。）(For details, see Appendix B.3.)

即VaCoAl放弃了BCH“严格通过计算修复”的做法，完成了哥伦布蛋式的逆转：“通过计算分散，通过统计（多数投票）聚集”。饰演 松本That is, VaCoAl abandons BCH’s approach of “strictly repairing by computation” and accomplishes a Columbus-egg-like reversal: “scatter by computation, gather by statistics (majority voting).” As Matsumoto[25]和安德森and Anderson[6]观察到，“从根本上来说，大脑的功能就像一台推理机器，它优先考虑检索和关联而不是计算。”这种见解与 VaCoAl 的基本原则非常一致，而且这种架构从计算的魔咒中解放出来，因此实现了超越传统限制的压倒性速度和低功耗。observe, “fundamentally, the brain functions as a reasoning machine that prioritises retrieval and association over computation.” This insight is profoundly consonant with VaCoAl’s foundational principles, and this architecture, liberated from the spell of computation, consequently achieves overwhelming speed and low power consumption that transcend conventional limits.

生物学合理性：海马体中的硬连线随机投影Biological Plausibility: Hardwired Random Projection in the Hippocampus

值得注意的是，VaCoAl 的工程方法——用固定逻辑电路代替正交化（随机投影）步骤——表现出与实际大脑惊人的功能同源性。菲特的研究It is remarkable that VaCoAl’s engineering approach—substituting a fixed logic circuit for the orthogonalisation (random projection) step—exhibits a striking functional homology with the actual brain. Research by Fiete等人。et al. [12, 40, 7]，特别是钱德拉, and in particular Chandra等人。et al. [7]研究表明，由内嗅皮层 (EC) 中的网格细胞形成的残数系统 (RNS) 代码作为固定的随机投影投射到海马 CA3 区域，为大量情景记忆存储“充当不可变的支架”。 令人惊讶的是，这种随机的布线结构——信息的容器——是在幼儿期有限的经历中完成并冻结的；然后，所有后续的内存处理都作为正交映射到这个不变容器上执行。这与婴儿失忆症的数学基础是一致的（另见 Stoencheva, has shown that the Residue Number System (RNS) code formed by grid cells in the entorhinal cortex (EC) is projected onto the hippocampal CA3 region as a fixed random projection,” serving as an immutable scaffold” for the vast store of episodic memory. What is astonishing is that this random wiring structure—the vessel for information—is completed and frozen during the limited experiences of early childhood; all subsequent memory processes are then executed as orthogonalising mappings onto this invariant vessel. This is consonant with the mathematical underpinnings of infantile amnesia (see also Stoencheva等人。et al. [41]）。换句话说，生物大脑并不是每次都重新执行复杂的随机计算；而是每次都重新进行复杂的随机计算。相反，它). In other words, the biological brain does not perform elaborate random computations afresh each time; rather, it硬连线hardwires在婴儿期将随机投影作为物理电路，此后只需通过该电路路由信号即可保证正交化。 VaCoAl 将正交化机制实现为 SRAM 上固定 LFSR 电路的策略，从最字面的意义上来说，是这一生物事实的数字逻辑再现——AD 功能归纳法的最终实例。”the random projection as a physical circuit during infancy, and thereafter guarantees orthogonalisation simply by routing signals through that circuit. VaCoAl’s strategy of implementing the orthogonalisation mechanism as a fixed LFSR circuit on SRAM is, in the most literal sense, a digital-logic reproduction of this biological truth—the ultimate instance of AD Functional Induction.”

3.3 分而治之：分布式存储的归纳Divide and Conquer: Induction of Distributed Storage

从物理上来说，将数千到数万位的分散高维向量视为单个内存地址是不可能的。 （1,000 位地址需要It is physically impossible to treat a diffused high-dimensional vector of thousands to tens of thousands of bits as a single memory address. (A 1,000-bit address requires21,0002^{1{,}000}VaCoAl 和 PyVaCoAl 通过根据可用的 SRAM/DRAM 容量将这些向量“分割”为多个块（128、256、512 或 1024）并将它们分配到并行的 SRAM/DRAM 组来解决这个问题。memory cells—exceeding the number of atoms in the universe.) VaCoAl and PyVaCoAl solve this problem by “segmenting” these vectors into multiple blocks—128, 256, 512, or 1024, according to the available SRAM/DRAM capacity—and distributing them to parallel SRAM/DRAM groups.

3.3.1 通过分布式表示和树突计算进行 SRAM/DRAM 阵列之间的功能类比Functional Analogy between SRAM/DRAM Arrays via Distributed Representations and Dendritic Computation

输入的高维向量（长度The input high-dimensional vector (lengthLL) 分为) is divided intoNN块，每个长度blocks, each of lengthq=L/Nq=L/N位。每个块用作独立的 SRAM/DRAM 地址bits. Each block functions as an independent SRAM/DRAM address with2q2^{q}的话。在写入（学习）阶段，与输入数据的概念相对应的唯一“入口地址（EA）”被写入每个SRAM/DRAM块中的相应地址。words. In the write (learning) phase, a unique “Entry Address (EA)” corresponding to the concept of the input data is written to the corresponding address in every SRAM/DRAM block.

同时继承了Kanerva分布式内存的基本原理While inheriting the basic principle of Kanerva’s distributed memory[17]，该架构在结构上有以下几个方面的不同。首先，Kanerva 模型使用基于汉明距离的概率地址选择，而本方法在每个块内执行确定性的精确或最佳匹配检查。其次，内存分配和恢复是通过封闭代数运算（块划分和多数投票）来保证的，在这个框架中，输入输出关系完全由有限集上的离散运算决定，而不依赖于随机过程（本文将其称为随机过程）。, this architecture differs structurally in the following respects. First, whereas the Kanerva model uses probabilistic address selection based on Hamming distance, the present method performs deterministic exact- or best-match checking within each block. Second, memory distribution and recovery are guaranteed by closed algebraic operations—block division and majority voting—a framework in which input–output relationships are completely determined by discrete operations over finite sets, without recourse to stochastic processes (this paper terms this代数决定论algebro-deterministic）。通过将单个输入事件分布在多个 SRAM/DRAM 块上，针对局部故障和噪声的鲁棒性直接源自这种代数确定性结构。). By distributing a single input event across multiple SRAM/DRAM blocks, robustness against local failures and noise is directly derived from this algebro-deterministic structure.

此外，值得注意的是，这种通过块划分和多数投票的决策方法在结构上类似于生物神经元中的树突计算。根据波伊拉齐的说法Furthermore, it is noteworthy that this decision method by block division and majority voting is structurally analogous to Dendritic Computation in biological neurons. According to Poirazi等人。et al. [33]和伦敦and London等人。et al. [24]在大脑皮层和海马体的锥体细胞中，每个树突分支作为独立的计算单元执行局部非线性处理，体细胞在空间上整合这些输出以生成轴突的最终输出——充当一种“双层神经网络”。本地检查, in pyramidal cells of the cerebral cortex and hippocampus, each dendritic branch performs local nonlinear processing as an independent computational unit, and the soma spatially integrates those outputs to generate the final output from the axon—functioning as a sort of “two-layer neural network.” The local checking acrossNNVaCoAl 中的独立块随后进行多数投票整合，与表 2 中所示的生物分层结构具有对应关系。independent blocks followed by majority-vote integration in VaCoAl shares the correspondence with this biological hierarchical structure shown in Table 2.

然而，这种类比存在明显的局限性。尽管分级的、时间相关的非线性（例如 NMDA 尖峰）在生物树突中占主导地位，但本方法中的局部检查是离散的精确匹配或最佳匹配决策。此外，体细胞中的积分是一个非线性过程，伴随着距离相关的衰减，这与该方法的等权多数投票完全不同。因此，在本文中，我们将两者之间的关系定位为数学同构而非数学同构。However, a clear limit to this analogy exists. Whereas graded, time-dependent nonlinearities such as NMDA spikes are dominant in biological dendrites, local checking in the present method is a discrete exact- or best-match decision. Additionally, integration in the soma is a nonlinear process accompanied by distance-dependent decay, which differs strictly from the equal-weight majority voting of this method. Therefore, in this paper, we position the relationship between the two not as a mathematical isomorphism but as a结构流等价structural flow equivalence计算流程：“除法of the computational flow: “division→\rightarrow本地处理local processing→\rightarrow整合”。integration.”

3.3.2 通过分布式表示的 SRAM/DRAM 阵列SRAM/DRAM Arrays via Distributed Representations

输入的高维向量（长度The input high-dimensional vector (lengthLL) 分为) is divided intoNN块，每个长度blocks, each of lengthq=L/Nq=L/N位。每个块用作独立的 SRAM/DRAM 地址bits. Each block functions as an independent SRAM/DRAM address with2q2^{q}的话。在写入（学习）阶段，与输入数据的概念相对应的唯一“入口地址（EA）”被写入每个SRAM/DRAM块中的相应地址。该架构不仅优化了Kanerva的“分布式内存”概念，而且还优化了Kanerva的“分布式内存”概念。它将生物模型的“依赖于神经元放电随机性的记忆”转换为代数确定性的“分而治之”策略，与数字记忆的物理结构完全一致。通过将单个事件（输入）分布在多个 SRAM/DRAM（对应于神经元组）上，可以代数确定性地生成针对局部故障和噪声的强大恢复能力。words. In the write (learning) phase, a unique “Entry Address (EA)” corresponding to the concept of the input data is written to the corresponding address in every SRAM/DRAM block. This architecture does not merely optimise Kanerva’s concept of “distributed memory”; it converts the biological model’s “memory relying on the stochasticity of neuron firing” into an algebro-deterministic “divide and conquer” strategy perfectly aligned with the physical structure of digital memory. By distributing a single event (input) across multiple SRAM/DRAM (corresponding to neuron groups), robust recovery resilience against local failures and noise is generated algebro-deterministically.

3.3.3 碰撞容限（无关）Collision Tolerance (Don’t Care)

在传统的散列方法或搜索树中，“冲突”（指向同一地址的不同数据）被视为致命错误，需要不惜一切代价避免。然而，VaCoAl“并不害怕碰撞”。当给定块中发生冲突时（即新数据尝试覆盖现有的有效条目），系统只需在该块上设置“冲突标志”并将其视为“不关心”（忽略）信息。为什么这是允许的？因为有In conventional hashing methods or search trees, “collisions”—different data pointing to the same address—are treated as fatal errors to be avoided at all costs. VaCoAl, however, “does not fear collisions.” When a collision occurs in a given block (i.e. new data attempts to overwrite an existing valid entry), the system simply sets a “collision flag” on that block and treats it as “Don’t Care” (ignored) information. Why is this permissible? Because there areNN块（例如 1,000）。即使冲突发生在 50 个区块中，假设剩余 950 个区块包含正确的信息。这实现了大脑的冗余——即使某些神经元失火或死亡，功能也能维持——作为一种强大的数字逻辑状态。blocks (e.g. 1,000). Even if collisions occur in 50 blocks, suppose the remaining 950 hold correct information. This implements the brain’s redundancy—where function is maintained even if some neurons misfire or die—as a robust digital-logic state.

更值得注意的是，这种Don’t Care机制并不局限于单一搜索过程中的“空间噪声容忍（冗余）”；在连续推理（多跳搜索）中，它表现为“时间和基于路径的选择函数”。正如第 5 节的实证实验将证明的那样，随着搜索世代的加深，由于“不关心”而导致的微小衰减会像复利一样累积，从而产生“自然地修剪长而迂回的路线并选择性地保留短而直接的路线”（奥卡姆剃刀）的惊人行为。架构的物理约束无意中成为产生复杂“语义选择”的决定性机制。What is still more remarkable is that this Don’t Care mechanism is not confined to “spatial noise tolerance (redundancy)” in a single search process; in continuous reasoning (multi-hop search), it manifests as a “temporal and path-based selection function.” As the empirical experiments of Section 5 will demonstrate, as search generations deepen, the minute decay due to Don’t Care accumulates like compound interest, giving rise to the astonishing behaviour of “naturally pruning long, circuitous routes and selectively preserving short, direct ones” (Occam’s razor). The physical constraints of the architecture inadvertently become a decisive mechanism engendering sophisticated “semantic selection.”

3.4 代数多数投票：通过决定论再现统计推断Algebraic Majority Voting: Reproducing Statistical Inference via Determinism

在读阶段，系统如何从噪声中恢复正确的内存？ VaCoAl采用“全局代数多数投票”而非简单的随机处理。In the read phase, how does the system recover the correct memory from noise? VaCoAl adopts “Global Algebraic Majority Voting” rather than simple random processing.

重要的是，虽然这个过程是“严格计算（决定论）”，但它具有相当于“理想随机数（概率论）”的性质。正如前面所解释的，伽罗瓦域运算的数据扩散在数学上保证具有“伪随机”的均匀分布。因此，消除了对特定模式的偏见，并且统计“大数定律”在不掷物理骰子的情况下成立。The important point is that, although this process is a “strict computation (determinism),” it possesses properties equivalent to an “ideal random number (probability theory).” Data diffusion by Galois-field operations is mathematically guaranteed to have a uniform distribution as “Pseudo-Random,” as explained earlier. Therefore, bias toward specific patterns is eliminated, and the statistical “Law of Large Numbers” holds without rolling physical dice.

简而言之，该方法通过“代数确定性计算的准确性（再现性）”实现了“概率多数投票的强度（鲁棒性）”。In short, this method realises the “strength (robustness) of probabilistic majority voting” through the “accuracy (reproducibility) of algebro-deterministic computation.”

3.4.1 通过雪崩效应过滤噪声Noise Filtering via the Avalanche Effect

对搜索查询（输入数据）执行相同的代数确定性扩散和分割。入口地址是从每个 SRAM/DRAM 块中读取的。如果输入包含噪声会发生什么？由于伽罗瓦场扩散过程的雪崩效应，输入位模式中的微小错误会将该特定块的地址代数确定性地更改为完全不可预测的随机位置。The same algebro-deterministic diffusion and segmentation are performed on the search query (input data). The Entry Address is read from each SRAM/DRAM block. What happens if the input contains noise? Due to the Avalanche Effect of the Galois-field diffusion process, a slight error in the input bit pattern changes the address of that specific block algebro-deterministically into a completely unpredictable, random location.

  • •

    包含噪声的块：Blocks containing noise:由于这些访问不相关的地址，因此它们输出随机条目地址（本底噪声）或空值。Because these access unrelated addresses, they output random Entry Addresses (noise floor) or empty values.

  • •

    清洁块：Clean blocks:不受噪声影响的块组继续一致输出正确的条目地址。The group of blocks unaffected by noise continue to output the correct Entry Address in unison.

到目前为止，该机制就像一个强大的过滤器，可以自动将“信号（一致投票）”与“噪音（分散投票）”分开，而无需复杂的比较逻辑。然而，VaCoAl（和 PyVaCoAl）的真正本质在于一种架构，它不仅“消除”噪声，而且将其“恢复”为正确的信号。Up to this point, the mechanism functions as a powerful filter that automatically separates “signal (consistent votes)” from “noise (scattered votes)” without complex comparison logic. The true essence of VaCoAl (and PyVaCoAl), however, lies in an architecture that not only “eliminates” noise but “Recovers” it as a correct signal.

然而，当目标数据规模达到数百万时，不仅会因输入噪声而发生散射，而且不同数据之间也会不可避免地发生内存冲突，从而产生甚至来自干净块的信号也可能被埋在本底噪声中的危险。为了突破这一限制，该方法引入了精确的“多级内存碰撞避免和救援电路”。该电路不仅仅将本地块级发生的冲突或无效输出作为简单错误丢弃；它通过系统的全局代数多数投票和多阶段重新检查过程动态纠正它们。换句话说，即使个别区块陷入致命的噪音或碰撞，它们也会被吸收为系统整体结构流中可挽救的波动，并最终恢复到完美的信号。When the target data scale reaches the millions, however, not only scattering due to input noise but inevitable memory collisions between different data occur, creating the danger that signals even from clean blocks may be buried in the noise floor. To break through this limit, the method introduces a precise “multi-stage memory collision avoidance and rescue circuit.” This circuit does not merely discard collisions or invalid outputs occurring at the local block level as simple errors; it dynamically corrects them through the system’s global algebraic majority voting and multi-stage re-checking process. In other words, even if individual blocks fall into fatal noise or collisions, they are absorbed as rescuable fluctuations within the system’s overall structural flow and ultimately restored to a perfect signal.

值得注意的是，这一机制不仅仅是一个理论概念。在使用专有程序“PyVaCoAl”（第 5 节）的实证实验中，该多级救援电路在虚拟 DRAM-CAM 上完美运行。事实上，即使在总记录超过 2550 万条的超大规模多跳语义推理中，它也成功地将本应不可避免发生的数据冲突完全抑制为零。It is noteworthy that this mechanism is not merely a theoretical concept. In the empirical experiments using the proprietary programme “PyVaCoAl” (Section 5), this multi-stage rescue circuit functioned perfectly on a virtual DRAM-CAM. In fact, even in ultra-large-scale multi-hop semantic reasoning exceeding 25.5 million total records, it succeeded in completely suppressing data collisions—which should have inevitably occurred—to zero.666在使用现实世界的大规模本体数据库进行推理时，不仅输入值的物理噪声，而且潜伏在数据结构本身中的逻辑噪声（例如“A的导师是B，同时B的导师是A”等双向循环）成为使系统陷入无限循环的致命因素。本文用于演示的 PyVaCoAl 实现了预纯化算法，将搜索空间维持为健全的有向无环图 (DAG)，自动识别并消除从 WIKIDATA 提取的大约 470,000 条原始数据记录中的 369 个双向样本。这种实用的数据净化机制的集成也是重要的工程基础，能够稳定完成跨度高达 57 代、超过 2550 万条记录的深度多跳推理。In reasoning using real-world large-scale ontology databases, not only the physical noise of input values but the logical noise lurking in the data structure itself (e.g. bidirectional cycles such as “A’s mentor is B, and simultaneously B’s mentor is A”) becomes a fatal factor plunging the system into infinite loops. The PyVaCoAl used for the demonstration in this paper implements a pre-purification algorithm to maintain the search space as a sound Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG), automatically identifying and eliminating 369 bidirectional samples from the roughly 470,000 raw data records extracted from WIKIDATA. The integration of such practical data-purification mechanisms is also an important engineering foundation that enabled the stable completion of deep multi-hop reasoning spanning up to 57 generations and over 25.5 million records.这一成就证明了该架构超越了简单的噪声滤波器；它是一个极其强大的推理基础，即使在极端的大数据环境中，也可以在给定的前沿大小（FS 相对精确匹配）内确定性地导出精确匹配，并可靠地恢复和识别原始信号。然而，如第 3.5 节所述，在涉及组合爆炸的 DAG 搜索中，这种“精确匹配”是一个依赖于 FS、操作模式和内存深度的条件概念，并不意味着对整个数据库的组合空间进行穷举匹配。This achievement proves that this architecture transcends a simple noise filter; it is an extremely robust reasoning foundation that can, even in extreme big-data environments, deterministically derive Exact Matches within a given Frontier Size (FS-relative Exact Match) and reliably recover and identify the original signal. However, as detailed in Section 3.5, in searches on DAGs involving combinatorial explosions, this “exact match” is a conditional concept dependent on FS, operating mode, and memory depth, and does not imply an exhaustive match against the combinatorial space of the entire database.

3.4.2 通过欧拉数保证收敛Guarantee of Convergence via Euler’s Number

系统统计所有的输出The system tallies the outputs from allNN阻止并选择获得最多票数的入口地址。至关重要的是，这不是一个概率过程，而是代数多数投票，其胜利在数学上是有保证的。由于伽罗瓦场扩散会产生与随机过程同构的分布，因此可以使用“大数定律”严格证明这一胜利的确定性。考虑投票公式：blocks and selects the Entry Address that received the most votes. Crucially, this is not a probabilistic process but an algebraic majority vote whose victory is mathematically guaranteed. Because Galois-field diffusion generates a distribution isomorphic to a random process, the certainty of this victory can be rigorously proved using the “Law of Large Numbers.” Consider the voting formula:

WV​a​C​o​A​l=argmaxv​∑i=1Nδ​(vi,v)W_{VaCoAl}=\operatorname*{argmax}_{v}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\delta(v_{i},v) (8)

这个等式本质上执行了选举的“计票”过程：This equation essentially executes the “ballot-counting” process of an election:

  • •

    viv_{i}（投票）：每个单独的区块（SRAM）投一票说：“我相信正确的答案是(Vote): Each individual block (SRAM) casts one vote saying, “I believe the correct answer isvv.”

  • •

    δ​(vi,v)\delta(v_{i},v)（计数）：克罗内克三角洲。它检查：“这是对候选人的投票吗？(Tally): The Kronecker delta. It checks: “Is this vote for candidatevv?”

  • •

    ∑\sum(Sum)：候选人的总票数(Sum): The total number of votes for candidatevv.

  • •

    argmax\operatorname{argmax}（获胜者）：得票最多的候选人获胜。(Winner): The candidate with the maximum votes wins.

为什么这种简单多数投票就能保证消除噪音？因为，由于伽罗瓦场扩散，信号（正确答案）和噪声（错误）的行为在以下两个方面存在显着差异：Why does this simple majority vote guarantee noise elimination? Because, thanks to Galois-field diffusion, the behaviours of signal (correct answer) and noise (error) are dramatically different in the following two respects:

  • •

    A. 信号行为（点集中）：A. Signal Behaviour (Point Concentration):如果输入正确，所有区块自然会投票给正确的条目地址（例如“ID：777”）。即使存在噪音，如果 100 个区块中有 80 个不受影响，那么这 80 票将投给“ID：777”。这就形成了不可动摇的坚强“巅峰”。If the input is correct, all blocks naturally vote for the correct Entry Address (e.g. “ID: 777”). Even with noise, if 80 out of 100 blocks are unaffected, those 80 votes are cast for “ID: 777.” This forms an unshakably strong “peak.”

  • •

    B. 噪声行为（薄扩散）：B. Noise Behaviour (Thin Diffusion):这是最重要的一点。剩下的 20 个受噪音影响的街区会怎样？由于雪崩效应，即使输入发生轻微变化，每个受影响块的地址也会跳转到不可预测的随机位置。因此，来自这些区块的投票变得随机：This is the most important point. What happens to the remaining 20 blocks affected by noise? Due to the Avalanche Effect, if the input shifts even slightly, the address of each affected block jumps to an unpredictable, random location. The votes from these blocks therefore become random:

    • –

      区块 1 对“ID: 123”的错误投票Block 1’s error votes for “ID: 123”

    • –

      区块 2 对“ID: 985”的错误投票Block 2’s error votes for “ID: 985”

    • –

      区块 3 的错误投票给“ID：004”Block 3’s error votes for “ID: 004”

    • –

      ……等等。…and so on.

因此，来自错误区块的投票并不集中于任何特定数字；它们独立且不同地分散在广阔的记忆空间中。这就是“平场”的意义。The votes from erroneous blocks, therefore, do not concentrate on any specific number; they scatter independently and disparately across the vast memory space. This is the meaning of the “Flat Field.”

数学保证（泊松分布）：Mathematical Guarantee (Poisson Distribution):

现在概率论来回答这个问题：“分散的噪声投票是否有可能意外地串通形成假峰值？”该保证由基于欧拉数的泊松分布提供（Probability theory now enters to answer the question: “Is there any possibility that scattered noise votes accidentally collude to form a fake peak?” The guarantee is provided by the Poisson distribution based on Euler’s number (e≈2.718​…e\approx 2.718\ldots）。在巨大的地址空间（例如一百万种可能性）中，概率). In a vast address space (e.g. one million possibilities), the probabilityP​(k)P(k)那个thatkk随机投票无意中集中在一个特定的错误答案上：random votes accidentally concentrate on a specific wrong answer is:

P​(k)=λk​e−λk!P(k)=\frac{\lambda^{k}\,e^{-\lambda}}{k!} (9)

在这里，Here,λ\lambda（意外巧合的预期数量）极小，因为空间广阔。请注意(the expected number of accidental coincidences) is extremely small because the space is vast. Note thek!k!（阶乘）在分母中。随着所需票数的增加，分母(factorial) in the denominator. As the required number of votes increases, the denominatork!k!爆炸（explodes (1×2×3×…1\times 2\times 3\times\ldots）。因此，虽然“意外获得 2 票”的概率已经很低，但“意外获得 3 票”的概率却变得低得惊人。有关严格的数学推导，表明使用切尔诺夫界限，该误差概率以指数方式接近零，请参阅附录 A。). Consequently, while the probability of “accidentally gathering 2 votes” is already low, the probability of “accidentally gathering 3 votes” becomes astronomically low. For a rigorous mathematical derivation showing that this error probability approaches zero exponentially using the Chernoff bound, see Appendix A.

结论：Conclusion:
  • •

    机制：Mechanism:所有区块都进行投票，并做出多数决定。All blocks vote, and a majority decision is taken.

  • •

    现象：Phenomenon:正确的选票形成“高山”，错误的选票成为“平地”。Correct votes form a “tall mountain,” while erroneous votes become a “flat field.”

  • •

    证明：Proof:根据欧拉数定律，“平地意外形成一座山”的概率几乎为零。The probability of “a mountain forming accidentally from a flat field” is guaranteed to be virtually zero by the law of Euler’s number.

因此，在附录A的前提条件（不发生内存冲突的理想环境）下，该系统的误报概率在物理上可以认为为零。然而，在现实大数据环境中的多跳搜索中，3.5 节中讨论的 FS 相对性和操作模式的选择会影响搜索结果，因此这种“绝对正确”仅限于上述理想条件。Therefore, under the prerequisite conditions of Appendix A (an ideal environment in which memory collisions do not occur), the false-positive probability of this system can be considered physically zero. However, in multi-hop searches within real-world big-data environments, the FS relativity and the choice of operating mode discussed in Section 3.5 influence the search results, so this “infallibility” is limited to the ideal conditions stated above.

4 计算复杂性分析和代数解析 绑定问题Computational Complexity Analysis and Algebraic Resolution of the Binding Problem

本节首先评估 VaCoAl 的计算复杂度和 相对于深度学习和现有硬件的能源效率，那么 检查下一代人工智能的核心定性能力： 绑定问题的数字解决方案。This section first evaluates VaCoAl’s computational complexity and energy efficiency relative to deep learning and existing hardware, then examines the qualitative capability central to next-generation AI: a digital solution to the Binding Problem.

4.1 计算复杂性和能源效率Computational Complexity and Energy Efficiency

4.1.1 学习成本大幅降低Order-of-Magnitude Reduction in Learning Cost

现代人工智能的主要瓶颈是训练成本。一个常规的 用于手写数字识别（MNIST 级）的 CNN 必须迭代 向前和向后传递数千次以优化数百万次 重量参数，可能需要 peta 规模（The dominant bottleneck in modern AI is training cost. A conventional CNN for handwritten-digit recognition (MNIST-class) must iterate forward and backward passes thousands of times to optimize millions of weight parameters, potentially requiring peta-scale (101510^{15}） 乘法累加 (MAC) 运算) multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations[29, 37]; 最近拥有数千亿参数的法学硕士推高了这一成本 更进一步。; recent LLMs with hundreds of billions of parameters push this cost further still.

相比之下，VaCoAl 的学习在VaCoAl’s learning, by contrast, completes in a单程single pass:

    扩散（地址生成）：Diffusion (address generation):伽罗瓦菲尔德 操作将输入转换为内存地址。Galois-field operations convert the input into a memory address.

  2. 2.

    写：Write:生成的地址被写入 相应的SRAM/DRAM条目。The generated address is written to the corresponding SRAM/DRAM entry.

无迭代、无偏微分、无浮点矩阵 涉及代数。大冢等人。No iteration, no partial differentiation, and no floating-point matrix algebra are involved. This efficiency opens the door to genuine real-time, on-device learning on battery-powered edge hardware.

4.1.2 通过 1/N 激活规则实现超低功耗Ultra-Low Power via the 1/N Activation Rule

传统 TCAM 驱动器Conventional TCAM drives全部all存储单元同时 平行比较，产生极端热度，限制实用 容量达到千比特范围（“电源墙”）。 VaCoAl 打破了这一点 通过仅激活屏障memory cells simultaneously for parallel comparison, producing extreme heat and confining practical capacity to the kilobit range (the “power wall”). VaCoAl breaks this barrier by activating only theNN对应的块 分段输入向量：blocks that correspond to the segmented input vector:

    TCAM：TCAM:激活 100% 的细胞。activates 100 % of cells.
