    瓦钴铝：VaCoAl:激活activates1/N1/N总容量 （例如，0.1 %of total capacity (e.g., 0.1 % forN=1,000N=1{,}000).

在 SRAM/DRAM-CAM 硬件/软件实现中，这In an SRAM/DRAM-CAM hardware/software realization, this1/N1/N规则预计 将功率降低一个因子rule is expected to reduce power by a factor ofNN相对于 TCAM。经验主义 然而，验证仅限于 PyVaCoAl（软件）；硬件 测量仍然是未来的工作。标准 SRAM 的低待机功耗和 与工艺扩展的兼容性进一步增强了 VaCoAl 的性能 能源效率前景。relative to TCAM. Empirical verification, however, is limited to PyVaCoAl (software); hardware measurements remain future work. Standard SRAM’s low standby power and compatibility with process scaling further reinforce VaCoAl’s energy-efficiency prospects.

4.1.3 解决 CAM 悖论Resolving the CAM Paradox

超高维向量搜索长期以来面临着我们所说的困境 的Ultra-high-dimensional vector search has long faced a dilemma we term theCAM悖论CAM Paradox：TCAM 提供速度但不提供可扩展能力； DRAM 上的软件散列可提供可扩展容量，但不能提供物理容量 并行性。: TCAM delivers speed but not scalable power; software hashing on DRAM delivers scalable capacity but not physical parallelism.

VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 解决了这个悖论。而不是驱动所有细胞 同时（TCAM）或执行顺序哈希查找（软件）， 它执行基于 LFSR 的低成本扩散和并行多数投票 直接在标准 SRAM/DRAM 上实现内存计算 绕过CPU-内存通信瓶颈。结果熔断 TCAM 级速度以及商用 SRAM/DRAM 级容量和功率 效率。VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl dissolves this paradox. Rather than driving all cells simultaneously (TCAM) or executing sequential hash lookups (software), it performs low-cost LFSR-based diffusion and parallel majority voting directly on standard SRAM/DRAM—achieving in-memory computing that bypasses the CPU–memory communication bottleneck. The result fuses TCAM-class speed with commodity-SRAM/DRAM-class capacity and power efficiency.

软件-硬件性能关系。Software–hardware performance relationship.

本文所有实验均使用PyVaCoAl（基于DRAM的软件）； SRAM-CAM 硬件测量All experiments in this paper use PyVaCoAl (DRAM-based software); SRAM-CAM hardware measurements[27]留待今后的工作。 一个remain future work. A下界lower-bound两者之间的关系成立 实施：relationship holds between the two implementations:

4.2 结合问题的代数解析及其推广 大规模推理Algebraic Resolution of the Binding Problem and Extension to Large-Scale Reasoning

大脑如何立即组合出不同的属性——“红色”和“红色” “苹果”——统一认知“红苹果”？这个How does the brain instantly compose distinct attributes—“red” and “apple”—into the unified percept “red apple”? This装订 问题Binding Problem是神经科学领域的一个核心实施挑战 认知科学is a central implementation challenge in neuroscience and cognitive science[6, 42]。在常规的 神经网络，同时编码“红车”和“蓝苹果” 经常会触发一个. In conventional neural networks, simultaneously encoding “red car” and “blue apple” often triggers a叠加灾难superposition catastrophe: 特征纠缠 不可逆地产生虚假组合，例如“红苹果”或 “蓝色的车。”: features entangle irreversibly, producing spurious combinations such as “red apple” or “blue car.”

4.2.1 HDC 代数运算和 VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 的优化HDC Algebraic Operations and VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl’s Optimization

超维计算 (HDC) — 尤其是 Plate 的全息简化表示 (HRR)Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC)—notably Plate’s Holographic Reduced Representation (HRR)[32]以及 Eliasmith 等人的语义指针架构 (SPA)and Eliasmith et al.’s Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA)[11]，Kleyko 等人全面调查了 HDC/VSA 模型中更广泛的绑定操作系列。, with the broader family of binding operations across HDC/VSA models comprehensively surveyed by Kleyko et al.[21]——通过显式代数运算解决这一挑战：—addresses this challenge through explicit algebraic operations:

𝑅𝑒𝑝𝑟𝑒𝑠𝑒𝑛𝑡𝑎𝑡𝑖𝑜𝑛=(𝐶𝑜𝑙𝑜𝑟⊗𝑅𝑒𝑑)+(𝑆ℎ𝑎𝑝𝑒⊗𝐴𝑝𝑝𝑙𝑒),\mathit{Representation}=(\mathit{Color}\otimes\mathit{Red})+(\mathit{Shape}\otimes\mathit{Apple}), (10)

哪里where⊗\otimes表示denotes装订Binding（将两个向量映射到 正交积）和(mapping two vectors to an orthogonal product) and++表示denotes捆绑Bundling（叠加 同一空间中的多个概念）。高维正交性 防止相互干扰；(superposing multiple concepts in the same space). High-dimensional orthogonality prevents mutual interference;解绑Unbinding恢复个体 通过逆运算得到元素。recovers individual elements via the inverse operation.

VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 的贡献是以最小的成本实现这个 HDC 框架： 绑定简化为异或并在伽罗瓦域上进行移位——纯位 操作于VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl’s contribution is to realize this HDC framework at minimal cost: Binding reduces to XOR and shift over Galois fields—pure bit operations atO​(N)O(N)——而不是浮点循环 卷积（—rather than the floating-point circular convolution (O​(N​log⁡N)O(N\log N)) SPA 要求。多阶段 防碰撞电路进一步确保，即使数百万 概念被捆绑到一个共享空间中，各个元素可以 准确无束缚。在救援模式下（或在高内存深度），这 精度达到FS相对精确匹配。结果是 具有事后可验证性的组合概括——透明 深度学习的黑盒嵌入无法实现推理。 （与 SPA 的详细复杂性比较见附录 B.5。）) required by SPA. The multi-stage collision-avoidance circuit further ensures that, even when millions of concepts are Bundled into a shared space, individual elements can be accurately Unbound. In Rescue mode (or at high memory depth), this precision reaches FS-relative exact match. The result is compositional generalization with post-hoc verifiability—transparent inference unattainable by the black-box embeddings of deep learning. (A detailed complexity comparison with SPA appears in Appendix B.5.)

4.3 透明度作为可解释的人工智能 (XAI)Transparency as Explainable AI (XAI)

VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 直接解决了深度学习的黑盒问题，在 其决策原理分散在数百万个不透明权重中。VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl directly addresses the black-box problem of deep learning, in which decision rationale is diffused across millions of opaque weights.

4.3.1 置信度分数Confidence Score

因为 VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 的推论依赖于多数投票，所以投票结果 产生即时的、定量的置信度测量：Because VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl’s inference rests on majority voting, the vote tally yields an immediate, quantitative confidence measure:

𝐶𝑜𝑛𝑓𝑖𝑑𝑒𝑛𝑐𝑒=votes for the winnerN,\mathit{Confidence}=\frac{\text{votes for the winner}}{N}, (11)

哪里whereNN是块的总数。 99％同意的决定 显然比 51% 同意的更值得信赖。不像 深度学习模型，经常表现出毫无根据的 过度自信，VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 透明地报告了不确定性程度 每次检索——对于安全敏感领域（例如 如医疗诊断和自动驾驶。is the total number of blocks. A 99 %-agreement decision is manifestly more trustworthy than a 51 %-agreement one. Unlike deep-learning models, which frequently exhibit unwarranted overconfidence, VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl transparently reports the degree of ambiguity in each retrieval—a property critical for safety-sensitive domains such as medical diagnosis and autonomous driving.

4.3.2 通过代数可逆性实现数学可审计性Mathematical Auditability via Algebraic Reversibility

在深度神经网络中，信息整合是有效的 不可逆：非线性激活函数融合成分信号， 排除事后分解。这种不透明性阻碍了In deep neural networks, information integration is effectively irreversible: nonlinear activation functions fuse constituent signals, precluding post-hoc decomposition. This opacity obstructs the构图表征compositional representation—灵活的装订和 解除概念的束缚——萨加德—the flexible binding and unbinding of concepts—that Thagard[42]标识为 一个基本的认知架构要求。identifies as an essential cognitive-architecture requirement.

相比之下，VaCoAl?PyVaCoAl 的 Binding 是通过 XOR 和 伽罗瓦域上的平移。给定VaCoAl?PyVaCoAl’s Binding, by contrast, is an algebraic structuring via XOR and shift over Galois fields. GivenC=A⊗Shift⁡(B)C=A\otimes\operatorname{Shift}(B)，逆运算, the inverse operationC⊗Shift⁡(B)C\otimes\operatorname{Shift}(B)（减法等于异或(subtraction equals XOR inGF​(2)\mathrm{GF}(2)）完全恢复) exactly recoversAA在没有噪音的情况下。 鉴于 SPA 的循环卷积需要有损压缩， VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 实现in the absence of noise. Whereas the SPA’s circular convolution entails lossy compression, VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl achieves无损可逆性lossless reversibility at O​(N)O(N)成本。这个 能够从复合概念中自由提取成分， 同时满足 Thagard 的标准cost. This enables free extraction of constituents from composite concepts, simultaneously satisfying Thagard’s criteria of系统性systematicity和and生产力productivity并提供事后可验证的 XAI： 复合表示是代数结构的并且可审计 所有的时间。and delivering post-hoc verifiable XAI: composite representations are algebraically structured and auditable at all times.

4.3.3 AD 横向抑制功能诱导和 赢者通吃的动态AD Functional Induction of Lateral Inhibition and Winner-Take-All Dynamics

VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 功能性诱导生物电路实现的机制 通过专用的抑制性中间神经元，无物理抑制 接线或参数调整。生成的 Don’t Care 状态 雪崩效应充当过滤器：来自不匹配块的输出 扩散到无特征的背景噪声中，有效抑制 错误的候选者（侧抑制）并只让正确的候选者 候选出现——理想的稀疏分布式表示。 （一 正式证明见附录 B.4。）VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl functionally induces mechanisms that biological circuits realize through dedicated inhibitory interneurons, without physical inhibitory wiring or parameter tuning. The Don’t Care state generated by the avalanche effect acts as a filter: outputs from mismatched blocks diffuse into featureless background noise, effectively suppressing erroneous candidates (lateral inhibition) and letting only the correct candidate emerge—an ideal sparse distributed representation. (A formal proof appears in Appendix B.4.)

这种抑制超出了空间赢家通吃的范围。在多跳中 推理，“不关心”惩罚沿着时间轴累积， 削弱废弃的深层路径——功能上类似于 STDP 的角色 （尖峰时间相关的可塑性）This inhibition extends beyond spatial winner-take-all. In multi-hop reasoning, the Don’t Care penalty accumulates along the time axis, weakening disused deep paths—a role functionally analogous to STDP (Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity)[16]。相互作用 空间和时间选择的影响在真实的环境中得到定量证明 第 5 节中的数据。. The interplay of spatial and temporal selection is quantitatively demonstrated on real data in Section 5.

综上所述，通过翻译Kanerva的SDM理论和小脑/ Kandel 等人描述的海马计算原理 等人。In summary, by translating Kanerva’s SDM theory and the cerebellar / hippocampal computational principles described by Kandel et al.[16]—扩展编码和侧向抑制—进入 代数确定性数字逻辑（XOR 和 Don’t Care）、VaCoA/PyVaCoAll 弥合了神经科学启发之间长期缺失的联系 理论和实际低功耗实现。—expansion coding and lateral inhibition—into algebro-deterministic digital logic (XOR and Don’t Care), VaCoA/PyVaCoAll bridges the long-standing missing link between neuroscience-inspired theory and practical low-power implementation.

5 使用 PyVaCoAl 进行百万维 HDC 分析演示Demonstration of Million-Dimensional HDC Analysis Utilizing PyVaCoAl

前面的四节介绍了“VaCoAl架构”——一种基于SRAM/DRAM的代数确定性逻辑，能够实现与生物大脑的计算原理一致的AD功能归纳——作为解决困扰现代人工智能的“叠加灾难”和功耗上限的答案。我们在那里证明了，给定足够数量的块（The preceding four sections have laid out the “VaCoAl Architecture”—an algebro-deterministic logic on SRAM/DRAM that enables AD functional induction consonant with the computational principles of the biological brain—as an answer to the “Superposition Catastrophe” and the power-consumption ceiling that beset modern AI. We proved there that, given a sufficient number of blocks (BB）、存储深度（), memory depth (2m2^{m}) 和维数 (), and dimensionality (NN）的高维二元正交空间，“最佳匹配”收敛到“精确匹配”（附录 A）。然而，无论模型在纸面上多么优雅，除非其属性在大规模、充满噪声的现实世界数据上得到证实，否则其作为真正的下一代多跳逻辑推理基础设施的工程资格仍然是临时的。) of the high-dimensional binary orthogonal space, “Best Match” converges to “Exact Match” (Appendix A). Yet however elegant a model may be on paper, unless its properties are vindicated on large-scale, noise-laden real-world data, its engineering credentials as a genuine next-generation multi-hop logical-reasoning infrastructure remain provisional.

5.1 VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl：从理论基础到极端条件下展现的优越性VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl: From Theoretical Foundation to Demonstrated Superiority under Extreme Conditions

因此，本节报告了使用“PyVaCoAl”进行的实际实证实验，“PyVaCoAl”是 VaCoAl 的 Python 实现，部署到要求严格的大数据环境中。This section therefore reports practical empirical experiments conducted with “PyVaCoAl,” a Python implementation of VaCoAl, deployed into a demanding big-data environment.777VaCoAl 的 FPGA 实现在商业上可作为“SRAM-CAM”（An FPGA implementation of VaCoAl is commercially available as “SRAM-CAM” (https://shuharisystem.com/?page_id=1336），但不幸的是在撰写本文时不可用。正如本文中所进行的，在百万维空间中利用多个谓词的 HDC 分析目前似乎仅适用于 PyVaCoAl。), but was unfortunately unavailable at the time of writing. HDC analysis exploiting multiple predicates in a million-dimensional space, as carried out in this paper, appears at present to be feasible only with PyVaCoAl.

具体来说，实验分两个阶段进行。 (A) 从全球最大的开放知识图谱 WIKIDATA（https://query.wikidata.org/）中，我们提取了从公元前 10 世纪至今约 47 万条“以数学家为中心的师生关系”记录（包括 372,853 名学生）。然后，我们以 64 名历史菲尔兹奖获得者（其中一位缺乏有记录的导师）为起点，执行谱系搜索，将导师链追溯到过去。(B) 然后，我们对所得的语料库进行了大约 2550 万条谱系路径记录（大约 32 GB 文本文件）到超维计算（HDC）操作 万维空间。Specifically, the experiment proceeded in two stages. (A) From WIKIDATA (https://query.wikidata.org/), the world’s largest open knowledge graph, we extracted approximately 470,000 records of ”mentor–student relationships centred on mathematicians” (including 372,853 students) spanning from the 10th century BCE to the present. We then took the 64 historical Fields Medalists (one of whom lacks a recorded mentor) as starting points and executed a genealogical search that traces the chain of mentors backwards into the past.(B) We then subjected the resulting corpus of approximately 25.5 million genealogical path records (approximately 32 GB as a text file) to Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) operations in a million-dimensional space.888更准确地说，我们进行了数百次 HDC 操作包括： 模糊查询（百分位数 TRAFFIC 版本），解除范式分析（前/中/后）， 世代窗口分析，“巨人的肩膀”——谱系学 微积分的源头，高速公路假设验证，CSV 输出（修正分数版本 / Rescue 5_Correction 2_Improved 5 结果），学术语言转变分析，机构中心 分析、微积分创始人强化分析、综合 范式转变裁决，托莱多翻译学院的影响 分析、检测代数和天文学中的高压信号爆炸， 用于挖掘隐藏奇点的通用“巨谱” 在任何领域，DICT vs PyVaCoAl Top 20，FIELD-质心比较， 和虚假的高速公路检测。More precisely, we carried out several hundred HDC operations encompassing: Fuzzy querying (percentile TRAFFIC version), Unbinding paradigm analysis (Pre/Interim/Post), generation-window analysis, “Shoulders of Giants”—genealogical headwaters of calculus, Super Highway hypothesis verification, CSV output (corrected-score version / Rescue 5_Correction 2_Improved 5 results), academic-language shift analysis, institutional-hub analysis, calculus-founder reinforcement analysis, comprehensive paradigm-shift adjudication, Toledo School of Translators impact analysis, detection of HV-signal explosions in algebra and astronomy, a general-purpose “Giant Score” for unearthing hidden singularities in any field, DICT vs PyVaCoAl Top 20, FIELD-centroid comparison, and spurious super-highway detection.这些行动审查了家谱网络案件 跨越千年的数学史。 PyVaCoAl 在两个阶段 (A) 和 (B) 中均得到应用，目的是证明 VaCoAl 架构将高维二元正交空间内的致命内存冲突限制在可忽略不计的水平，忠实地追溯了十多个世纪的导师-学生谱系，此外，还构成了通过 HDC 特有的代数运算阐明历史动态的强大工具。These operations scrutinised genealogical-network cases in mathematical history spanning more than a millennium. PyVaCoAl was employed in both stages (A) and (B), with the aim of demonstrating that the VaCoAl architecture confines fatal memory collisions within the high-dimensional binary orthogonal space to a negligible level, faithfully traces mentor–student genealogies across more than ten centuries, and furthermore constitutes a powerful instrument for elucidating historical dynamism through the algebraic operations peculiar to HDC.

为了对 PyVaCoAl 的功能进行基准测试，我们还为阶段 (A) 开发了一个名为“Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1)”的参考实现，它依赖于 Python LIST 和 DICT 哈希函数；对于阶段 (B)，称为“Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2)”的对应项将 Python LIST 与To benchmark PyVaCoAl’s capabilities, we also developed, for stage (A), a reference implementation dubbed “Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1)” that relies on Python LISTs and the DICT hash function; and for stage (B), a counterpart dubbed “Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2)” that couples Python LISTs with火炬HDtorchhd,999https://github.com/hyperdimensional-computing/torchhd众所周知的支持 GPU 的 PyTorch 模块。利用a well-known GPU-capable PyTorch module. Taking advantage of火炬HDtorchhd为了同时支持 GPU 和 CPU，我们在 NVIDIA GPU 配置和仅 CPU 配置中运行 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2)。 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) 和 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2) 都被设计为尽可能以与 PyVaCoAl 脚本相同的格式发出输出文件。尽管 PyVaCoAl 的脚本在当前实现中是分开的，但它可以同时执行阶段 (A) 和 (B)。’s support for both GPUs and CPUs, we ran Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2) in an NVIDIA GPU configuration and in a CPU-only configuration. Both Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) and Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2) were engineered to emit output files in the same format as the PyVaCoAl script wherever possible. Although PyVaCoAl’s script is separated in the present implementation, it can execute stages (A) and (B) simultaneously.

虽然完整的结果将在后续小节中详细介绍，但主要发现如下。对于阶段 (B)，伪 PyVaCoAl(2) 的 CPU 版本需要的时间是 PyVaCoAl 的两倍（60 分钟）。相比之下，整个 PyVaCoAl 实验在 2014 年制造的 Intel Xeon E5-1650,v3 (3.50 GHz) CPU 上只需 20-30 分钟即可完成。此外，GPU 实现While the full results are detailed in the subsequent subsections, the key findings are as follows. For stage (B), the CPU version of Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(2) required twice as much time as PyVaCoAl (60 minutes). In contrast, the entire PyVaCoAl experiment was completed in just 20–30 minutes on an Intel Xeon E5-1650,v3 (3.50 GHz) CPU manufactured in 2014. Furthermore, the GPU implementation of火炬HDtorchhd需要超过 100GB 的 VRAM，并且对于这种规模的数据会因内存不足 (OOM) 错误而终止。 PyVaCoAl 在十年前的 CPU 上完成相同的任务这一事实证明，与以尖端 GPU 硬件为前提的深度学习框架相比，其计算特性存在根本不同。这提供了切实的证据，表明该架构实际上可以部署在访问最新硬件受到限制的边缘设备和消费类 PC 上，从而证实了我们对超高维 PyVaCoAl 的“超高速、低功耗、低成本”的主张。requires over 100GB of VRAM and terminates with an Out of Memory (OOM) error on data of this scale. The fact that PyVaCoAl completes the same task on a decade-old CPU testifies to its fundamentally different computational character compared to deep-learning frameworks that presuppose cutting-edge GPU hardware. This provides tangible evidence that the architecture can be practically deployed on edge devices and consumer PCs where access to the latest hardware is limited—thereby substantiating our claim of “ultra-high-speed, low-power, low-cost” for ultra-high-dimensional PyVaCoAl.

尽管从 WIKIDATA 获得了跨越两千年的师生关系数据，但无论经过多少参数调整，都无法成功追踪 11 世纪以后的谱系。我们将这个障碍指定为Although mentor–student relationship data spanning over two millennia was obtained from WIKIDATA, no amount of parameter tuning succeeded in tracing the genealogy beyond the 11th century. We designate this barrier the《修道院的围墙》“Monastery Wall”并通过研究其存在的原因，评估 WIKIDATA 作为数据源的力量和局限性。我们将证明，这座 11 世纪的墙反映的不是 PyVaCoAl 或 Wikidata 的技术缺陷，而是反映了and, by examining the reasons for its existence, assess both the power and the limitations of WIKIDATA as a data source. We shall show that this 11th-century wall reflects not a technical shortcoming of PyVaCoAl or Wikidata, but the历史事实是，这个时代标志着知识传播媒介的根本转变historical fact that the era marked a fundamental shift in the very medium through which knowledge was propagated。事实上，HDC 的分析提供了令人着迷的一瞥：在修道院围墙之外，伊斯兰-阿拉伯黄金时代的伟大博学者所积累的巨大知识库确实存在。. Indeed, the HDC analysis affords tantalising glimpses that, beyond the Monastery Wall, a vast reservoir of learning accumulated by the great polymaths of the Islamic-Arabic Golden Age assuredly existed.

PyVaCoAl 本身的一个重要区别： 记忆深度引起的相变（A Critical Distinction within PyVaCoAl Itself: The Phase Transition Induced by Memory Depth (M​B=2mMB=2^{m}）和 救援率（) and Rescue Rate (R​RRR)

这种对比提出了其余部分的核心问题 第 5 节：两种配置都可追溯家谱 57 代；按照 Python 哈希的标准，两者都是 功能，“精确”。然而，正如我们将要看到的，家谱 他们创建的网络This contrast poses the central question of the remainder of Section 5. The answer, developed across Sections 5.6.1 through 5.6.4, is emphaticallyno: 分歧 揭示了一种新兴的语义选择机制——功能上 与生物神经回路中的 STDP 同源——即 任何基于哈希的搜索在结构上都无法访问。之前 可以审查该机制，但是，我们必须处置一个 先前的问题：“精确匹配”对于 DAG 搜索受到组合爆炸的困扰？: the divergence exposes an emergent semantic-selection mechanism—functionally homologous to STDP in biological neural circuits—that is structurally inaccessible to any hash-based search. Before that mechanism can be examined, however, we must dispose of a prior question: what does “Exact Match” even mean for a DAG search beset by combinatorial explosion?

“精确匹配”的相对性——超越哈希函数的神话The Relativity of “Exact Match”—Beyond the Mythology of Hash Functions

本文中反复出现的短语“FS 相对精确匹配”乍一看可能会被解读为“不完美匹配”。相反，这是对一个更基本问题的坦诚回答：The phrase “FS-relative Exact Match” that recurs throughout this paper might at first blush be read as “an imperfect match.” It is, rather, a candid answer to a more fundamental question:对于受组合爆炸困扰的大规模 DAG 搜索，是否存在绝对精确匹配？does an absolute Exact Match even exist for large-scale DAG searches beset by combinatorial explosion?

如前所述，我们实验中的多位奖牌获得者达到了As noted, multiple medalists in our experiments reachedFS=600,000\mathrm{FS}=600{,}000。即使使用 Python DICT 的哈希函数，任何分支点的谱系选择也只能在预设的 FS 上限内进行。因此，哈希函数保证的“精确匹配”是精确匹配. Even with Python DICT’s hash function, genealogical selection at any branch point operates only within the pre-set FS ceiling. The “Exact Match” a hash function guarantees is therefore an exact matchFS内within the FS，除此之外什么也没有。在, and nothing beyond. AtFS=25,000\mathrm{FS}=25{,}000，Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) 耗尽了系统内存，即使有 128 GB 主内存也变得无法执行（表, Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) exhausted system memory and became unexecutable even with 128 GB of main memory (Table5）。这清楚地表明 DICT 哈希函数的精确匹配保证是). This starkly demonstrates that the DICT hash function’s Exact Match guarantee is a与可扩展性墙相冲突的条件保证conditional guarantee that collides with a scalability wall在大规模 DAG 搜索中。in large-scale DAG searches.

5.2 大规模本体数据库的谱系获取及其准确性验证Genealogy Acquisition from a Large-Scale Ontology Database and Verification of Its Accuracy

在实验的第一阶段（A）中，我们提取了从公元前10世纪至今的约47万条“以数学家为中心的师生关系”记录。以所有 64 位历史菲尔兹奖获得者为起点，我们追溯了他们的导师链。预纯化算法检测并消除了 369 个双向链接（循环），从而实现了最大深度达到 57 代的非循环深度搜索。In the first stage (A) of the experiment, we extracted approximately 470,000 records of “mentor–student relationships centred on mathematicians” from the 10th century BCE to the present. Taking all 64 historical Fields Medalists as starting points, we traced their chains of mentors backward in time. A pre-purification algorithm detected and eliminated 369 bidirectional links (cycles), enabling an acyclic deep search that reached a maximum depth of 57 generations.

PyVaCoAl 与救援电路接合（PyVaCoAl with the rescue circuit engaged (R​R=1RR=1; 128_10配置） 取得了; 128_10 configuration) achieved a完美匹配perfect match—100% — 在全部 2550 万条记录中使用 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1)。这构成了直接、独立的验证，证明 PyVaCoAl 的代数确定性处理可以生成逻辑上与哈希函数等效的结果，尽管遍历的是完全不同的数学路径（伽罗瓦场扩散）。完美的匹配证明了，在—100%—with Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) across the full 25.5 million records. This constitutes direct, independent verification that PyVaCoAl’s algebro-deterministic processing can generate results logically equivalent to a hash function, despite traversing an entirely different mathematical path—Galois-field diffusion. The perfect match proves that, underFS=20,000\mathrm{FS}=20{,}000，配备救援的 PyVaCoAl 确定性地再现了 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) 的 Python DICT 哈希搜索。, the rescue-equipped PyVaCoAl deterministically reproduces the Python DICT hash search of Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1).

然而，这种等价性就像一枚硬币的正面，而硬币的反面则消除了 CR2 的判别能力：救援电路将每个候选者的 CR2 均质化为 1.0，从而抑制了 VaCoAl 的原生语义选择能力（第 3.5 节）。当救援电路被禁用时（This equivalence, however, is the obverse of a coin whose reverse is the annihilation of CR2’s discriminating power: the rescue circuit homogenises CR2 to 1.0 for every candidate, suppressing VaCoAl’s native semantic-selection capability (Section 3.5). When the rescue circuit is disabled (R​R=0RR=0; 128_27 配置：128 个块，内存深度; 128_27 configuration: 128 blocks, memory depth2272^{27}），PyVaCoAl 生成大约 2543 万条记录、1,043 个唯一节点和 1,408 个图边——结果与伪 PyVaCoAl(1) 和配备救援的 PyVaCoAl 不同。这种分歧提供了 VaCoAl 的 Don't Care 机制（第 5.6 节）所造成的紧急语义选择的直接证据。因此，本节中报告的 HDC 分析基于 PyVaCoAl 的系谱输出（), PyVaCoAl generates approximately 25.43 million records, 1,043 unique nodes, and 1,408 graph edges—results that diverge from both Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) and rescue-equipped PyVaCoAl. This divergence furnishes direct evidence of the emergent semantic selection wrought by VaCoAl’s Don’t Care mechanism (Section 5.6). Accordingly, the HDC analyses reported in this section are based on the genealogical output of PyVaCoAl (R​R=0RR=0），仅靠它就可以可靠地执行 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) 无法访问的路径相关剔除功能。), which alone can reliably exercise the path-dependent culling function inaccessible to Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1).

5.3 百万维空间中的 HDC 执行及其优越性HDC Execution in a Million-Dimensional Space and the Superiority of theO​(1)O(1)建筑Architecture

然而，这项研究的真正目的远远超出了单谓词 DAG 搜索或仅基于导师与学生联系的谱系追踪。当从海量家谱流量中识别出的约 1,043 个中心个体（跨越多达 57 代、总计约 2550 万条记录、超过 32GB）通过新扩展的“多谓词本体数据库”（B 阶段）投射到超维计算空间时，该架构展现了其全部威力。The true purpose of this study, however, extends well beyond single-predicate DAG search or genealogical tracing based on mentor–student links alone. The architecture reveals its full power when the approximately 1,043 hub individuals identified from the massive genealogy traffic—spanning up to 57 generations, totalling some 25.5 million records, and exceeding 32 GB—are projected into a Hyperdimensional Computing space via a newly expanded “multi-predicate ontology database” (Phase B).

具体来说，对于每个识别的节点（个人），我们使用 WIKIDATA 的 SPARQL 查询接口动态附加最多 12 个谓词维度——“使用的语言”(LANGUAGE)、“附属机构”(EMPLOYER)、“附属社团”(MEMBER_OF)、“出生地”(BIRTH_PLACE)，以及“活跃时代”(ERA) 和“学术领域”（FIELD）——并将这些纳入 HDC 管道中。然后，使用伽罗瓦域上的可逆多项式运算，将这些谓词在百万维超高维正交超向量 (HV) 空间中进行代数绑定、未绑定和捆绑。随后在 1,043 个 HV 编码节点、它们的谓词相关 HV 和人际 HV 之间执行了数百次 XOR 操作，通过汉明距离相似性来表征节点特征和时代特征。下面介绍的“微积分学位”、“巨分（综合、领域）”、“基于网络的历史结构变迁检测”、“以莱布尼茨时代为中心的托马斯·库恩式范式转变的检测”，都是本次HDC分析的产物。Concretely, for each identified node (individual), we used WIKIDATA’s SPARQL query interface to dynamically attach up to 12 predicate dimensions—“Language Used” (LANGUAGE), “Affiliated Institution” (EMPLOYER), “Affiliated Society” (MEMBER_OF), “Place of Birth” (BIRTH_PLACE), in addition to “Active Era” (ERA) and “Academic Field” (FIELD)—and folded these into the HDC pipeline. These predicates were then algebraically Bound, Unbound, and Bundled within a million-dimensional ultra-high-dimensional orthogonal Hyper-Vector (HV) space using reversible polynomial operations over Galois fields. Hundreds of XOR operations were subsequently executed between the 1,043 HV-encoded nodes, their predicate-related HVs, and inter-personal HVs, characterising node traits and era traits via Hamming-distance similarities. The “Calculus Degree,” the “Giant Score (comprehensive and field-specific),” the “network-based detection of historical structural change,” and the “detection of a Thomas Kuhn-style paradigm shift centred on the era of Leibniz” presented below are all products of this HDC analysis.

5.3.1 PyVaCoAl 的计算速度优势PyVaCoAl’s Computational Speed Superiority

该架构相对于现有的基于 GPU 的 HDC 实现在计算速度和内存效率方面表现出压倒性的优势，值得特别强调。诸如基于 PyTorch 的库The overwhelming superiority in computational speed and memory efficiency that this architecture demonstrates over existing GPU-based HDC implementations deserves particular emphasis. Libraries such as the PyTorch-based火炬HDtorchhd提供了在深度学习框架上实现 HDC 的标准方法，但在本次实验的繁重工作量（数十万维度、数千万条目）下，两者之间的性能差距由三个结构性因素决定。provide a standard means of realising HDC on deep-learning frameworks, but under the severe workloads of this experiment—hundreds of thousands of dimensions, tens of millions of entries—the performance gap between the two is governed by three structural factors.

第一First，计算复杂度和算术范式的差异。 GPU 实现以浮点运算为前提，并在查询向量和内存中的每个项目之间执行详尽的矩阵运算（例如点积），从而产生, the difference in computational complexity and arithmetic paradigm. GPU implementations presuppose floating-point arithmetic and execute exhaustive matrix operations (e.g. dot products) between the query vector and every item in memory, yieldingO​(N×D)O(N\times D)通过大规模张量重建很容易耗尽内存的复杂性。相比之下，PyVaCoAl 仅通过伽罗瓦场扩散 (LFSR) 进行整数/按位算术（XOR 等）运算。它实现了类似散列函数的行为——从多项式余数直接跳转到目标内存地址——使得搜索成本具有确定性complexity that readily exhausts memory through massive tensor reconstruction. PyVaCoAl, by contrast, operates exclusively in integer/bitwise arithmetic (XOR, etc.) via Galois-field diffusion (LFSR). It realises hash-function-like behaviour—jumping directly to a target memory address from a polynomial remainder—making the search cost a deterministicO​(1)O(1)查找完全独立于条目数量look-up entirely independent of the number of entriesNN.

第二Second，避免内存架构瓶颈。当 GPU 耗尽其专用 VRAM（通常为 12-16 GB）时，它会通过窄带宽 PCIe 总线（共享内存模式）回退到主内存 (DRAM)，从而遭受灾难性的性能崩溃。 PyVaCoAl将构建在大容量主存中的数据库作为内存数据库驱动，与宽带宽的CPU连接，不会产生这样的瓶颈。, avoidance of memory-architecture bottlenecks. When a GPU exhausts its dedicated VRAM (typically 12–16 GB), it falls back to main memory (DRAM) over the narrow-bandwidth PCIe bus (shared-memory mode), suffering a catastrophic performance collapse. PyVaCoAl drives the database constructed in high-capacity main memory as an in-memory database, connected to the CPU with wide bandwidth, and incurs no such bottleneck.

第三ThirdHDC分析程序中新实现的“多级存储器救援电路”实现了极高的存储器效率。动态存储桶方案仅将高密度的冲突数据打包到平面阵列中，消除死区并将 CPU 缓存命中率推至实际极限，同时充分利用 DRAM 容量。, the extremely high memory efficiency achieved by the “multi-stage memory rescue circuit” newly implemented in the HDC analysis programme. A dynamic-bucket scheme packs only collided data at high density into a flat array, eliminating dead space and pushing the CPU cache-hit rate to its practical limit while fully exploiting DRAM capacity.

5.3.2 内存深度和缓存局部性之间的权衡The Trade-off between Memory Depth and Cache Locality

为了精确分离 VaCoAl 架构的特征，我们比较了相同数据集上四种配置的计算时间（表To isolate the characteristics of the VaCoAl architecture with precision, we compared computation times across four configurations on the identical dataset (Table4).

表中的显着发现The salient finding in Table4是那个is that内存深度越浅，执行速度越快shallower memory depth yields faster execution。这一优势的源泉是伽罗瓦场扩散阶段。更准确地说：虽然 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) (Python DICT) 在单个 Python 解释器线程上为边界中的每条路径顺序迭代哈希计算和指针追踪，但 PyVaCoAl 的谱系检索调度 Galois 域 LFSR 扩散（. The wellspring of this advantage is the Galois-field diffusion stage. More precisely: whereas Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) (Python DICT) iterates sequentially through hash calculations and pointer chasing on a single Python-interpreter thread for each path in the frontier, PyVaCoAl’s genealogy retrieval dispatches the Galois-field LFSR diffusion (_galois_diffuse_numba）通过 Numba JIT 编译的内核跨 12 个线程处理 128 个块。这个) across 12 threads for 128 blocks via a Numba JIT-compiled kernel. This用并行伽罗瓦域运算取代 DICT 的顺序哈希计算replaces DICT’s sequential hash computation with parallelised Galois-field operations;与向量化内存读取一致（; in concert with the vectorised memory read (内存[np.arange(128)，地址]memory[np.arange(128), addrs]）和 Numba JIT 投票统计（) and the Numba JIT vote tally (_count_votes_numba），构成了PyVaCoAl的速度优势), it constitutes PyVaCoAl’s speed advantage家谱检索期间during genealogy retrieval。请注意表. Note that Table4测量学习期间的检索时间，而不是写入速度。measures retrieval time, not write speed during learning.

对于 128_10 配置（内存深度For the 128_10 configuration (memory depth2102^{10})，其中 PyVaCoAl (), where PyVaCoAl (R​R=1RR=1）退化为伪 PyVaCoAl(1)，人们可能会认为 DICT 哈希表同样适合 CPU 的 L1/L2 缓存。仔细观察就会发现事实并非如此。 VaCoAl 内存占用为) degenerates into Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1), one might suppose that the DICT hash table likewise fits in the CPU’s L1/L2 cache. A closer look reveals otherwise. The VaCoAl memory footprint is128×1,024×4​bytes=512​KB128\times 1{,}024\times 4\;\mathrm{bytes}=512\;\mathrm{KB}，轻松驻留在二级缓存中；但是，Python DICT 存储了大约 470,000 条导师-学生记录（以字符串为键，以整数为值），运行量达到数十兆字节，并溢出了二级缓存。因此，128_10 中 19.7% 的加速来自于, comfortably resident in the L2 cache; but the Python DICT storing the approximately 470,000 mentor–student records, keyed by strings and valued by integers, runs to tens of megabytes and overflows the L2 cache. The 19.7% speedup in 128_10 therefore arises from a双dual优点：advantage:用并行伽罗瓦域扩散代替顺序散列the substitution of sequential hashing with parallel Galois-field diffusion, 复合为, compounded by非对称缓存效率，其中只有 PyVaCoAl 端实现缓存驻留an asymmetric cache efficiency in which only the PyVaCoAl side achieves cache residency.

Only the parallel Galois-field diffusion advantage survives, partially offset by the loss of cache locality and the rescue-path bottleneck, compressing the speed differential.

总之，PyVaCoAl 的速度优势源于 Numba 并行化伽罗瓦场扩散，包括In summary, PyVaCoAl’s speed superiority is rooted in the Numba-parallelised Galois-field diffusion comprisingf1f_{1}和andf2f_{2}。存储深度和救援电路充当了这一基本优势的放大器或衰减器。. Memory depth and the rescue circuit act as amplifiers or attenuators of this fundamental advantage.

这些结果对于 VaCoAl 的 SRAM-CAM 硬件化身具有重要意义。由于 PyVaCoAl（一种软件 DRAM-CAM）通过直接在主内存上驱动内存数据库，其性能已经优于 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1)，因此将该角色委托给 SRAM（其访问延迟比 DRAM 低一个数量级），预计将进一步大幅提升速度。此外，在 SRAM-CAM 中，执行每块 LFSR 计算These results carry important implications for VaCoAl’s hardware incarnation as SRAM-CAM. Because PyVaCoAl—a software DRAM-CAM—already outperforms Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) by driving the in-memory database directly on main memory, entrusting that role to SRAM, whose access latency is an order of magnitude lower than DRAM’s, can be expected to yield a further substantial speedup. In SRAM-CAM, moreover, the per-block LFSR computation is performed内存中in-memory。在 VaCoAl 的硬件实现中，整个表都驻留在专用 SRAM 中，DRAM 带来的缓存优势成为架构上的必然。因此，上面报告的 PyVaCoAl 时序可以解释为. The cache advantage that DRAM confers becomes, in VaCoAl’s hardware implementation where the entire table resides in dedicated SRAM, an architectural inevitability. The PyVaCoAl timings reported above can therefore be interpreted as下限lower boundsSRAM-VaCoAl 的性能。on the performance of SRAM-VaCoAl.

5.3.3 前沿规模的可扩展性和“相变”Frontier-Size Scalability and “Phase Transitions”

对不同 FS 值的系统速度比较揭示了 DICT 和 PyVaCoAl 之间存在“相变”（表A systematic speed comparison across varying FS values reveals the existence of a “phase transition” between DICT and PyVaCoAl (Table5).

At FS=2,000\mathrm{FS}=2{,}000，DICT 速度快 2.4 倍。在, DICT is 2.4 times faster. AtFS=10,000\mathrm{FS}=10{,}000两者是有竞争力的。在the two are competitive. AtFS=20,000\mathrm{FS}=20{,}000，PyVaCoAl 超越。在, PyVaCoAl overtakes. AtFS=25,000\mathrm{FS}=25{,}000，Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) 耗尽了系统内存，即使主内存为 128 GB，也变得无法执行。它的状态搜索要求每个节点过度分配访问集、元组结构和哈希表，并且内存占用量随 FS 超线性增长。相比之下，PyVaCoAl 凭借其通过固定长度数组进行的无状态投影，稳定地完成了相同的工作负载。因此，该架构具有决定性的实际优势：在传统实现遇到困难的情况下，它仍能保持功能。, Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) exhausted system memory and became unexecutable even with 128 GB of main memory. Its stateful search demands per-node overallocation of visited sets, tuple structures, and hash tables, and the memory footprint grows super-linearly with FS. PyVaCoAl, by contrast, completed the same workload stably, thanks to its stateless projection via fixed-length arrays. The architecture thus possesses a decisive practical advantage: it remains functional at scales where conventional implementations hit a wall.

这种逆转的本质是结构性的。 Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) 是一种与 Python 对象模型相关的“有状态”搜索； PyVaCoAl 是使用固定长度数组的“无状态”投影。前者需要访问集、元组和哈希表的每个节点副本，并且其内存占用随着 FS 的增长而爆炸。后者通过固定长度保持高速缓存一致的连续内存访问The essence of this reversal is structural. Pseudo-PyVaCoAl(1) is a “stateful” search tethered to the Python object model; PyVaCoAl is a “stateless” projection using fixed-length arrays. The former demands per-node copies of visited sets, tuples, and hash tables, and its memory footprint explodes as the FS grows. The latter maintains cache-coherent contiguous memory access through fixed-length整型32int32通过 Numba JIT 执行数组和本机机器代码。这种结构性鸿沟——arrays and native machine-code execution via Numba JIT. This structural divide—搜索（有状态）与投影（无状态）search (stateful) versus projection (stateless)——是 PyVaCoAl 在大规模数据上具有压倒性的可扩展性和内存效率的源泉。—is the wellspring of PyVaCoAl’s overwhelming scalability and memory efficiency on large-scale data.

5.4 解绑语义距离测量：“微积分度”与“巨分”的延续Semantic-Distance Measurement via Unbinding: Continualization of the “Calculus Degree” and the “Giant Score”

PyVaCoAl 的真正创新在于 HDC 特有的“解除绑定操作”，在这个强大的空间上执行。传统的软件哈希表不可逆地将组合键压缩为单个哈希值，从而排除了尊重多个谓词独立性的并行计算。相比之下，在当前架构中，可以通过解除绑定从复合捆绑向量中提取特定属性组，并且到目标概念的汉明距离（例如用于合成微积分度复合 HV 的 26 个字段标记）产生每个节点语义亲和力的连续标量度量。我们将这种衡量标准称为“微积分程度”。The genuine innovation of PyVaCoAl resides in the “Unbinding operation” distinctive to HDC, executed upon this robust space. Conventional software hash tables irreversibly compress composite keys into a single hash value, precluding parallel computation that respects the independence of multiple predicates. In the present architecture, by contrast, specific attribute groups can be extracted from a compositely Bundled vector through Unbinding, and the Hamming distance to a target concept (e.g. the 26 field tokens used to synthesise the Calculus Degree composite HV) yields a continuous scalar measure of each node’s semantic affinity. We term this measure the “Calculus Degree.”

为了同时捕获每个节点的学术相关性及其对谱系网络的结构影响，我们进一步定义To capture simultaneously each node’s academic relevance and its structural influence on the genealogical network, we further define the巨分Giant Score如下。首先，通过解除绑定从每个节点的复合 HV 中提取 FIELD 组件，并计算与 26 个微积分相关令牌 HV 中每一个的汉明相似度；最大值被视为该节点的微积分度as follows. First, the FIELD component is extracted from each node’s composite HV via Unbinding, and the Hamming similarity with each of the 26 calculus-related token HVs is computed; the maximum is taken as that node’s Calculus Degreess。由于不相关的二元 HV 之间的预期相似度为 0.5，因此我们重新调整：. Since the expected similarity between uncorrelated binary HVs is 0.5, we rescale:s^=max⁡(s−0.5, 0)×10\hat{s}=\max(s-0.5,\;0)\times 10, 映射, mapping[0.5, 0.6][0.5,\,0.6]到onto[0, 1][0,\,1]。接下来，每个节点的路径计数（通过它的菲尔兹奖得主家谱的数量）通过网络范围内的最大值进行归一化，以产生. Next, each node’s path count (the number of Fields Medalist genealogies passing through it) is normalised by the network-wide maximum to yieldt^∈[0, 1]\hat{t}\in[0,\,1]。然后，巨人分数被定义为他们的乘积：. The Giant Score is then defined as their product:

G=s^×t^G\;=\;\hat{s}\;\times\;\hat{t} (12)

这种乘法定义确保只有排名靠前的节点This multiplicative definition ensures that only nodes ranking high on两者both与微积分的语义接近度semantic proximity to calculus和and作为家谱中心的结构重要性获得高分。仅在一个维度上提升的节点（与微积分无关的频繁访问的中心，或缺乏网络影响力的微积分专家）会获得较低的分数。structural importance as a genealogical hub attain a high score. A node elevated on one dimension alone—a heavily traversed hub unrelated to calculus, or a calculus specialist lacking network influence—receives a low score.

通过这种动态测量，可以为科学史上长期存在的争论提供客观、定量的答案：“谁是微积分的真正鼻祖？” （表Through this dynamic measurement, it becomes possible to furnish an objective, quantitative answer to the perennial dispute in the history of science: “Who is the true progenitor of calculus?” (Table6).

莱布尼茨获得了 0.5973 的压倒性微积分巨人分数，其基础是 0.5686 的微积分度和 47 个遍历路径。相比之下，牛顿获得了 0.5336 的微积分学位，但仅获得了 4 条路径（总体排名第 91），这从数学上证明，在向后代直接传播知识（即作为推理中心）方面，他明显落后于莱布尼茨。Leibniz registered an overwhelming Calculus Giant Score of 0.5973, underpinned by a Calculus Degree of 0.5686 and 47 traversing paths. Newton, by contrast, attains a respectable Calculus Degree of 0.5336 but musters a mere 4 paths (91st overall), demonstrating mathematically that, in terms of direct knowledge transmission to posterity—that is, as a hub of reasoning—he falls decisively short of Leibniz.

5.4.1 “巨人肩膀”的数学识别：从伊斯兰科学到现代数学的知识接力（按时间顺序）Mathematical Identification of the “Shoulders of Giants”: The Knowledge Relay from Islamic Science to Modern Mathematics (Chronological Order)

这一分析进一步为科学史上的一个基本问题提供了定量答案：“现代科学的建筑师站在谁的巨人肩膀上？”在成为推理谱系最上游中心的 213 个人（如下所列）中，其最大路径数为 54，甚至超过了莱布尼茨，其中包括以下五位阿拉伯-伊斯兰科学的杰出人物。This analysis further yields a quantitative answer to a foundational question in the history of science: “On whose shoulders of giants did the architects of modern science stand?” Among the 213 individuals (enumerated below) who emerge as the furthest-upstream hubs of the reasoning genealogy—registering a maximum path count of 54 and surpassing even Leibniz—are the following five towering figures of Arabic-Islamic science.

这条链条中的第一个环节“沙拉夫”和最强大的枢纽“纳西尔”都来自位于当今伊朗东北部的图斯，该地区在 12 世纪和 13 世纪是数学和天文学的世界级枢纽，在那里形成的知识成为后来科学的基石。然而，这张桌子最引人注目的特点是接力棒的传递。伊斯兰科学的谱系在伊朗（图斯）和中亚（布哈拉）孕育，最终汇聚于格里戈里奥斯·乔尼亚德斯——一位来自东罗马-希腊圈拜占庭帝国的学者。当他在波斯学习并将知识带回西方时，Both “Sharaf,” the first link in this chain, and “Nasir,” the mightiest hub, hail from Tus in present-day northeastern Iran—a region that functioned in the 12th and 13th centuries as a world-class nexus of mathematics and astronomy, the knowledge forged there becoming a cornerstone of subsequent science. The most dramatic feature of this table, however, is the baton pass. The genealogy of Islamic science, nurtured in Iran (Tus) and Central Asia (Bukhara), ultimately converges on Grigorios Choniades—a scholar from the Byzantine Empire, the Eastern Roman–Greek sphere. When he studied in Persia and carried that knowledge back to the West,101010https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gregory_Chioniades通道首次打开，让“伊斯兰教的智慧”流入“欧洲文艺复兴（和现代数学）”。the channels opened for the first time, allowing the “wisdom of Islam” to flow into “the European Renaissance (and modern mathematics).”

这五人并不是狭义上的“数学家”。然而，在他们的遗产中深深植根着与“伊斯兰黄金时代”的决定性联系，“伊斯兰黄金时代”保存并扩展了古希腊的学问。 64 名菲尔兹奖获得者中，有 54 名 (84.4%) 的家谱集中在 12 世纪和 13 世纪的五位伊斯兰科学家身上。为了衡量这种融合的重要性：这五个记录占 470,000 条记录数据库的不到 0.001%。偶然出现 84.4% 收敛率的概率极低——统计证据表明菲尔兹奖得主谱系在整个数据库中存在极其单一的结构偏差。 PyVaCoAl 的 HDC 推理不仅仅检索“处理配方的个人”；它以数学方式挖掘了欧洲科学史本身的宏伟基础设施——“从古希腊到伊斯兰科学，再到文艺复兴的知识丝绸之路”——作为一种网络拓扑。These five were not “mathematicians” in the narrow sense. Yet embedded deep in their legacies is a decisive connection to the “Islamic Golden Age” that preserved and extended the learning of ancient Greece. Fully 54 of the 64 Fields Medalists (84.4%) trace genealogies that converge on five Islamic scientists of the 12th and 13th centuries. To gauge the non-triviality of this convergence: these five account for less than 0.001% of the 470,000-record database. The probability of an 84.4% convergence rate arising by chance is astronomically low—statistical evidence that the Fields Medalist genealogy harbours an extremely singular structural bias within the overall database. PyVaCoAl’s HDC reasoning did not merely retrieve “individuals who handled formulae”; it mathematically excavated the grand infrastructure of European scientific history itself—the “Silk Road of knowledge from ancient Greece, through Islamic science, to the Renaissance”—as a network topology.

前 5 个路径计数的频率惊人地高：213 个个体（54 个路径）、22 个（50 个）、5 个（49 个）、8 个（47 个）和 2 个（45 个）。如下图所示，他们按世纪细分，显示全部都是 17 世纪之前的人物，其中超过 70% 是文艺复兴时期的学者。从 18 世纪开始，唯一拥有最多 47 条道路的人是戈特弗里德·威廉·莱布尼茨（Gottfried Wilhelm Leibniz，1646-1716 年）。The frequencies of the top 5 path counts are strikingly high: 213 individuals (54 paths), 22 (50), 5 (49), 8 (47), and 2 (45). Their breakdown by century, shown below, reveals that all are pre-17th-century figures, with over 70% being Renaissance scholars. From the 18th century onward, the sole individual commanding the maximum of 47 paths is Gottfried Wilhelm Leibniz (1646–1716).

5.4.2 “寺墙”：知识传播媒介的历史转折点The “Monastery Wall”: A Historical Turning Point in the Medium of Knowledge Propagation

在谱系分析的过程中，我们试图通过每一种可以想象的参数值组合来扩展谱系。尽管做出了这些详尽的努力，家谱仍无法延伸到第 57 代之后。原因：54 条路径中的每一条都终止于坎特伯雷大主教兰弗朗克 (Lanfrancus Cantuariensis) 或沙特尔的伊沃，如上表最顶部所示。In the course of this genealogical analysis, we attempted to extend the lineage through every conceivable combination of parameter values. Despite these exhaustive efforts, the genealogy could not extend beyond the 57th generation. The cause: every one of the 54 paths terminates at Archbishop Lanfranc of Canterbury (Lanfrancus Cantuariensis) or Ivo of Chartres, shown at the very top of the preceding table.

大主教兰弗兰克（Lanfranc，1005-1089 年，出生于帕维亚）和沙特尔的伊沃（Ivo of Chartres，1040-1116 年）是中世纪欧洲知识传统中最杰出的学者，但维基数据中并未记录他们自己的“导师”（博士或学术顾问）。Archbishop Lanfranc (1005–1089, born in Pavia) and Ivo of Chartres (1040–1116) were scholars of the highest eminence in the intellectual tradition of medieval Europe, yet their own “mentors” (doctoral or academic advisors) are not recorded on Wikidata.111111维基数据：Wikidata:https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q317098（兰弗兰克），(Lanfranc),https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q124775（沙特尔的伊沃）。作为耶格(Ivo of Chartres). As Jaeger[15]历史上有记载，兰弗兰克曾在贝克修道院任教，伊沃则在沙特尔大教堂学校任教，但他们自己的“导师”却没有官方记录。details, it is historically documented that Lanfranc taught at the Abbey of Bec and Ivo at the cathedral school of Chartres, but their own “mentors” survive in no official record.由于没有指定这两个人的导师，所以所有的家谱都终止了。我们称这种现象为Because no mentor is specified for these two, all genealogies terminate. We call this phenomenon the《修道院的围墙》“Monastery Wall”，并大胆尝试手动突破它。, and venture a modest attempt to breach it manually.

家谱搜索在 11 世纪左右一直停止。这种停止反映的不是 PyVaCoAl 或 Wikidata 的技术限制，而是作为一种网络拓扑，检测到这样一个历史事实：知识传播的媒介从个人导师-学生关系转向修道院社区。换句话说，PyVaCoAl 拥有从历史结构中读取历史结构变化的能力。The genealogy search consistently halts around the 11th century. This cessation reflects not a technical limitation of PyVaCoAl or Wikidata but the detection, as a network topology, of the historical fact that the medium of knowledge propagation shifted from personal mentor–student bonds to monastic communities. PyVaCoAl, in other words, possesses the capacity to read historical structural change from the数据缺失本身的模式pattern of data absence itself。这构成了计算深层谱系分析（计算Prosopography）的一个新颖的方法论方面：不仅“可以看到什么”，而且“视觉失败的地方”都可以解释为历史证据。. This constitutes a novel methodological facet of computational deep-genealogy analysis (Computational Prosopography): not only “what can be seen” but “where vision fails” becomes interpretable as historical evidence.

饰演拉什达尔As Rashdall[34]系统论证和 De Ridder-Symoenssystematically argued and De Ridder-Symoens[10]用现代术语来说，从 10 世纪到 11 世纪的教育都是围绕修道院学校展开的（recast in modern terms, education from the 10th to the 11th century revolved around monastic schools (修道院学校scholae monasticae）和大教堂学校。修道院的理想) and cathedral schools. The monastic ideal of稳定位点stabilitas loci（地方的稳定性）使教育成为一项以理解经文和教义传播为导向的公共事务，而不是培养个人的学术血统。知识是通过非正式渠道传递的——口头传统、联合手稿抄写、礼仪的默契教育——而这些导师与学生的关系，从根本上不同于现代的同行，在结构化数据中没有留下任何痕迹(stability of place) made education a communal affair oriented toward scriptural understanding and doctrinal transmission, rather than the cultivation of personal academic lineages. Knowledge was handed down through informal channels—oral tradition, joint manuscript copying, the tacit pedagogy of liturgy—and these mentor–student relationships, fundamentally unlike their modern counterparts, left no trace in structured data[34, 10].

在寺墙的另一边 饰演 Shuntaro ItohOn the other side of the Monastery Wall, as Shuntaro Itoh[14]详细文献记载表明，伊斯兰黄金时代的杰出人物胡瓦里兹米（al-Khuwārizmī，Q13955；780-850）无疑积累了巨大的学识宝库，他为数学和天文学留下了巨大的贡献；哲学家兼医生伊本·鲁世德（Ibn Rushd，阿威罗伊，Q178496；1126-1198），因其对亚里士多德的不朽评论而闻名；以及许多其他人。伊藤写道：“我特别想强调的是，西欧世界遭遇了伊斯兰文明（12世纪）。也就是说，西欧世界原本只是一个封闭的、地方性的文化圈，接触了阿拉伯的先进文明，吸收了希腊和阿拉伯先进的学术和文化，从而改变了自己的文明形态。”documents in detail, there assuredly lay a vast storehouse of learning amassed by the luminaries of the Islamic Golden Age: al-Khuwārizmī (Q13955; 780–850), who left towering contributions to mathematics and astronomy; the philosopher-physician Ibn Rushd (Averroes, Q178496; 1126–1198), celebrated for his monumental commentaries on Aristotle; and many others. Itoh writes: “What I particularly wish to emphasise is the fact that the Western European world encountered Islamic civilisation (in the 12th century). That is, the Western European world, which had been nothing more than a closed, provincial cultural sphere, came into contact with the advanced civilisation of Arabia, absorbed the sophisticated scholarship and culture of Greece and Arabia, and thereby transformed its own civilisational form.”

在伊斯兰世界，早于西欧大学的系统性高等教育机构——例如布哈拉（今乌兹别克斯坦）的宗教学校——已经很完善。他们的大量知识通过“大翻译运动”流入西欧In the Islamic world, systematic institutions of higher learning antedating the Western European university—such as the madrasas of Bukhara (present-day Uzbekistan)—were already well established. Their vast store of knowledge flowed into Western Europe through the “Great Translation Movement”121212根据伊藤的说法（According to Itoh ([14]正是在 12 世纪，在皇室的大力支持下，阿拉伯文本（包括希腊原文和希腊文本本身的阿拉伯译本）到拉丁语的积极翻译在四个中心展开：阿拉贡学派（西班牙东北部）、托莱多学派、西西里学派和北意大利学派。), precisely in the 12th century, under vigorous royal patronage, active translation of Arabic texts (including Arabic renderings of Greek originals and the Greek texts themselves) into Latin unfolded across four centres: the Aragonese school (northeast Spain), the Toledo school, the Sicilian school, and the North Italian school.从阿拉伯语到拉丁语，是由西西里王国的跨文化共存和伊比利亚半岛的收复失地运动（特别是托莱多的收复）所催化的。这种知识的大规模传播——通过书籍和翻译来传播，超越了十字军东征等军事行动和个人教育系统的框架——是一种文明转移的形式，而维基数据的“师生关系”这一谓语永远无法捕捉到这种形式。from Arabic into Latin, catalysed by intercultural coexistence in the Kingdom of Sicily and the Reconquista on the Iberian Peninsula (notably the recapture of Toledo). This massive propagation of knowledge—transmitted through books and translators, transcending the frameworks of military campaigns like the Crusades and of personal educational systems—was a form of civilisational transfer that can never be captured by Wikidata’s predicate of “mentor–student relationship.”

这里使用的导师-学生数据库中大约 470,000 条记录中，确实出现了托莱多翻译学院的杰出人物，如克雷莫纳的杰拉德（Q367240，卒于 1187 年）和迈克尔·斯科特（Q559548，卒于 1232 年），但不幸的是，他们的家谱与菲尔兹奖获得者没有联系。正如伊藤Among the roughly 470,000 records in the mentor–student database used here, such luminaries of the Toledo School of Translators as Gerard of Cremona (Q367240, d. 1187) and Michael Scot (Q559548, d. 1232) do appear, though unfortunately their genealogies did not connect to the Fields Medalists. As Itoh[14]据叙述，1085 年卡斯蒂利亚和莱昂国王阿方索六世从穆斯林统治下夺回托莱多后，在大主教雷蒙德一世的领导下成立了一个阿拉伯学术研究中心。在莫扎拉布（阿拉伯化基督徒）和皈依犹太人的合作下，法拉比（Q168536；870-950）和阿维森纳（伊本）创作了一流的哲学和科学著作。新浪，Q8011；980-1037）被翻译成拉丁文。尤其是克雷莫纳的杰拉德，从意大利出发寻找托勒密（Q34933，卒于 175 年）的天文学论文recounts, after King Alfonso VI of Castile and León recaptured Toledo from Muslim rule in 1085, an Arabic academic research centre was established under Archbishop Raymond I. With the collaboration of Mozarabs (Arabicised Christians) and converted Jews, first-rate philosophical and scientific works by al-Fārābī (Q168536; 870–950) and Avicenna (Ibn Sina, Q8011; 980–1037) were rendered into Latin. Gerard of Cremona, in particular, journeyed from Italy in search of Ptolemy’s (Q34933, d. 175) astronomical treatise天文学大成Almagest最终将超过 87 部希腊和阿拉伯学术著作翻译成拉丁文，包括亚里士多德（Q868，卒于公元前 321 年）、欧几里得（Q8747，卒于公元前 270 年）、阿基米德（Q8739，卒于公元前 212 年）和盖伦（Q171485，卒于公元前 216 年）的著作and ultimately translated more than 87 Greek and Arabic scholarly works into Latin, including texts by Aristotle (Q868, d. 321 BCE), Euclid (Q8747, d. 270 BCE), Archimedes (Q8739, d. 212 BCE), and Galen (Q171485, d. 216)[14].

值得注意的是，尽管维基数据的导师-学生谓词无法捕捉到这一翻译事业的影响，但它通过 PyVaCoAl 的 HDC 操作在 Field 信号的时间序列分析中作为定量轨迹持续存在。具体来说，如下表所示，天文学的相似度得分急剧上升Remarkably, although the impact of this translation enterprise cannot be captured by Wikidata’s mentor–student predicate, it persists as a quantitative trace in the time-series analysis of the FIELD signal via PyVaCoAl’s HDC operations. Specifically, as shown in the table below, the similarity score for astronomy rises sharply by+0.0025+0.0025 (▲​▲\blacktriangle\blacktriangle）相对于 1250-1300 年间的上一代窗口，与第二托莱多时期一致) relative to the preceding generation window during 1250–1300, coinciding with the second Toledo period131313在这里，我们定义了托莱多第一阶段（1100-1200）、托莱多第二阶段（1200-1300）和文艺复兴时期（1300-1600：文艺复兴早期1300-1400；高峰/知识爆炸1450-1500；文艺复兴后期到科学革命过渡1500-1600）。相比之下，对于代数，FIELD 相似性在对应于托莱多第 2 阶段的 1300-1350 窗口中仅表现出小数点后第四位的正变化。Here we define Toledo Phase 1 (1100–1200), Toledo Phase 2 (1200–1300), and the Renaissance period (1300–1600: early Renaissance 1300–1400; peak / knowledge explosion 1450–1500; late Renaissance through transition to the Scientific Revolution 1500–1600). For algebra, by contrast, the FIELD similarity exhibits only a fourth-decimal-place positive change in the 1300–1350 window corresponding to Toledo Phase 2.。更引人注目的是，在文艺复兴时期（1450-1500）的鼎盛时期，天文学的发展迅猛发展。. Still more strikingly, in the peak of the Renaissance (1450–1500), astronomy surges by+0.0065+0.0065 (▲​▲​▲​▲​▲​▲\blacktriangle\blacktriangle\blacktriangle\blacktriangle\blacktriangle\blacktriangle)——几乎是第二个托莱多增量的三倍。值得注意的是，这个时代与哥白尼（Nicolaus Copernicus，Q14227；1473-1543）的一生重叠，他的日心说在很大程度上借鉴了伊斯兰天文学（特别是马拉加天文台学校）的成就。)—nearly three times the second Toledo increment. It is worth noting that this era overlaps with the lifetime of Copernicus (Nicolaus Copernicus, Q14227; 1473–1543), whose heliocentric proposal drew heavily on the achievements of Islamic astronomy (notably the Maragha observatory school).

自20世纪中叶以来，科学史学家已经用无可辩驳的数学和语言学证据证实了这一点（SalibaThis point has been established with irrefutable mathematical and philological evidence by historians of science since the mid-20th century (Saliba[36]）。法国科学史家皮埃尔·迪昂（Pierre Duhem，1861-1916）的研究尤其值得注意。伊藤). The research of the French historian of science Pierre Duhem (1861–1916) is particularly noteworthy. Itoh[14]写道：writes:

“有关中世纪科学的手稿被发现。随着对这些手稿的研究，人们开始认识到‘惯性定律’和‘落体定律’的种子——迄今为止被认为是 17 世纪的伽利略和笛卡尔——以及主张地球自转的日心说和解析几何，在 14 世纪就已经存在了。……杜昂提出了现代科学并非开始的革命性论点。 17 世纪有伽利略、笛卡尔和牛顿，但有 14 世纪的经院哲学家。”“Manuscripts concerning medieval science were discovered. As such manuscripts came to be studied, it began to be recognised that the seeds of the ‘law of inertia,’ and indeed of the ‘law of falling bodies’—hitherto ascribed to Galileo and Descartes in the 17th century—as well as the heliocentric theory asserting the Earth’s rotation, and analytic geometry, already existed in the 14th century. … Duhem advanced the revolutionary thesis that modern science did not begin in the 17th century with Galileo, Descartes, and Newton, but with the Scholastics of the 14th century.”

此外，根据乔治·萨利巴（George Saliba）的说法（Moreover, according to George Saliba ([36]），所记录的证据简直令人震惊：不懂阿拉伯语的哥白尼，从伊斯兰天文学巨头 al-Dīn al-Urḍī (1200–1266)、Naṣīr al-Dīn al-Ṭūsī (1201–1274)、Ibn al-Shāṭir 那里借用了图表和数学模型。 （1304-1375）——比他的时代早 200-300 年创作，在一位翻译熟人的帮助下将它们直接翻译成拉丁文，并用它们来论证日心说。), the documented evidence is nothing short of astonishing: Copernicus, who could not read Arabic, borrowed diagrams and mathematical models from the giants of Islamic astronomy—al-Dīn al-Urḍī (1200–1266), Naṣīr al-Dīn al-Ṭūsī (1201–1274), Ibn al-Shāṭir (1304–1375)—composed 200–300 years before his time, had them translated directly into Latin with the assistance of a translator acquaintance, and deployed them to argue the case for heliocentrism.

尽管没有明确列出，HDC 解绑行动还发现同一时期地理重心从阿巴斯哈里发国转移到了神圣罗马帝国。这表明，以托莱多翻译学院为桥梁的“墙外”知识的涌入，虽然在师生关系的官方记录中不可见，但作为学术领域语义重心在超高维空间中的迁移是可以观察到的。Although not explicitly tabulated, HDC unbinding operations also detected a shift in the geographic centre of gravity from the Abbasid Caliphate to the Holy Roman Empire during the same period. This suggests that the influx of knowledge from “beyond the wall,” bridged by the Toledo School of Translators, though invisible in the official record of mentor–student relationships, is observable as a migration of the semantic centre of gravity of academic fields in ultra-high-dimensional space.

综合来看，“修道院墙”既是这种分析的局限性，也是对科学史具有独立意义的发现。 54 个人 (84%) 的家谱——他们是 64 位菲尔兹奖获得者中血统最长的人——在这堵墙附近汇聚，从网络拓扑的角度来看，这从数量上证实了现代西方科学完全依赖于极少数通过机构手段跨越修道院墙获得古代知识的个人。它们就像是在墙上打出的洞。Judged in the round, the “Monastery Wall” is simultaneously a limitation of this analysis and a discovery of independent significance for the history of science. That the genealogies of 54 individuals (84%)—those who traced the longest lineage among the 64 Fields Medalists—converge just short of this wall quantitatively corroborates, from the standpoint of network topology, that modern Western science depended decisively on an exceedingly small number of individuals who received ancient knowledge across the Monastery Wall by institutional means. They are, as it were, holes punched through the wall.

方法论考虑：区分数据缺失和历史结构变化Methodological Consideration: Distinguishing Data Absence from Historical Structural Change

对于 11 世纪家谱的终止，两种解释在原则上是相互竞争的。第一个读作Two interpretations compete in principle for the 11th-century cessation of genealogies. The first reads it as结构性数据丢失structural data loss植根于中世纪修道院教育中非正式的师生关系。第二个读作rooted in the informality of mentor–student ties in medieval monastic education. The second reads it as历史结构变化的网络检测network detection of historical structural change：知识传播的媒介从个人导师转向修道院社区。: the medium of knowledge propagation shifted from personal mentorship to monastic communities.

至关重要的是，这些解释是Crucially, these interpretations are不互相排斥not mutually exclusive。如果说知识未被记录的原因本身就是历史结构变迁的结果，那么“损失模式”和“结构变迁信号”就是一个硬币的两个面。饰演拉什达尔. If the very reason knowledge was not recorded is itself a consequence of historical structural change, then the “loss pattern” and the “signal of structural change” are two faces of a single coin. As Rashdall[34]和德·里德·西蒙斯and De Ridder-Symoens[10]文件中，寺院教育中知识传播的非正式性——口头传统、联合抄写、礼拜仪式——支持这样的解释：document, the informality of knowledge transmission in monastic education—oral tradition, joint copying, liturgical pedagogy—supports the interpretation that记录的缺失本身就反映了那个时代的制度特征the absence of records itself reflects the institutional character of the era.

因此，本文明确采用了计算 Prosopography 的方法论立场：这种停止并不仅仅被视为“数据限制”而被忽视，而是This paper therefore explicitly adopts the methodological stance of Computational Prosopography: the cessation is not dismissed as a mere “data limitation” but is作为历史证据本身来解读——“可见的东西”和“视觉失败的地方”都是一样的read as historical evidence in its own right—“what can be seen” and “where vision fails” alike。然而，要全面验证这种解释，需要对维基数据的记录偏差与独立历史来源进行额外的交叉引用，这被认为是未来工作的一项任务。. Full verification of this interpretation, however, requires additional cross-referencing of Wikidata’s recording biases against independent historical sources, and this is noted as a task for future work.

5.5 知识谱系“范式转变”的量化论证Quantitative Demonstration of “Paradigm Shifts” in the Genealogy of Knowledge

当从宏观角度审视由约 2550 万条交通记录编织而成的“知识谱系”时，托马斯·库恩理论化的“范式转变（常规科学的建立）”的动态（When the “genealogy of knowledge” woven from approximately 25.5 million traffic records is surveyed from a macroscopic vantage point, the dynamics of the “paradigm shift (establishment of normal science)” theorised by Thomas Kuhn ([22]) 在) in科学革命的结构The Structure of Scientific Revolutions一旦镜头对准戈特弗里德·威廉·莱布尼茨（Gottfried Wilhelm Leibniz，Q3033；1646-1716）——一位将 17 世纪不同学科统一并系统化的博学者，这些问题就会以惊人的定量清晰度显现出来。emerge with startling quantitative clarity once the lens is trained on Gottfried Wilhelm Leibniz (Q3033; 1646–1716)—the polymath who unified and systematised the disparate disciplines of the 17th century.

5.5.1 沙漏模型与“高速公路”的形成The Hourglass Model and the Formation of the “Superhighway”

对以莱布尼茨为中心的 20 代导师和 15 代学生的推理流量进行可视化分析，揭示了典型的“沙漏（漏斗）”结构（图Visual analysis of reasoning traffic across 20 mentor generations and 15 student generations centred on Leibniz reveals a canonical “hourglass (funnel)” structure (Figure1）。莱布尼茨上游的节点（导师方向）平均有 5.3 条遍历路径；下游（学生方向），该数字爆炸至 53.4。 10.12的厚度比预示着一种极端的交通不对称：分散的前范式知识被整合到一个特定的枢纽，一举打造出一条直接连接现代数学的巨大“高速公路”。). Nodes upstream of Leibniz (mentor direction) carry an average of 5.3 traversing paths; downstream (student direction), the figure explodes to 53.4. This thickness ratio of 10.12 bespeaks an extreme traffic asymmetry: scattered pre-paradigmatic knowledge was integrated at a specific hub, from which a massive “superhighway” connecting directly to modern mathematics was forged in a single stroke.

高斯、希尔伯特和欧拉也证实了相同的宏观收敛现象（表The same macroscopic convergence phenomenon is confirmed for Gauss, Hilbert, and Euler (Table9）。与同时代独立发现微积分、被视为莱布尼茨在科学史上的最高对手的艾萨克·牛顿的交通结构呈现出鲜明的对比（图).The traffic structure of Isaac Newton—who discovered calculus independently in the same era and is regarded as Leibniz’s supreme rival in the annals of science—presents a stark contrast (Figure2).141414关于牛顿阵营发起的微积分创立优先权之争，参见Maria Rosa Antognazza的系列作品On the priority dispute over the founding of calculus, initiated by the Newton camp, see the series of works by Maria Rosa Antognazza[4] 等人。et al.牛顿及其随行人员对莱布尼茨进行的无情诽谤几乎令人难以置信。The relentless campaign of vilification waged against Leibniz by Newton and his entourage is scarcely credible.只有 4 名菲尔兹奖得主的谱系可以追溯到牛顿。在任一方向上都不存在流量的链式扩展，即不存在收缩和爆炸模式；他的推理路径只是一根细细的、孤立的线索。Only 4 Fields Medalists trace their genealogy to Newton. There is no chain-like expansion of traffic—no constriction-and-explosion pattern—in either direction; his reasoning path is merely a thin, solitary thread.

与此同时，莱布尼茨出现在 64 名菲尔兹奖得主中的 47 名的家谱中，证实了他统一并系统化了 17 世纪不同学科的论点，以及穿过他的路径形成了一个巨大的沙漏形范式漏斗。相比之下，牛顿的奖牌获得者只有 4 名，如图所示Leibniz, meanwhile, appears in the genealogies of a full 47 of the 64 Fields Medalists—corroborating the thesis that he unified and systematised the diverse 17th-century disciplines, and that the paths passing through him formed a massive hourglass-shaped paradigm funnel. Newton’s reach, by contrast, terminates at 4 medalists, and as Figure2很明显，他的流量概况在两个方向上都没有表现出收缩和爆炸模式，只是一条细直线。从网络几何的角度来看，莱布尼茨和牛顿之间的这种交通几何学的残酷对比有力地证实了，真正的“高速公路的起源”——整合知识并将其传播给下一代的节点——至少在现代数学范式的形成中不是牛顿，而是莱布尼茨。makes plain, his traffic profile exhibits no constriction-and-explosion pattern in either direction—nothing but a thin straight line. This brutally contrasting traffic geometry between Leibniz and Newton decisively corroborates, from the vantage of network geometry, that the true “origin of the superhighway”—the node that integrated knowledge and propagated it to the next generation—was, at least in the formation of the modern mathematics paradigm, not Newton but Leibniz.

5.5.2 制度化和媒体的爆炸式出现Institutionalisation and the Explosive Emergence of Media

莱布尼茨对 17 世纪学科的巩固也可以表现为托马斯·库恩式的范式转变。库恩将“专业学会的建立”和“标准教科书的建立”作为新范式形成的标志，PyVaCoAl 中引入的 12 维复合谓词分析以惊人的保真度捕捉了这一历史转变（表Leibniz’s consolidation of 17th-century disciplines can also be demonstrated as a Thomas Kuhn-style paradigm shift. Kuhn cites “the founding of specialised societies” and “the establishment of standard textbooks” as hallmarks of a new paradigm’s formation, and the 12-dimensional composite-predicate analysis introduced in PyVaCoAl captures this historical transition with remarkable fidelity (Table10).

莱布尼茨之前（前时期）学者的社团会员率仅为 6.5%（每人 0.08 个社团）；在莱布尼茨（后时期）之后，该比例飙升至 82.1%（每人 3.81 个社团）——平均会员人数爆炸性增长约 46 倍。学术语言的香农熵测量定量地证实了媒体的标准化：从中世纪的多样化语言分布（熵 3.263），到文艺复兴时期拉丁语和德语的融合，再到英语的最终整合。这些正是常规科学诞生的标志。顺便说一句，根据 WIKIDATA，莱布尼茨本人精通七种语言。The society-membership rate among scholars before Leibniz (Pre-period) stood at a mere 6.5% (0.08 societies per person); after Leibniz (Post-period) it soared to 82.1% (3.81 societies per person)—an explosive increase of roughly 46-fold in average memberships. Shannon-entropy measurement of academic languages quantitatively confirmed the standardisation of media: from the diverse linguistic distribution of the Middle Ages (entropy 3.263), through convergence on Latin and German in the Renaissance, to ultimate consolidation around English. These are precisely the hallmarks heralding the birth of normal science. Incidentally, according to WIKIDATA, Leibniz himself was proficient in seven languages.

两个排名之间的反差十分惊人。在这两方面，第一名都是托马修斯（Thomasius），他被誉为莱布尼茨的导师；但在 PyVaCoAl 中，莱布尼茨跃升至第二位（1321 万票），微积分的主流——欧拉（第 7 位）、约翰·伯努利（第 8 位）、拉格朗日（第 17 位）、泊松（第 20 位）——进入了前 20 名The contrast between the two rankings is striking. 这种分歧通过HDC解绑操作得到了验证。将所有 DICT Top-20 成员的复合 HV 捆绑在一起并解除绑定 FIELD 组件，产生了一个 FIELD 质心，其中解剖学排名第一（相似度 0.5322），数学下降到第四位。对于 PyVaCoAl 的前 20 名，质心将数学排名第一 (0.5258)，将数学分析排名第三 (0.5163)。在对 64 位菲尔兹奖得主的谱系分析中，以解剖学为中心的菲尔兹质心在历史上是不协调的； PyVaCoAl 的结果更忠实地符合历史现实。This divergence was verified through HDC unbinding operations. Bundling the composite HVs of all DICT Top-20 members and unbinding the FIELD component yielded a FIELD centroid in which anatomy ranked 1st (similarity 0.5322) and mathematics sank to 4th. For PyVaCoAl’s Top 20, the centroid placed mathematics 1st (0.5258) and mathematical analysis 3rd (0.5163). In a genealogical analysis of 64 Fields Medalists, a FIELD centroid topped by anatomy is historically incongruous; PyVaCoAl’s result accords far more faithfully with historical reality.

我们进一步比较了五名 DICT 独家参赛者和五名 PyVaCoAl 独家参赛者之间的微积分程度和巨人分数（3-P 分析）。We further compared the Calculus Degree and Giant Score (3-P analysis) between the five DICT-exclusive entrants and the five PyVaCoAl-exclusive entrants.

DICT 专有组的平均微积分度为 0.5011，与随机噪声基线 0.5000 几乎没有区别；其平均巨型分数仅为微不足道的 0.0114。相比之下，PyVaCoAl 专有组的平均微积分度为 0.5320，平均 Giant Score 为 0.2674，Giant Score 的差异大约为 23 倍。简而言之，DICT 提升的数字是“语义上空洞的中转点”，它们只是碰巧位于谱系图上的古代交汇处； PyVaCoAl 所提升的那些是“高速公路的真正组成部分”，极大地促进了微积分的发展。The DICT-exclusive group’s mean Calculus Degree of 0.5011 is virtually indistinguishable from the random-noise baseline of 0.5000; its mean Giant Score stands at a paltry 0.0114. The PyVaCoAl-exclusive group, by contrast, registers a mean Calculus Degree of 0.5320 and a mean Giant Score of 0.2674—a roughly 23-fold difference in Giant Score. The figures elevated by DICT are, in short, “semantically hollow transit points” that merely happen to sit at ancient junctions on the genealogical graph; those elevated by PyVaCoAl are “genuine components of the superhighway” that materially advanced the development of calculus.

5.6.3 阐明机制：深度相关的指数衰减惩罚Elucidating the Mechanism: Depth-Dependent Exponential Decay Penalty

VaCoAl 的 Don't Care 机制相当于谱系搜索中的“奥卡姆剃刀”。每次在给定的一代随机发生块碰撞时，路径积分值 CR2 都会乘法收缩。对于浅路径，这种衰减可以忽略不计；对于深层路径，它就像兴趣一样复合。结果是：在 FS 修剪过程中，长的迂回路径首先被剔除，短的直接路径选择性地生存。去掉不必要的冗长解释，保留最简洁的解释——这就是奥卡姆剃刀的原则；巧合的是，William of Ockham 本人也跻身 DICT 和 PyVaCoAl 的前 20 名之列。VaCoAl’s Don’t Care mechanism functions as “Occam’s Razor” in genealogical search. Each time a block collision stochastically occurs at a given generation, the path-integral value CR2 contracts multiplicatively. For shallow paths this decay is negligible; for deep paths it compounds like interest. The result: during FS pruning, long circuitous routes are culled first and short direct routes selectively survive. To pare away unnecessarily long explanations and retain the most parsimonious—this is the very principle of Occam’s Razor; and it is a fitting coincidence that William of Ockham himself figures in the Top 20 of both DICT and PyVaCoAl.

我们证明，即使对于We show that “Occam’s Razor” effect emerges even for theB=128B=128和andM=227M=2^{27}配置，因为除了以下情况外，它可以获得接近 1.0 的最高 CR1configuration because it could attain the highest CR1 near 1.0 except for the case ofB=64B=64和andM=228M=2^{28}容易发生拓扑混乱的地方。事实上，即使在这种情况下，假设 CR1 下降到where topological Confusion tends to occur. Indeed, even in this case, assuming CR1 drops to127/128≈0.992127/128\approx 0.992每次发生 Don't Care 时，CR2 按代衰减如下：each time a Don’t Care occurs, CR2 decays by generation as follows:

前沿生存率分析直接证实了这一假设。 DICT 专有群体（解剖学医师）集中在 21-26 代（深度）； PyVaCoAl 专有群体（数学家）聚集在第 9-12 代（浅层）——代际深度的差异超过两倍。在DICT/rescue-on配置中，CR1被锁定在1.0，因此深路径和浅路径被同等对待，而解剖学家只是古老的交汇点，在流量方面占据了上层行列。在无救援配置中，CR2 衰变自然会修剪深层路径，导致数学主流（“接近且直接”地接近菲尔兹奖得主）上升到顶峰。Frontier survival-rate analysis directly corroborates this hypothesis. The DICT-exclusive group (physicians of anatomy) clusters in generations 21–26 (deep); the PyVaCoAl-exclusive group (mathematicians) clusters in generations 9–12 (shallow)—a more than twofold difference in generational depth. In the DICT / rescue-on configuration, CR1 is locked at 1.0, so deep and shallow paths are treated identically, and physicians of anatomy who are merely ancient junction points dominate the upper ranks by traffic volume. In the no-rescue configuration, CR2 decay naturally prunes deep paths, causing the mainstream of mathematics—“close and direct” to Fields Medalists—to rise to the top.

对于菲尔兹奖得主来说，数学的主流（欧拉For Fields Medalists, the mainstream of mathematics (Euler→\to伯努利Bernoulli→\toLeibniz）是一条大约 10 代的短路径；医学和解剖学分支（Fabricius、Falloppio、Leibniz) is a short path of roughly 10 generations; the branch toward medicine and anatomy (Fabricius, Falloppio,等人。et al.）是一个超过 25 代的弯路。 VaCoAl 的剃刀保留了前者并切断了后者。这种选择并不是源于架构“理解”语义；而是源于架构“理解”语义。这是一种新兴现象，其中“不关心”机制的物理衰退自然地利用了“路径深度”和“学科的历史接近性”之间的强相关性。) is a detour exceeding 25 generations. VaCoAl’s razor preserves the former and severs the latter. This selection does not arise from the architecture “understanding” semantics; it is an emergent phenomenon in which the physical decay of the Don’t Care mechanism naturally exploited the strong correlation between “path depth” and “historical proximity of academic discipline.”

两组的 CR2 均值实际上相同（0.9406 与 0.9407）同样与该机制一致。 CR2 较低的深层路径在 FS 剪枝期间被消除，使幸存群体的 CR2 均值向上偏置。观察到的匹配不应被理解为“没有差异”，而应被理解为“在路径发生变化后的生存偏差”That the CR2 means of the two groups are virtually identical (0.9406 vs. 0.9407) is likewise consistent with this mechanism. Deep paths with low CR2 are eliminated during FS pruning, biasing the surviving population’s CR2 mean upward. The observed match should be understood not as “there is no difference” but as “survivorship bias after paths that做了did不同的已经被淘汰了。”differ have already been culled.”

5.6.4 类似 STDP 的衰变和路径依赖的出现STDP-like Decay and the Emergence of Path Dependence

上面记录的“不关心”衰变在功能上发挥了作用 相当于尖峰时间相关塑性 (STDP) 生物神经回路。 STDP是一项重要的学习 大脑原理：两个神经元尖峰时间越接近 发生时，它们之间的突触连接越多 加强；距离越远，距离就越远 削弱（The Don’t Care decay documented above plays a role functionally equivalent to Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity (STDP) in biological neural circuits. STDP is a cardinal learning principle of the brain: the closer in time two neuronal spikes occur, the more the synaptic connection between them is strengthened; the further apart, the more it is weakened ([35]）。 VaCoAl 的 Don't Care 机制 在谱系空间中制定相同的逻辑：路径 世代接近菲尔兹奖得主的起点 保持高 CR2，而世代远程路径受到影响 CR2 呈指数衰减并在 FS 修剪期间被剔除。的 由此产生的选择——“强化近的，弱化近的” 远”——自然地铭刻了知识的历史 作为物理量的传递：突触强度 大脑； VaCoAl 中 CR2 的大小。). VaCoAl’s Don’t Care mechanism enacts the same logic in genealogical space: paths generationally close to the Fields Medalist starting point retain high CR2, whereas generationally remote paths suffer exponential CR2 decay and are culled during FS pruning. The resulting selection—“strengthen the near, weaken the far”—naturally inscribes the history of knowledge transmission as a physical quantity: synaptic strength in the brain; the magnitude of CR2 in VaCoAl.

但值得注意的是，一个重要因素 使这一机制如此有效地发挥作用的原因是 历史导师-学生数据集是一个有向无环图 （DAG）具有很强的时间顺序。It should be noted, however, that a major factor enabling this mechanism to function so effectively is that the historical mentor–student dataset is a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) possessing strong temporal ordering.肉体的腐朽 因此，CR2 的语义值与 “世代相近。”该机制如何发挥作用 不同拓扑的本体空间——具有 例如，强大的双向循环是一个重要的 对今后工作的疑问。The physical decay of CR2 therefore correlates cleanly with the semantic value of “generational proximity.” How the mechanism behaves in ontological spaces of different topologies—networks with strong bidirectional loops, for instance—is an important question for future work.

这一发现具有更广泛的意义。除了 附录 B.4 中证明了侧向抑制（空间选择）， Don’t Care 机制同时实现了临时性 选择——路径深度相关的衰减特性This discovery has a broader significance. In addition to the lateral inhibition (spatial selection) proven in Appendix B.4, the Don’t Care mechanism simultaneously realises temporal selection—a path-depth-dependent decay characteristic设计时无意的unintended at design time。递归结构 CR2 乘法积分 （. Within the framework of “convergent computational equivalence” defined in Appendix B.1, VaCoAl’s Don’t Care decay and the brain’s STDP are convergent phenomena: they arrived at the same functional structure by independently solving the same computational problem on different implementation platforms. This constitutes the first quantitative grounding, based on large-scale real data, for the interpretive proposition advanced in Appendix B.3.151515定量物种规模锚 CA3类比：与CA3循环络的功能类比 附录 B.3 中开发的——基于递归 结构A Quantitative Species-Scale Anchor for the CA3 Analogy: The functional analogy with CA3 recurrent collaterals developed in Appendix B.3—based on the recursive structureC​R2(n)=C​R2(n−1)×C​R1(n−1)CR_{2}^{(n)}=CR_{2}^{(n-1)}\times CR_{1}^{(n-1)}— 可以锐化为具体的、定量的物种规模 通过观察 Treves-Rolls 容量的比较 公式（— can be sharpened into a concrete, quantitative species-scale comparison by noting how the Treves–Rolls capacity formula ([35]，等式。 9.3),, Eq. 9.3),pmax≈CR​C/(ap​ln⁡(1/ap))​kp_{\max}\approx C_{RC}/(a_{p}\ln(1/a_{p}))\,k, 识别 每个 CA3 细胞的循环侧支突触数量，, identifies the number of recurrent-collateral synapses per CA3 cell,CR​CC_{RC}，作为决定性的容量参数。劳斯莱斯 强调“CA3-CA3循环抵押系统 在猕猴中比在老鼠中更广泛” （, as the decisive capacity parameter. VaCoAl’s true capability is therefore demonstrated in its native Don’t Care mode, not in the rescue mode that guarantees agreement with DICT.

5.7 第 5 节的结论Conclusion of Section 5

本节中报告的实验表明，PyVaCoAl（一种代数确定性数字推理架构）是一个具有质量上不同的路径质量量化（CR2）和语义选择（Don’t Care 机制）能力的系统，大大超越了单纯的“能够进行 FS 相对精确匹配的高速数据库”的框架。The experiments reported in this section have demonstrated that PyVaCoAl—an algebro-deterministic digital reasoning architecture—is a system endowed with qualitatively distinct capabilities of path-quality quantification (CR2) and semantic selection (Don’t Care mechanism), transcending by a wide margin the framework of a mere “high-speed database capable of FS-relative Exact Match.”

首先，通过利用HDC正交空间和多项式运算的硬件模型，我们获得了一个宏观显微镜，能够测量数千万个数据点上的“知识的连续梯度（微积分度）”，并能够调查“历史的结构转变（范式转变）”。First, by harnessing the HDC orthogonal space and the hardware model of polynomial operations, we acquired a macroscope capable of measuring the “continuous gradient of knowledge (Calculus Degree)” across tens of millions of data points and of surveying the “structural transformations of history (paradigm shifts).”

其次，PyVaCoAl 的多重配置比较表明，VaCoAl 的 Don't Care 机制起到了“奥卡姆剃刀”的作用，具有类似 STDP 的机制，可以通过指数衰减自然地修剪深层路径。这种机制忠实地反映了学术史上固有的路径依赖，自然地将 DICT 的异常结果——“解剖学家占据了菲尔兹奖获得者谱系的前 20 名”——转化为历史上有效的结果：“数学主流正确地上升到了前 20 名。”Second, multiple-configuration comparison of PyVaCoAl revealed that VaCoAl’s Don’t Care mechanism functions as “Occam’s Razor,” harbouring an STDP-like mechanism that naturally prunes deep paths through exponential decay. This mechanism faithfully mirrors the path dependence inherent in the history of scholarship, naturally converting DICT’s anomalous result—“anatomists occupy the Top 20 of Fields Medalist genealogies”—into the historically valid outcome: “the mainstream of mathematics correctly rises to the Top 20.”

第三，这一发现首次为 CA3 循环络合物的功能类比提供了植根于大规模真实数据的定量基础，而 CA3 循环络合物的功能类比仍然是附录 B.3 中的“解释性命题”。 VaCoAl 的 Don't Care 衰减和大脑的 STDP 通过独立解决相同的计算问题，收敛于相同的功能结构：“信息的路径相关加权和不必要路径的修剪”。这种“收敛计算等价性”的证明构成了最直接的证据，表明VaCoAl不仅仅是一个高速搜索引擎，而是一个建立在与大脑记忆和推理系统基本等同的计算原理基础上的“HDC-AI”。Third, this discovery provided, for the first time, quantitative grounding rooted in large-scale real data for the functional analogy with CA3 recurrent collaterals that had remained an “interpretive proposition” in Appendix B.3. VaCoAl’s Don’t Care decay and the brain’s STDP converged on the same functional structure by independently solving the same computational problem: “path-dependent weighting of information and pruning of unnecessary paths.” This demonstration of “convergent computational equivalence” constitutes the most direct evidence that VaCoAl is not a mere high-speed search engine but an “HDC-AI” founded on computational principles fundamentally equivalent to those of the brain’s memory and reasoning systems.

这些结果有力地证明，在架构层面解决“绑定问题”的系统可以在规模和复杂性巨大的现实世界知识空间中实现弹性和创造性的“组合推理”。These results stand as robust evidence that a system resolving the “Binding Problem” at the architectural level can realise resilient and creative “compositional reasoning” in a real-world knowledge space of formidable scale and complexity.

6 结论：从计算到检索和关联——下一代神经符号人工智能的基础Conclusion: From Computation to Retrieval and Association—The Foundation for Next-Generation Neuro-symbolic AI

本文提出了超高速、低成本、超高维 SRAM/DRAM-CAM 架构“VaCoAl”及其 Python 实现“PyVaCoAl”，作为突破现代人工智能面临的双重限制的一种手段：困扰大型语言模型 (LLM) 的绑定问题和学习停滞，以及限制现有神经拟态技术的功耗和实现规模障碍（CAM 悖论）和 TCAM。该方法植根于 Pentti Kanerva 的 SDM，通过伽罗瓦域运算将范式从依赖于随机性的物理仿生学转向代数确定性的“AD 功能归纳法”。这消除了传统超维计算（HDC）固有的大量计算开销，成功地将推理基础重建为即使在边缘设备上也可部署的非常实用的数字逻辑。This paper has proposed “VaCoAl,” an ultra-high-speed, low-cost, ultra-high-dimensional SRAM/DRAM-CAM architecture, together with its Python implementation “PyVaCoAl,” as a means of breaking through the dual limitations confronting modern AI: the Binding Problem and learning stagnation afflicting Large Language Models (LLMs), and the power-consumption and implementation-scale barriers (the CAM Paradox) that have constrained existing neuromorphic technologies and TCAMs. Rooted in Pentti Kanerva’s SDM, the approach shifts the paradigm away from randomness-dependent physical biomimicry toward algebro-deterministic “AD Functional Induction” via Galois-field operations. This has eliminated the massive computational overhead inherent in conventional Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC), successfully reconstructing the foundation of reasoning as an eminently practical digital logic deployable even on edge devices.

在理论方面，VaCoAl在物理和软件上实现了松本所设想的“用检索和关联（推理）代替计算的基于内存的架构（LUT）”On the theoretical front, VaCoAl physically and in software realises the “memory-based architecture (LUT) that replaces computation with retrieval and association (reasoning)” envisioned by Matsumoto[25]，在 SRAM 和 DRAM 上。, on SRAM and DRAM.

这些理论和经验优势得到了第 5 部分中部署的规模空前的“计算 Prosopography”的有力证实。在一项艰巨的深度搜索中，追踪了多达 57 代的家谱和超过 2550 万条总记录——从所有 64 位历史领域奖章获得者开始，并利用从 WIKIDATA 中提取的大约 470,000 条记录的本体数据库——VaCoAl 的 HDC 类型代数运算（绑定/解除绑定）既实现了标准哈希函数无法实现的语义灵活性，又实现了相对于前沿大小的“精确匹配”精度。在救援电路禁用的 Don't Care 模式下，VaCoAl 以放弃与 DICT 的完美一致性为代价，实现了基于路径质量的紧急语义选择，从而能够阐明 DICT 无法达到的历史结构。通过连续测量（微积分学位），我们成功地对历史动态进行了定量和基于证据的可视化，从而使不同的知识汇聚在莱布尼兹等节点上，与托马斯·库恩的几何结构一起形成了现代数学的“高速公路”（These theoretical and empirical superiorities were robustly substantiated by the unprecedented-scale “Computational Prosopography” deployed in Section 5. In a demanding deep search tracing genealogies up to 57 generations and over 25.5 million total records—starting from all 64 historical Fields Medalists and drawing on an ontology database of approximately 470,000 records extracted from WIKIDATA—VaCoAl’s HDC-type algebraic operations (Binding/Unbinding) achieved both semantic flexibility inaccessible to standard hash functions and the precision of “Exact Match” relative to the Frontier Size. In the Don’t Care mode with the rescue circuit disabled, at the cost of relinquishing perfect agreement with DICT, VaCoAl realised emergent semantic selection grounded in path quality, enabling the elucidation of historical structures unattainable by DICT. Through continuous measurement (Calculus Degree), we succeeded in quantitatively and evidence-based visualisation of the historical dynamics whereby diverse knowledge converged on nodes such as Leibniz to form the “superhighway” of modern mathematics, together with the geometric structure of a Thomas Kuhn ([22]）式范式转变（科学新常态的建立）。)-style paradigm shift (the establishment of a new normal science).

此外，这项研究最引人注目的发现是 VaCoAl 的“Don’t Care”机制的新兴特性，这是通过多级防撞（救援）电路的比较验证而揭示的。该机制最初仅作为空间噪声容限（横向抑制）实现，但在多阶段深度推理中，会产生依赖于路径深度的指数衰减（惩罚），起到“奥卡姆剃刀”的作用，自然地修剪长而迂回的路径，并有选择地保留短而直接的路径。这构成了与 STDP（尖峰时序相关可塑性）的功能等价The most striking discovery of this study, moreover, is the emergent property of VaCoAl’s “Don’t Care” mechanism, brought to light through comparative verification of the multi-stage collision-avoidance (rescue) circuit. Originally implemented as mere spatial noise tolerance (lateral inhibition), this mechanism gave rise, in multi-stage deep reasoning, to an exponential decay (penalty) dependent on path depth, functioning as an “Occam’s razor” that naturally prunes long, circuitous routes and selectively preserves short, direct paths. This constitutes functional equivalence with STDP (Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity)[16]，它削弱了神经回路中未使用的突触连接，提供了一个原始的演示，即超出设计者意图的复杂的“紧急语义选择”在由真实数据构建的逻辑网络上自主驱动。, which weakens unused synaptic connections in neural circuits, providing an original demonstration that sophisticated “emergent semantic selection” beyond the designer’s intent drives autonomously on logical networks constructed from real data.

VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 同时通过无损可逆操作、支持事后验证的数学可审计性以及基于置信度分数的可解释 AI (XAI) 的实现，同时提供了绑定问题的解决方案。这构成了一种实用的解决方案，突破了“仿生学”在计算复杂性和制造成本方面的限制，在实用的硅芯片上实现了结合可解释性和高可靠性的“超维人工智能（HDC-AI）”。这项研究为 HDC 的实际实施指明了具体方向，证明它可以作为现有方法的可行替代方案，特别是对于大规模多跳推理和部署到边缘设备。VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl simultaneously delivers a solution to the Binding Problem through lossless reversible operations, mathematical auditability enabling post-hoc verification, and the realisation of Explainable AI (XAI) grounded in confidence scores. This constitutes a practical solution that breaks through the “limits of biomimicry” with respect to computational complexity and manufacturing cost, realising a “Hyperdimensional AI (HDC-AI)” combining explainability and high reliability on practical silicon chips. This study charts a concrete direction for the practical implementation of HDC, demonstrating that it can serve as a viable alternative to existing methods—particularly for large-scale multi-hop reasoning and deployment to edge devices.
