结论Conclusion

在没有内存冲突的理想条件下，切尔诺夫边界分析表明，误报概率的上限为Under ideal conditions free from memory collisions, the Chernoff-bound analysis demonstrates that the false-positive probability is bounded above bye−2606e^{-2606}。本底噪声之间的“间隙”（. The “gap” between the noise floor (μ=1\mu=1）和多数阈值（) and the majority threshold (θ=500\theta=500）是概率波动无法克服的。) is insurmountable by probabilistic fluctuations.161616本附录假设在通过伽罗瓦场扩散进行伪随机生成期间不会发生内存冲突。在实践中，碰撞确实会根据维度而发生（This appendix assumes that no memory collisions occur during pseudo-random generation via Galois-field diffusion. In practice, collisions do arise depending on the dimensionality (nn) 高维二进制空间的块数 () of the high-dimensional binary space, the number of blocks (NN）和地址空间的大小（), and the size of the address space (MM）。的影响). The influence ofMM特别大。例如，在第 5 节详细介绍的 PyVaCoAl 模拟实验中，is especially large. For instance, in the PyVaCoAl simulation experiments detailed in Section 5, withn=12,800n=12{,}800和andN=128N=128，地址空间为, an address space ofM=210M=2^{10}导致每个块中的每个单元格发生碰撞；然后，所有后扩散学习和检索完全由防撞电路处理。当冲突是部分的时，只有受影响的单元才会调用救援电路。因此，在救援模式（电路打开）下，PyVaCoAl 实现了零碰撞；在“无关”模式（电路关闭）下，可以容忍微小的冲突，从而实现基于 CR2 路径积分的语义选择——这是一种经过深思熟虑的权衡。 PyVaCoAl 允许打开和关闭该电路，因为在地址空间causes collisions at every cell in every block; all post-diffusion learning and retrieval is then handled entirely by the collision-avoidance circuit. When collisions are partial, only the affected cells invoke the rescue circuit. Accordingly, in Rescue mode (circuit ON) PyVaCoAl achieves zero collisions; in Don’t Care mode (circuit OFF), minute collisions are tolerated, enabling the CR2-path-integral-based semantic selection—a deliberate trade-off. PyVaCoAl allows this circuit to be toggled on and off, because at address spaces ofM=227−1M=2^{27}-1以上，内存冲突几乎可以消除。第 5 节的实验结果支持这样的结论：结合防撞电路，即使在真实的大数据环境中，也可以近似维持这种保证。and above, memory collisions can be virtually eliminated. The Section 5 experimental results support the conclusion that, in combination with the collision-avoidance circuit, this guarantee is approximately maintained even in real big-data environments.

附录BAppendix B框架的转变——从“模仿”到“趋同进化”A Shift in Framing—From “Mimicry” to “Convergent Evolution”

B.1: 树突B.1: Dendrites→\rightarrow索马Soma→\rightarrowAxon 和 PyVaCoAl：三层对应Axon and PyVaCoAl: Three-Layer Correspondence

神经拟态计算的主流方法——IBM的TrueNorthThe mainstream approach in neuromorphic computing—IBM’s TrueNorth[26]，英特尔的Loihi, Intel’s Loihi[9], SpiNNaker [13]以及它们的同类——以硅上生物神经元的模拟、基于尖峰的特性的物理模仿为设计原则。, and their kin—takes as its design principle the physical mimicry of biological neurons’ analog, spike-based characteristics on silicon.

本文提出的 VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl 架构采取了根本不同的立场。 VaCoAl 确实The VaCoAl/PyVaCoAl architecture proposed in this paper adopts a fundamentally different stance. VaCoAl does不模仿not mimic生物神经元。相反，它独立地解决了biological neurons. Instead, it independently solves the相同的计算问题same computational problems—通过代数确定性数字逻辑实现高维正交化、模式完成、因果方向保存、跨代记忆集成。—high-dimensional orthogonalisation, pattern completion, causal-direction preservation, cross-generational memory integration—through algebro-deterministic digital logic.

我们将这种关系定义为“数学同构”而不是“数学同构”We define this relationship not as “mathematical isomorphism” but as“计算等价”“computational equivalence”：不同的实现基础（模拟与数字代数）通过独立解决相同的计算问题，达到了相似的功能结构——与生物学中的趋同进化相同的逻辑模式。: different implementation substrates (analog vs. digital algebra) have, by independently solving the same computational problems, arrived at similar functional structures—the same logical pattern as convergent evolution in biology.

至关重要的是，VaCoAl 通过这种融合获得了神经形态设备所具有的三个特性Crucially, VaCoAl acquires, through this convergence, three properties that neuromorphic devices are由于模拟实现的限制本来就无法实现inherently unable to realise due to the constraints of analog implementation:

  1. 1.

    完美的可逆性：Perfect Reversibility:通过伽罗瓦域运算实现的绑定/解除绑定的代数可逆性可以实现编码信息的无损、完整恢复。热噪声和器件变化使得这在模拟电路中原则上无法实现。The algebraic reversibility of Binding/Unbinding via Galois-field operations enables lossless, complete recovery of encoded information. Thermal noise and device variation render this unachievable in principle in analog circuits.

  2. 2.

    数学可审计性：Mathematical Auditability:CR2 分数的路径积分允许对“为什么产生给定答案”进行代数回溯。在分布式计算的尖峰神经网络中，类似的回溯在结构上是很棘手的。The path integral of the CR2 score permits algebraic backtracking of “why a given answer was produced.” In spiking neural networks, where computation is distributed, comparable backtracking is structurally intractable.

  3. 3.

    展示零碰撞（救援模式）：Demonstrated Zero Collision (Rescue Mode):在救援模式下处理的超过 2550 万条记录中，零冲突得到了实证验证。 （在“无关”模式下，冲突是可以容忍的，并且这种容忍成为第 5.6 节中报告的类似 STDP 的选择的源泉。）在模拟设备中，量化噪声和串扰排除了零冲突的数学保证。Zero collisions were empirically verified across more than 25.5 million records processed in Rescue mode. (In Don’t Care mode, collisions are tolerated, and this tolerance becomes the wellspring of the STDP-like selection reported in Section 5.6.) In analog devices, quantisation noise and crosstalk preclude a mathematical guarantee of zero collision.

必须明确说明一个重要的限制。尽管生物树突中的 NMDA 尖峰表现出时间依赖性、分级非线性，但 VaCoAl 中的局部检查是离散的精确匹配决策。在本附录中，我们将两者之间的关系定位为数学同构而非数学同构。An important limitation must be stated explicitly. Whereas NMDA spikes in biological dendrites exhibit time-dependent, graded nonlinearities, local checking in VaCoAl is a discrete exact-match decision. In this appendix, we position the relationship between the two not as a mathematical isomorphism but as a结构流等价structural flow equivalence计算流程：“除法of the computational flow: “division→\rightarrow本地处理local processing→\rightarrow整合”。integration.”

B.3：CA3 循环络脉的功能类比——解释性命题和未来的实验挑战B.3: Functional Analogy with CA3 Recurrent Collaterals—Interpretive Proposition and Future Experimental Challenges

劳斯莱斯强调的CA3最重要的特性The most important characteristic of CA3 emphasised by Rolls[35]是 CA3 神经元通过循环络脉向其他 CA3 神经元产生吸引子动力学。通过这些循环附属连接，实现了从部分或扭曲的输入“完成”完整模式（模式完成）的能力。is that CA3 neurons generate attractor dynamics through Recurrent Collaterals to other CA3 neurons. Through these recurrent-collateral connections, the ability to “complete” a full pattern from a partial or distorted input (pattern completion) is realised.

在 PyVaCoAl 中，存在功能类似于这些循环抵押品的结构In PyVaCoAl, a structure functionally analogous to these recurrent collaterals exists隐含地implicitly.

CR2 乘法代代积分的递归性质：Recursive Nature of CR2 Multiplicative Generational Integration:

在更新规则中In the update ruleC​R2(n)=C​R2(n−1)×C​R1(n−1)CR_{2}^{(n)}=CR_{2}^{(n-1)}\times CR_{1}^{(n-1)}，术语, the termC​R2(n−1)CR_{2}^{(n-1)}——上一代的累积置信度——递归地输入到下一代的计算中。这与循环抵押品的功能本质是同构的，即“过去的计算结果反馈到当前的计算中”。—the cumulative confidence of the previous generation—is recursively input into the computation for the next generation. This is isomorphic to the functional essence of recurrent collaterals, where “past computational results are fed back into current computations.”

更具体地说，由于每个阶段选择的候选节点不仅根据预先设定的边界大小（FS）进行剪枝，而且还根据CR1（块间投票率）和CR2（作为CR1的累积乘积的路径积分值）进行剪枝，因此只有幸存的节点成为下一代的搜索起点。因此，PyVaCoAl 的操作在以下方面与海马 CA3 区域的操作在功能上相似，其中被激发的神经元群通过循环性络脉重新激活“与自身相关的模式”：More specifically, because the candidates selected at each stage are pruned not only by the pre-set Frontier Size (FS) but also by CR1 (inter-block voting rate) and CR2 (path-integral value as the cumulative product of CR1s), only the surviving nodes become the search starting points for the next generation. The operation of PyVaCoAl is thus functionally similar, in the following respects, to the operation of the CA3 region of the hippocampus, where a fired neuron population reactivates “patterns related to themselves” through recurrent collaterals:

  • •

    从部分模式完成完整模式： PyVaCoAl 的 FS 中的候选者按 CR2 的降序排序，而 Python DICT 的哈希函数中的候选者按字母顺序等任意机械规则排序，导致两者的搜索结果出现分歧（Full-pattern completion from partial patterns: Candidates within PyVaCoAl’s FS are sorted in descending order of CR2, whereas candidates within Python DICT’s hash function are sorted by arbitrary mechanical rules such as alphabetical order, causing the search results of the two to diverge (==收敛到新的吸引子）。convergence to a new attractor).

  • •

    亚阈值模式的自然抑制： 停在Natural suppression of sub-threshold patterns: Halting atCR2<0.100\mathrm{CR2}<0.100（任意预设值）(an arbitrary pre-set value)==抑制向弱吸引子的收敛。suppression of convergence to weak attractors.

  • •

    竞争模式中的获胜者选择： 不关心规则Winner selection among competing patterns: Don’t Care rule==延迟确定多个吸引子之间的优越性。deferral of superiority determination among multiple attractors.

重要预订：Important Reservation:171717请注意，在第 5.6.5 节中，获得了基于真实数据的该解释命题的第一个定量佐证。Note that in Section 5.6.5, the first quantitative corroboration based on real data for this interpretive proposition was obtained.目前，我们将这种循环抵押类比定位为At present, we position this recurrent-collateral analogy as an解释命题interpretive proposition。 数学同构的正式证明和证明它的实验仍然是未来的挑战。有关具体的实验挑战，请参阅附录 B.5。. Formal proof of mathematical isomorphism and experiments to demonstrate it remain future challenges. For concrete experimental challenges, see Appendix B.5.

5.6 节中真实数据的部分佐证：CR1 和 CR2 的分歧揭示了递归反馈的现实Partial Corroboration by Real Data in Section 5.6: The Divergence of CR1 and CR2 Reveals the Reality of Recursive Feedback

针对上述“解释命题”，5.6节中的DICT与PyVaCoAl对比实验首次提供了基于真实数据的定量佐证。 核心证据是在救援电路禁用的情况下（128_27配置），PyVaCoAl中CR1和CR2的代际轨迹存在巨大差异（图In response to the “interpretive proposition” above, the DICT vs. PyVaCoAl comparison experiment in Section 5.6 provided quantitative corroboration based on real data for the first time. The core evidence is the dramatic divergence in the generational trajectories of CR1 and CR2 in PyVaCoAl with the rescue circuit disabled (128_27 configuration) (Figure4).

该图所示的事实定量地证实了本节中阐述的 CA3 循环络功能类比的核心。逻辑如下。The facts shown in this figure quantitatively corroborate the core of the functional analogy with CA3 recurrent collaterals formulated in this section. The logic is as follows.

首先，CR1的稳定性。First, the stability of CR1.在128_27配置（无救援）下，每代区块投票一致率CR1保持在0.995-0.999的极高水平。这意味着 VaCoAl 的代数多数投票在各个搜索步骤中保持了“近乎完美的搜索准确性”。 Don't Care 块仅占所有 128 个块中的 1-2 个，并且逐步查看，与配备救援的配置的性能差异几乎无法察觉。In the 128_27 configuration (without rescue), the per-generation block-voting agreement rate CR1 remains at an extremely high level of 0.995–0.999. This means that VaCoAl’s algebraic majority voting maintains “near-perfect search accuracy” at individual search steps. Don’t Care blocks amount to only 1–2 out of all 128 blocks, and viewed step by step, the performance difference from the rescue-equipped configuration is almost imperceptible.

其次，CR2的累积衰减。Second, the cumulative decay of CR2.因为BecauseCR2=∏i=1nCR1i\mathrm{CR2}=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{CR1}_{i}将这些微小的 CR1 波动一代又一代地相乘，它们像复利一样累积并单调衰减。如果multiplies these minute CR1 fluctuations generation by generation, they accumulate like compound interest and decay monotonically. IfCR1≈0.997\mathrm{CR1}\approx 0.997累积超过56代，累积乘积的理论值为accumulates over 56 generations, the theoretical value of the cumulative product is0.99756≈0.8460.997^{56}\approx 0.846。与测量值 0.905 的差异归因于“不关心”发生频率的代际差异；这一预测在数量上的成功不仅仅是在质量上的一致。这意味着设计者无意的紧急行为是可以预测的. The difference from the measured value of 0.905 is attributable to inter-generational variation in the frequency of Don’t Care occurrences; the quantitative success of this prediction goes beyond mere qualitative agreement. It means that emergent behaviour unintended by the designer is predictable先验的a priori通过 CR2 乘法积分的简单公式。这构成了我们对该机制的理解是正确的独立定量佐证，并且是可解释人工智能（XAI）的具体示例，表明该架构的行为原则上是可分析处理的。这种衰减是 CA3 循环抵押品递归性质的直接定量结果，其中“过去的计算结果反馈到当前的计算中”。through the simple formula of CR2 multiplicative integration. This constitutes independent quantitative corroboration that our understanding of the mechanism is correct, and is a concrete example of Explainable AI (XAI) showing that the behaviour of this architecture is, in principle, analytically tractable. This decay is a direct quantitative consequence of the recursive nature of CA3 recurrent collaterals, where “past computational results are fed back into current computations.”

三、与救援装备配置的对比。Third, the contrast with the rescue-equipped configuration.在 128_10 配置（带救援）中，CR1 和 CR2 在所有代中都完美保持在 1.0。救援电路通过强制解决块冲突来完全抑制Don’t Cares的发生，从而使CR2的递归反馈结构失效。配备救援的配置生成与 DICT（第 5.6.2 节）相同的搜索结果正是这种失效的结果。In the 128_10 configuration (with rescue), both CR1 and CR2 are maintained perfectly at 1.0 across all generations. The rescue circuit completely suppresses the occurrence of Don’t Cares by forcibly resolving block collisions, thereby invalidating the recursive feedback structure of CR2. That the rescue-equipped configuration generates the same search results as DICT (Section 5.6.2) is precisely the consequence of this invalidation.

第四，CR1-CR2 差异的语义后果。Fourth, the semantic consequences of this CR1–CR2 divergence.尽管 CR1 几乎不下降，但 CR2 却稳步下降。这种“微观上可忽略但宏观上决定性”的分歧是第 5.6 节中报告的“紧急语义选择”的数学基础。由于在 FS 剪枝期间候选者按 CR2 降序排序，由于 CR2 的累积衰减，通往深层代（DICT 专有组的医生：初级代 gen21-26）的节点的路径具有相对较低的优先级，而位于浅层代（PyVaCoAl 专有组的数学家：初级代 gen9-12）的节点的路径保留高度维护的 CR2。这就是 VaCoAl 的 Don't Care 机制起到“奥卡姆剃刀”作用的物理机制，也是从纯数字代数逻辑中产生功能上相当于大脑 STDP（“加强近处路径，削弱远路径”）的路径依赖选择过程的最直接证据。Although CR1 scarcely declines, CR2 steadily decays. This “microscopically negligible yet macroscopically decisive” divergence is the mathematical foundation of the “emergent semantic selection” reported in Section 5.6. Because candidates are sorted in descending CR2 order during FS pruning, paths to nodes located in deep generations (physicians of the DICT-exclusive group: primary generations gen21–26) carry relatively lower priority owing to the cumulative decay of CR2, while paths to nodes located in shallow generations (mathematicians of the PyVaCoAl-exclusive group: primary generations gen9–12) retain highly maintained CR2. This is the physical mechanism by which VaCoAl’s Don’t Care mechanism functions as “Occam’s razor,” and the most direct evidence of the process by which a path-dependent selection functionally equivalent to the brain’s STDP—“strengthening near paths and weakening far paths”—emerges from purely digital algebraic logic.

因此，我们得出的结论是，本节的“解释命题”得到了 5.6 节的实验结果和 CR1/CR2 轨迹数据的部分证实。剩下的挑战是系统地验证这种衰减特性是否可以在不同的数据集和不同的网络拓扑中重现。We therefore conclude that the “interpretive proposition” of this section was partially corroborated by the experimental results of Section 5.6 and this CR1/CR2 trajectory data. The remaining challenge is to verify systematically that this decay characteristic is reproducible across different datasets and different network topologies.

附录 B.4：通过数字代数实现出现类似 STDP 的函数 - 与模拟实现的收敛等价Appendix B.4: Emergence of STDP-like Functions via Digital Algebraic Implementation—Convergent Equivalence with Analog Implementations

B.4.1 问题抽象：STDP 解决什么计算问题？B.4.1 Abstraction of the Problem: What Computational Problem Is STDP Solving?

大脑中的 STDP（尖峰时序依赖性可塑性）通常被描述为神经回路的物理机制。然而，在本附录中，我们抽象了STDP (Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity) in the brain is often described as a physical mechanism of neural circuits. In this appendix, however, we abstract the计算问题本身computational problem itselfSTDP 解决如下：that STDP solves as follows:

“在多级信息传输中，根据距起点的距离（世代/时间遥远）以指数方式衰减路径的权重，有选择地保留近距离、直接的路径。”“In multi-stage information transmission, exponentially decay the weight of a path according to the distance (generational/temporal remoteness) from the starting point, selectively preserving close, direct paths.”

根据这个定义，很明显 STDP 本质上并不依赖于模拟尖峰之间毫秒精度定时差异的物理实现，而是依赖于Under this definition, it becomes clear that STDP does not inherently depend on the physical implementation of millisecond-precision timing differences between analog spikes, but depends on the分布式表示与阈值判断的抽象计算结构abstract computational structure of distributed representation and threshold judgment。因此，解决该计算问题的实现不限于模拟电路。. Implementations that solve this computational problem are therefore not limited to analog circuits.

B.4.2 模拟实现与 VaCoAl 数字实现之间的对比B.4.2 Contrast between Analog Implementations and VaCoAl’s Digital Implementation

传统的模拟神经形态实现和 VaCoAl 的数字代数实现通过表中所示的相应方法解决了上面抽象的计算问题The conventional analog neuromorphic implementation and VaCoAl’s digital algebraic implementation solve the computational problem abstracted above by the respective methods shown in Table17.

两者都解决相同的计算问题；只是实现基底不同。具体内容是这样的Both solve the same computational problem; only the implementation substrate differs. This is the concrete content of the收敛计算等价convergent computational equivalence定义见附录 B.1。defined in Appendix B.1.

B.4.3 数字化实现额外获得的三个属性B.4.3 Three Properties Additionally Acquired by the Digital Implementation

除了与模拟实现的融合之外，VaCoAl 的数字代数实现还获得了以下三个特性——每一个特性原则上都是模拟实现无法实现的。In addition to convergence with the analog implementation, VaCoAl’s digital algebraic implementation additionally acquires the following three properties—each of which is unachievable in principle by analog implementations.

(1) 完美的可逆性(1) Perfect Reversibility

绑定/解除绑定的伽罗瓦域运算（异或和移位）在代数上是可逆的，从而能够无损、完整地恢复编码信息。热噪声和器件变化使得这在模拟电路中原则上无法实现。The Galois-field operations (XOR and shift) of Binding/Unbinding are algebraically reversible, enabling lossless, complete recovery of encoded information. Thermal noise and device variation render this unachievable in principle in analog circuits.

(2) 数学可审计性(2) Mathematical Auditability

CR 的路径积分The path integral of the CR2分数允许对“为什么产生给定答案”进行代数回溯。在分布式计算的尖峰神经网络中，类似的回溯在结构上是很棘手的。score permits algebraic backtracking of “why a given answer was produced.” In spiking neural networks, where computation is distributed, comparable backtracking is structurally intractable.

(3) 零碰撞的确定性保证（仅限救援模式）(3) Deterministic Guarantee of Zero Collision (Rescue Mode only)

在救援模式下，超过 2550 万条记录的零碰撞得到了实证证明（第 5.2 节）。在模拟器件中，量化噪声和串扰原则上排除了这种数学保证。Zero collisions were empirically demonstrated in Rescue mode across more than 25.5 million records (Section 5.2). In analog devices, quantisation noise and crosstalk preclude such a mathematical guarantee in principle.

Don't Care 模式有意放弃第三个属性，以换取类似 STDP 衰变的紧急选择能力。那个这个Don’t Care mode intentionally relinquishes the third property in exchange for the emergent selection capability of STDP-like decay. That this权衡是可以通过设计切换的trade-off is switchable by design本身就是模拟实现中缺少的功能（表is itself a feature absent from analog implementations (Table18).

B.4.5 简要总结B.4.5 Brief Summary

本附录的权利要求可以用一句话来表述：The claim of this appendix may be stated in a single sentence:

STDP的功能本质不在于模拟尖峰计时精度，而在于分布式表示和阈值判断的计算结构。 VaCoAl 通过数字代数独立解决了这种计算结构，并额外获得了模拟实现原则上无法拥有的三个属性（可逆性、可审计性、零冲突保证）。The functional essence of STDP resides not in analog spike-timing precision but in the computational structure of distributed representation and threshold judgment. VaCoAl independently solves this computational structure via digital algebra, and additionally acquires three properties (reversibility, auditability, zero-collision guarantee) that analog implementations cannot, in principle, possess.

这不是“对模拟实现的否定”，而是以下逻辑：This is not a “negation of analog implementation” but the logic of趋同进化convergent evolution：不同的实现基础已经收敛到相同的计算问题。这与附录 B.1 中定义的框架完全一致。: different implementation substrates have converged on the same computational problem. This is fully consistent with the framework defined in Appendix B.1.

我们重申，本附录的主张是其中之一We reiterate that the claim of this appendix is one of结构流等价structural flow equivalence，而不是数学同构。前者指定计算流程“除法”中的对应关系, not mathematical isomorphism. The former designates a correspondence in the computational flow “division→\rightarrow本地处理local processing→\rightarrow整合”；后者需要严格的双向映射。本文的实验仅证明了前者。integration”; the latter demands strict bidirectional mapping. The experiments in this paper demonstrate the former only.

B.5：VaCoAl 和神经形态动力学之间的数学同构B.5: Mathematical Isomorphism between VaCoAl and Neuromorphic Dynamics

在这里，我们证明了 VaCoAl 和神经形态（拟脑）动力学之间的数学同构（包括通过 VaCoAl 超越构成法学硕士支柱的 McCulloch-Pitts 点神经元模型的局限性）。Here we prove the mathematical isomorphism between VaCoAl and neuromorphic (brain-mimetic) dynamics (including the transcendence, via VaCoAl, of the limitations of the McCulloch–Pitts point-neuron model that forms the backbone of LLMs).

(a) 生物神经元模型(a) Biological Neuron Model

输出The outputybioy_{\mathrm{bio}}生物神经元的作用被描述为非线性激活函数的应用of a biological neuron is described as the application of a nonlinear activation functionϕ\phi（阈值）到输入向量的内积(thresholding) to the inner product of the input vector𝐱∈ℝD\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{D}和突触权重向量and the synaptic weight vector𝐰∈ℝD\mathbf{w}\in\mathbb{R}^{D}:

ybio=ϕ​(𝐰⋅𝐱−θ)y_{\mathrm{bio}}=\phi(\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}-\theta) (18)

在向量是双极性的超维计算环境中In the Hyperdimensional Computing context where vectors are bipolar{−1,1}D\{-1,1\}^{D}，内积与汉明距离线性相关, the inner product is linearly related to the Hamming distancedH​(𝐰,𝐱)d_{H}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x}):

𝐰⋅𝐱=D−2​dH​(𝐰,𝐱)\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}=D-2\,d_{H}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x}) (19)

因此，触发条件相当于位于所存储权重的特定汉明距离半径内的输入。The firing condition is therefore equivalent to the input lying within a certain Hamming-distance radius of the stored weight.

(b) VaCoAl（数字感应）模型(b) VaCoAl (Digital Induction) Model

在 VaCoAl 中，输入In VaCoAl, the inputxx被扩散并划分为is diffused and partitioned intoNN块。 让blocks. Letδi\delta_{i}是块的匹配决策函数be the match-decision function for blockii（如果块与存储的条目匹配则返回 1，否则返回 0）。 当匹配的总和超过多数阈值时（通常(returning 1 if the block matches the stored entry, 0 otherwise). When the sum of matches exceeds the majority threshold (typicallyN/2N/2），系统输出正确答案。), the system outputs the correct answer.

输出形式化为：The output is formalised as:

yv​a​c=𝕀​(∑i=1Nδi>N2)y_{vac}=\mathbb{I}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\delta_{i}>\frac{N}{2}\right) (20)

哪里where𝕀​(⋅)\mathbb{I}(\cdot)是is the指示功能indicator function，精确定义为：, defined precisely as:

𝕀​(A)={1if condition ​A​ is true0otherwise\mathbb{I}(A)=\begin{cases}1&\text{if condition }A\text{ is true}\\ 0&\text{otherwise}\end{cases} (21)

该函数是 SRAM 阵列数字“阈值逻辑”的数学表达式，将模拟“投票数”转换为离散的“二元决策（开火或不开火）”。This function is the mathematical expression of the SRAM array’s digital “threshold logic,” converting the analog “vote count” into a discrete “binary decision (to fire or not).”

(c) 同构证明：通过单调性实现等价(c) Proof of Isomorphism: Equivalence via Monotonicity

VaCoAl 的读出功能被定义为对投票计数的阈值检查VaCoAl’s read-out function is defined as a threshold check on the vote countSS:

yv​a​c=𝕀​(S>θd​i​g​i​t​a​l),where ​S=∑i=1Nδiy_{vac}=\mathbb{I}\left(S>\theta_{digital}\right),\quad\text{where }S=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\delta_{i} (22)

根据大数定律，计票By the Law of Large Numbers, the vote countSS（匹配块的数量）随输入相似度的变化而变化。(number of matching blocks) varies with input similarity.至关重要的是，伽罗瓦场扩散保证的均匀误差分布导致Crucially, the uniform error distribution guaranteed by Galois-field diffusion causesSS充当整体相似性的“严格单调递增估计器”（内积to function as a “strictly monotone increasing estimator” of overall similarity (inner product𝐰⋅𝐱\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}).换句话说，随着模拟相似度In other words, as the analog similarity𝐰⋅𝐱\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}增加，数字投票计数increases, the digital vote countSS也严格单调增加（平均）。also increases (on average) strictly monotonically.

这种严格的单调性意味着存在唯一的一对一映射函数This strict monotonicity implies the existence of a unique one-to-one mapping functionff:

S≈f​(𝐰⋅𝐱)S\approx f(\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}) (23)

因为Becauseff严格单调，可逆。 数字阈值条件is strictly monotone, it is invertible. The digital threshold conditionS>θd​i​g​i​t​a​lS>\theta_{digital}因此可以重写为模拟阈值条件：can therefore be rewritten as an analog threshold condition:

f​(𝐰⋅𝐱)>θd​i​g​i​t​a​l⇔𝐰⋅𝐱>f−1​(θd​i​g​i​t​a​l)f(\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x})>\theta_{digital}\iff\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}>f^{-1}(\theta_{digital}) (24)

设置Settingθa​n​a​l​o​g=f−1​(θd​i​g​i​t​a​l)\theta_{analog}=f^{-1}(\theta_{digital})，我们得到：, we obtain:

yv​a​c≡𝕀​(𝐰⋅𝐱>θa​n​a​l​o​g)y_{vac}\equiv\mathbb{I}\left(\mathbf{w}\cdot\mathbf{x}>\theta_{analog}\right) (25)

这在形式上与生物神经元输出相同This is formally identical to the biological neuron outputyb​i​oy_{bio}定义在等式（6）中。defined in equation (6).

(d) 横向抑制和赢者通吃动态的 AD 功能诱导(d) AD Functional Induction of Lateral Inhibition and Winner-Take-All Dynamics

作为楚玛As Chuma[8]讨论，生物和神经形态系统通常需要三种类型的神经元——兴奋性神经元、抑制性神经元和中间神经元——来实现discusses, biological and neuromorphic systems typically require three types of neurons—excitatory, inhibitory, and interneurons—to implement侧抑制lateral inhibition和andSTDP（尖峰时间相关可塑性）STDP (Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity)。这些物质会在物理上抑制邻近的神经元，从而增强记忆检索和联想能力。 VaCoAl 取代了这种物理复杂性. These physically suppress neighbouring neurons, sharpening memory retrieval and association. VaCoAl replaces this physical complexity with“概率正交性。”“probabilistic orthogonality.”让LetVt​a​r​g​e​tV_{target}是正确记忆的票数和be the vote count for the correct memory andVn​o​i​s​eV_{noise}竞争记忆的投票计数。在生物系统中，抑制性神经元主动减少the vote count for a competing memory. In biological systems, inhibitory neurons actively reduceVn​o​i​s​eV_{noise}。在 VaCoAl 中，伽罗瓦场扩散通过错误位的均匀分布保证：. In VaCoAl, Galois-field diffusion guarantees, through the uniform distribution of error bits, thatVn​o​i​s​e≈0V_{noise}\approx 0（渐近）。 VaCoAl 的(asymptotically). VaCoAl’s“不关心”机制“Don’t Care” mechanism因此在功能上起着相同的作用therefore plays functionally the identical role of抑制性神经元inhibitory neurons，保证强大的赢者通吃的动力：, guaranteeing robust Winner-Take-All dynamics:

P​(Winner=Target)→1asVt​a​r​g​e​t≫Vn​o​i​s​eP(\text{Winner}=\text{Target})\to 1\quad\text{as}\quad V_{target}\gg V_{noise}

这证明 VaCoAl 不仅在物理上模拟神经元激活功能，而且在功能上诱导海马体的新兴网络特性。This proves that VaCoAl does not merely physically simulate neuronal activation functions but functionally induces the emergent network properties of the hippocampus.

(e) 解决绑定问题的计算优势（相对于 Thagard 的标准）(e) Computational Advantage in Solving the Binding Problem (vs. Thagard’s Criteria)

保罗·萨加德Paul Thagard[42]强调认知架构必须解决emphasises that a cognitive architecture must solve the绑定问题Binding Problem——将表征（例如“红色”和“苹果”）组合成一个结构化的整体——以考虑创造力和复杂的思维。萨加德引用了埃利亚史密斯的—combining representations (e.g. “red” and “apple”) into a structured whole—in order to account for creativity and complex thought. Thagard cites Eliasmith’s语义指针架构 (SPA)Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA) [11]作为领先的解决方案，但 SPA 依赖于as a leading solution, but SPA relies on循环卷积Circular Convolution，这需要很高的计算成本（, which entails high computational cost (O​(N​log⁡N)O(N\log N)）和大量的功耗。 VaCoAl 通过以下方式提供了数学上优越的解决方案) and substantial power consumption. VaCoAl provides a mathematically superior solution through伽罗瓦域运算（异或和移位）Galois-field operations (XOR and shift):

  • •

    水疗中心：SPA:需要浮点FFT和大规模矩阵运算。Requires floating-point FFT and large-scale matrix operations.

  • •

    瓦钴铝：VaCoAl:仅通过简单的按位运算即可实现相同的绑定正交性（Achieves the same binding orthogonality with simple bitwise operations alone (O​(N)O(N)).

因此，VaCoAl 满足了 Thagard 的高维结合和成分表示标准，其效率远高于传统的神经拟态方法，为“大脑-思维”架构提供了理想的基础。VaCoAl thus satisfies Thagard’s criteria for high-dimensional binding and compositional representation with overwhelmingly greater efficiency than conventional neuromorphic approaches, providing an ideal substrate for a “Brain-Mind” architecture.

(f) 超越法学硕士的麦卡洛克-皮茨点神经元极限(f) Beyond the McCulloch–Pitts Point-Neuron Limits of LLMs

澄清为什么基于 McCulloch-Pitts (M-P) 神经元模型的现代人工智能 (LLM) 无法有效地重现上述机制至关重要。 M-P 神经元计算输入的加权和并将其传递给非线性激活函数（It is critically important to clarify why modern AI (LLMs) based on the McCulloch–Pitts (M-P) neuron model cannot efficiently reproduce the mechanisms described above. The M-P neuron computes a weighted sum of inputs and passes it through a nonlinear activation function (y=ϕ​(∑wi​xi)y=\phi(\sum w_{i}x_{i})）。与 VaCoAl 相比，该架构存在两个基本缺陷：). Compared with VaCoAl, this architecture suffers from two fundamental deficiencies:

  1. 1.

    矢量结构丢失（标量陷阱）：Loss of Vector Structure (The Scalar Trap):M-P 神经元的功能是The M-P neuron functions as a“标量积分器”“Scalar Integrator,”将高维结构信息压缩为单个标量（激活级别）。为了解决crushing high-dimensional structural information into a single scalar (activation level). To solve the绑定问题Binding Problem因此，M-P 网络必须诉诸串联或大量深层来隐式学习关系，从而引发参数的组合爆炸。相比之下，VaCoAl 则利用杠杆, M-P networks must therefore resort to concatenation or massive deep layers that learn relationships implicitly, inviting a combinatorial explosion of parameters. VaCoAl, by contrast, leverages伽罗瓦域代数Galois-field algebra执行运算（异或/移位），同时to perform operations (XOR/shift) while保存preserving高维向量的结构，无需参数爆炸即可实现解除绑定和组合推理。the structure of high-dimensional vectors, enabling Unbinding and compositional reasoning without parameter explosion.

  2. 2.

    无法实现被动抑制：Inability to Achieve Passive Inhibition:在 M-P 网络中，In M-P networks,侧抑制lateral inhibition需要明确的负权重（requires explicit negative weights (wi​j<0w_{ij}<0）在所有竞争神经元之间。在一个高维空间中包含) between all competing neurons. In a high-dimensional space containingNN理念，实现“赢者通吃”机制要求concepts, realising a “Winner-Take-All” mechanism demandsO​(N2)O(N^{2})抑制连接——计算上禁止。 VaCoAl 的inhibitory connections—computationally prohibitive. VaCoAl’s“不关心”“Don’t Care”机制实现了这种抑制mechanism achieves this inhibition被动地、统计地passively and statistically通过利用空间的正交性，不需要任何额外的连接。by exploiting the orthogonality of the space, requiring no additional connections whatsoever.

因此，VaCoAl 不仅优化了 M-P 神经元，而且还优化了 M-P 神经元。它VaCoAl therefore does not merely optimise the M-P neuron; it超越transcends当代法学硕士普遍存在的基于标量的整合的结构局限性。the structural limitations of the scalar-based integration that pervades contemporary LLMs.
